`App.xaml.cs`:

```cs
using System.Threading;
using System.Windows;

namespace SCTrainer;

public partial class App : Application
{
    private Mutex? _instance;
    private bool _ownsInstance;
    private TrayApplication? _tray;

    protected override void OnStartup(StartupEventArgs e)
    {
        _instance=new Mutex(false,@"Local\SplinterAssaultTrainer.Gui");
        try { _ownsInstance=_instance.WaitOne(0); }
        catch(AbandonedMutexException) { _ownsInstance=true; }
        if(!_ownsInstance)
        {
            try {using var signal=EventWaitHandle.OpenExisting(TrayApplication.ShowStatusEvent);signal.Set();}
            catch(WaitHandleCannotBeOpenedException) {
                MessageBox.Show("The trainer is already running. Use its tray icon or press F6 in the game. Close any older trainer before starting this version.",
                    "Splinter Assault Trainer",MessageBoxButton.OK,MessageBoxImage.Information);
            }
            Shutdown();
            return;
        }
        base.OnStartup(e);
        try {
            var controller=new MainWindow();
            MainWindow=controller;
            _tray=new TrayApplication(controller,()=>Shutdown());
            _tray.Start();
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException) {
            MessageBox.Show("Could not start the tray trainer: "+ex.Message,"Splinter Assault Trainer",MessageBoxButton.OK,MessageBoxImage.Error);
            Shutdown();
        }
    }

    protected override void OnExit(ExitEventArgs e)
    {
        _tray?.Dispose();
        if(_ownsInstance)_instance?.ReleaseMutex();
        _instance?.Dispose();
        base.OnExit(e);
    }
}
```

`CheatSession.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;

namespace SCTrainer;

[Flags]
public enum CheatOptions { None=0, UnlimitedAmmo=1, NoReload=2, UnlimitedGadgets=4, ReducedDetection=8, MovementSpeed=16, Invisibility=32, Noclip=64, BypassLocks=128, QuietLights=256 }

public sealed record CheatSettings(CheatOptions Options, int SpeedPercent=100)
{
    public void Validate()
    {
        if (((int)Options & ~511) != 0 || SpeedPercent < 50 || SpeedPercent > 300)
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(SpeedPercent), "Speed must be between 0.5x and 3x.");
    }
    public string Command { get { Validate(); return $"--cheats:{(int)Options}:{SpeedPercent}"; } }
}

internal sealed class CheatSession : IDisposable
{
    private readonly Process _game;
    private readonly SafeProcessHandle _handle;
    private readonly uint _viewport,_player,_index,_maskAddress;
    private DateTime _lastPing = DateTime.UtcNow;
    public CheatSettings Settings { get; }

    private CheatSession(Process game, CheatSettings settings, string reply)
    {
        _game=game; Settings=settings;
        if(!reply.StartsWith("CHEATS:"))throw new InvalidOperationException("Unexpected cheat-loader response.");
        var values=reply.Substring(7).Split(':').Select(x=>uint.Parse(x,NumberStyles.HexNumber)).ToArray();
        if(values.Length!=4||values.Any(x=>x==0))throw new InvalidOperationException("Incomplete cheat-loader response.");
        (_viewport,_player,_index,_maskAddress)=(values[0],values[1],values[2],values[3]);
        _handle=OpenProcess(0x410,false,game.Id);
        if(_handle.IsInvalid){_handle.Dispose();throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());}
    }

    public static async Task<CheatSession> ApplyAsync(Process game, CheatSettings settings)
    {
        settings.Validate();
        if(settings.Options==CheatOptions.None)throw new InvalidOperationException("No options selected.");
        using var check=OpenProcess(0x410,false,game.Id);
        if(check.IsInvalid)throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
        var reply=await DirectLevelLoader.SetCheatsAsync(game,settings);
        try{return new CheatSession(game,settings,reply);}
        catch{await DirectLevelLoader.SetCheatsAsync(game,new(CheatOptions.None));throw;}
    }

    private uint U(uint address)
    {
        var bytes=new byte[4];
        if(!ReadProcessMemory(_handle,(IntPtr)(long)address,bytes,4,out var count)||count!=4)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Player data unavailable.");
        return BitConverter.ToUInt32(bytes);
    }

    public bool IsCurrent()
    {
        try{return !_game.HasExited && U(_viewport+0x34)==_player && U(_player+4)==_index &&
            U(_maskAddress)==(uint)Settings.Options;}
        catch(InvalidOperationException){return false;}
    }

    public async Task PulseAsync()
    {
        if(DateTime.UtcNow-_lastPing<TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5))return;
        await DirectLevelLoader.PingCheatsAsync(_game);
        _lastPing=DateTime.UtcNow;
    }

    public async Task DisableAsync()
    {
        if(IsCurrent())await DirectLevelLoader.SetCheatsAsync(_game,new(CheatOptions.None));
    }

    public void Dispose(){_handle.Dispose();_game.Dispose();}
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll",SetLastError=true)] private static extern SafeProcessHandle OpenProcess(uint access,bool inherit,int pid);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll",SetLastError=true)] [return:MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool ReadProcessMemory(SafeProcessHandle process,IntPtr address,[Out]byte[] bytes,nuint size,out nuint count);
}
```

`DirectLevelLoader.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Security.Cryptography;

namespace SCTrainer;

internal static class DirectLevelLoader
{
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> SupportedFiles = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        ["Engine.dll"] = "24F23217896114C8B9F7D5B6C1C16F9002C5977BF7102E45B10E949247FA9655",
        ["Core.dll"] = "F190F53428AE40E7C0FC34809F8D22B341AF8758F7E05A5ABA35BAAF69799FE3",
        ["WinDrv.dll"] = "FCADD480793A43DD2F836839E3C5601504476972AED2340FEE667E38C2D9E688"
    };

    private static readonly SemaphoreSlim Requests = new(1, 1);

    public static async Task<string> RequestAsync(Process game, string map)
    {
        if (map != "--probe" && (map.Length == 0 || map.Length > 100 ||
            map.Any(c => !(char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) || c == '_'))))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid map name.");
        if (!await Requests.WaitAsync(0))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("A loader request is already running.");
        try
        {
            ValidateGame(game);
            var version = await RunHelperAsync("--version");
            if (version != "SCTravelHost:0.23.0:protocol22")
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Loader version mismatch. Close the trainer and use Launch Trainer.cmd.");
            if (map == "--probe") return await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(), map);
            var state = await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(), "--state");
            if (!state.Equals("STATE:menu", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Return to the game's main menu before loading a map. No travel request was sent.");
            await GameWindowFocus.PrepareAsync(game.MainWindowHandle);
            await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(), map);
            var timeout = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            while (timeout.Elapsed < TimeSpan.FromSeconds(45))
            {
                await Task.Delay(1000);
                if (game.HasExited) throw new InvalidOperationException("The game exited during loading.");
                var title = game.MainWindowTitle;
                if (title.Contains("Critical Error", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("The game reported a critical error during loading.");
                // Avoid poking the game while its loading window is active.
                game.Refresh();
                if (game.MainWindowTitle.Equals("Loading", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) continue;
                state = await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(), "--state");
                if (state.Equals("STATE:" + map, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    return "Loaded " + map + ".";
            }
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Loading was not confirmed. Focus the game to let it resume; do not send another request while it is loading.");
        }
        finally { Requests.Release(); }
    }

    internal static async Task<string> SetHealthAsync(Process game, bool enabled)
    {
        if (!await Requests.WaitAsync(0)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Wait for the current loader request to finish.");
        try
        {
            ValidateGame(game);
            if (await RunHelperAsync("--version") != "SCTravelHost:0.23.0:protocol22")
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Loader version mismatch. Run Launch Trainer.cmd.");
            return await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(), enabled ? "--health-on" : "--health-off");
        }
        finally { Requests.Release(); }
    }

    internal static Task<string> SetCheatsAsync(Process game, CheatSettings settings) => CheatCommandAsync(game,settings.Command);
    internal static Task<string> FightGuardsAsync(Process game) => CheatCommandAsync(game,"--guards-fight");
    internal static Task<string> DisableCamerasAsync(Process game) => CheatCommandAsync(game,"--cameras-disable");
    internal static Task<string> PingCheatsAsync(Process game) => CheatCommandAsync(game,"--cheats-ping");

    private static async Task<string> CheatCommandAsync(Process game,string command)
    {
        if(!await Requests.WaitAsync(0))throw new InvalidOperationException("Wait for the current trainer request to finish.");
        try
        {
            ValidateGame(game);
            if(await RunHelperAsync("--version")!="SCTravelHost:0.23.0:protocol22")
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Loader version mismatch. Run Launch Trainer.cmd.");
            return await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(),command);
        }
        finally{Requests.Release();}
    }

    internal static async Task<NpcSnapshot[]?> ReadNpcsAsync(Process game)
    {
        if(!await Requests.WaitAsync(0))return null;
        try { return NpcSnapshot.Parse(await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(),"--npc-list")); }
        finally { Requests.Release(); }
    }

    internal static async Task<string> NpcActionAsync(Process game,string action,uint id=0,int health=100,NpcConditionEdit? condition=null,NpcActivityEdit? activity=null,NpcPosition? position=null,bool waitForCompletion=true)
    {
        string command=action switch {
            "spawn"=>"--npc-spawn",
            "front" when id>0=>$"--npc-front:{id}",
            "to-sam" when id>0=>$"--npc-to-sam:{id}",
            "position" when id>0 && position.HasValue && position.Value.IsValid=>FormattableString.Invariant($"--npc-position:{id}:{position.Value.X:R}:{position.Value.Y:R}:{position.Value.Z:R}"),
            "remove" when id>0=>$"--npc-remove:{id}",
            "activity" when id>0 && activity.HasValue && Enum.IsDefined(activity.Value)=>$"--npc-activity:{id}:{(int)activity.Value}",
            "condition" when id>0 && condition is NpcConditionEdit.Alive or NpcConditionEdit.KnockedOut or NpcConditionEdit.Dead=>$"--npc-condition:{id}:{(int)condition.Value}",
            "health" when id>0 && health is >=1 and <=100=>$"--npc-health:{id}:{health}",
            _=>throw new ArgumentException("Invalid NPC action.")
        };
        await Requests.WaitAsync();
        try
        {
            ValidateGame(game);
            if(await RunHelperAsync("--version")!="SCTravelHost:0.23.0:protocol22")
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Loader version mismatch. Run Launch Trainer.cmd.");
            await GameWindowFocus.PrepareAsync(game.MainWindowHandle);
            var reply=await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(),command);
            if(action is not ("remove" or "condition") || (action=="condition"&&!waitForCompletion))return reply;
            for(int attempt=0;attempt<(action=="condition"?75:25);++attempt)
            {
                await Task.Delay(200);game.Refresh();
                if(game.HasExited || game.MainWindowTitle.Contains("Critical Error",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("The game stopped during the NPC update.");
                var entries=NpcSnapshot.Parse(await RunHelperAsync(game.Id.ToString(),"--npc-list"));
                if(action=="remove"&&!entries.Any(n=>n.Id==id&&n.Present))return $"NPC {id} removed.";
                if(action=="condition") {
                    var npc=entries.FirstOrDefault(n=>n.Id==id&&n.Present);
                    string expected=condition switch {NpcConditionEdit.Alive=>"Alive",NpcConditionEdit.KnockedOut=>"Knocked out",_=>"Dead"};
                    if(npc?.Condition==expected)return $"NPC {id}: {expected}.";
                    if(npc is null)throw new InvalidOperationException("The selected NPC disappeared before its condition was confirmed.");
                }
            }
            throw new InvalidOperationException(action=="condition"?"Condition change requested but not yet confirmed. Leave the game focused and unpaused.":"Removal scheduled but not yet confirmed. Leave the game focused and unpaused.");
        }
        finally{Requests.Release();}
    }

    private static async Task<string> RunHelperAsync(params string[] arguments)
    {
        var host = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "SCTravelHost.exe");
        if (!File.Exists(host)) throw new FileNotFoundException("Native loader missing. Run Launch Trainer.cmd.", host);
        var start = new ProcessStartInfo(host)
        {
            UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true,
            RedirectStandardOutput = true, RedirectStandardError = true
        };
        foreach (var argument in arguments) start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
        using var helper = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not start level loader.");
        var output = helper.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
        var error = helper.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync();
        await helper.WaitForExitAsync();
        var message = ((await output) + (await error)).Trim();
        if (helper.ExitCode != 0)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(string.IsNullOrEmpty(message) ? "Loader request failed." : message);
        return message;
    }

    internal static void ValidateGame(Process game)
    {
        var checkedModules = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (ProcessModule module in game.Modules)
        {
            if (!SupportedFiles.TryGetValue(module.ModuleName, out var expected)) continue;
            using var stream = File.OpenRead(module.FileName);
            if (Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(stream)) != expected)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unsupported game build: " + module.ModuleName + ". Direct loading was not attempted.");
            checkedModules.Add(module.ModuleName);
        }
        if (checkedModules.Count != SupportedFiles.Count)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Game is still starting or required modules are missing. Wait for the main menu.");

    }
}
```

`EnemyMemoryReader.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Numerics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;

namespace SCTrainer;

internal sealed record EnemyMarker(Vector3 Position, float Height, string Name);
internal sealed record EnemySnapshot(Vector3 Camera, int Pitch, int Yaw, int Roll, float Fov, EnemyMarker[] Enemies);

internal sealed class EnemyMemoryReader : IDisposable
{
    private readonly Process _game;
    private readonly SafeProcessHandle _handle;
    private readonly uint _core;
    private readonly uint _viewportVtable;
    private uint _viewport;
    private readonly Dictionary<uint, string> _names = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<uint, string[]> _classes = new();
    private uint[] _actors = Array.Empty<uint>();
    private DateTime _nextScan;
    private uint _lastLevel;
    private byte[]? _scanEntries;
    private int _scanIndex;
    private readonly List<uint> _scanActors = new();
    public IntPtr GameWindow { get; }
    public int GameId => _game.Id;

    public EnemyMemoryReader(Process game)
    {
        _game = game;
        DirectLevelLoader.ValidateGame(game);
        uint core = 0, win = 0;
        foreach (ProcessModule module in game.Modules)
        {
            if (module.ModuleName.Equals("Core.dll", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) core = (uint)module.BaseAddress.ToInt64();
            if (module.ModuleName.Equals("WinDrv.dll", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) win = (uint)module.BaseAddress.ToInt64();
        }
        if (core == 0 || win == 0) throw new InvalidOperationException("Game modules not ready.");
        _core = core;
        // These RVAs and reflected field offsets are restricted to the validated Steam build.
        _viewportVtable = win + 0x12240;
        GameWindow = game.MainWindowHandle;
        if (GameWindow == IntPtr.Zero) throw new InvalidOperationException("Game window not ready.");
        _handle = OpenProcess(0x410, false, game.Id);
        if (_handle.IsInvalid) { _handle.Dispose(); throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error()); }
    }

    private byte[] Read(uint address, int size)
    {
        if (address < 0x10000 || size < 0 || size > 8_000_000) throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid game data.");
        var bytes = new byte[size];
        if (!ReadProcessMemory(_handle, (IntPtr)(long)address, bytes, (nuint)size, out var got) || got != (nuint)size)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Game data changed. Waiting for the next frame.");
        return bytes;
    }

    private uint U(uint address) => BitConverter.ToUInt32(Read(address, 4));
    private float F(uint address) => BitConverter.ToSingle(Read(address, 4));
    private Vector3 V(uint address)
    {
        var bytes = Read(address, 12);
        var vector = new Vector3(BitConverter.ToSingle(bytes, 0), BitConverter.ToSingle(bytes, 4), BitConverter.ToSingle(bytes, 8));
        if (!float.IsFinite(vector.X) || !float.IsFinite(vector.Y) || !float.IsFinite(vector.Z))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Camera or actor position is not ready.");
        return vector;
    }

    private string Name(uint index)
    {
        if (_names.TryGetValue(index, out var name)) return name;
        uint count = U(_core + 0x18DD80);
        if (index >= count || count > 1_000_000) return "";
        uint entry = U(U(_core + 0x18DD7C) + index * 4);
        var text = System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetString(Read(entry + 12, 128)).Split('\0')[0];
        _names[index] = text;
        return text;
    }

    private string[] ClassChain(uint cls)
    {
        if (_classes.TryGetValue(cls, out var chain)) return chain;
        var list = new List<string>();
        uint current = cls;
        for (int i = 0; i < 24 && current != 0; i++)
        {
            list.Add(Name(U(current + 0x20)));
            current = U(current + 0x2C);
        }
        chain = list.ToArray();
        _classes[cls] = chain;
        return chain;
    }

    private void Scan()
    {
        if (_scanEntries is null)
        {
            uint table = U(_core + 0x19518C), count = U(_core + 0x195190);
            if (count > 250_000) throw new InvalidOperationException("Unsupported actor table.");
            _scanEntries = Read(table, checked((int)count * 4));
            _scanIndex = 0;
            _scanActors.Clear();
            _classes.Clear();
        }
        // Bound discovery work so a complete object scan cannot stall one camera frame.
        int end = Math.Min(_scanEntries.Length, _scanIndex + 256 * 4);
        for (; _scanIndex < end; _scanIndex += 4)
        {
            uint obj = BitConverter.ToUInt32(_scanEntries, _scanIndex);
            if (obj == 0) continue;
            try
            {
                var header = Read(obj, 40);
                if (BitConverter.ToUInt32(header, 0) == _viewportVtable) _viewport = obj;
                var chain = ClassChain(BitConverter.ToUInt32(header, 0x24));
                if (chain.Contains("EAIPawn") && !chain.Contains("EAINonHostile")) _scanActors.Add(obj);
            }
            catch (InvalidOperationException) { }
        }
        if (_scanIndex == _scanEntries.Length)
        {
            _actors = _scanActors.ToArray();
            _scanEntries = null;
            _nextScan = DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(2);
        }
    }
    public EnemySnapshot Snapshot()
    {
        if (_game.HasExited) throw new InvalidOperationException("Game closed.");
        if (_scanEntries is not null || _viewport == 0 || DateTime.UtcNow >= _nextScan) Scan();
        if (_viewport == 0) throw new InvalidOperationException("Finding game actors…");
        uint player = U(_viewport + 0x34);
        if (!ClassChain(U(player + 0x24)).Contains("EPlayerController"))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Load a mission to show enemy markers.");
        uint level = U(player + 0x6C);
        if (level != _lastLevel)
        {
            _lastLevel = level;
            _actors = Array.Empty<uint>();
            _scanEntries = null;
            _scanActors.Clear();
            _classes.Clear();
            _nextScan = DateTime.MinValue;
            // Never reuse an actor list from the previous map.
            return new EnemySnapshot(Vector3.Zero, 0, 0, 0, 75, Array.Empty<EnemyMarker>());
        }
        uint camera = U(player + 0x608);
        if (!ClassChain(U(camera + 0x24)).Contains("EPlayerCam"))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Waiting for the gameplay camera.");
        var markers = new List<EnemyMarker>();
        foreach (uint actor in _actors)
        {
            try
            {
                var data = Read(actor, 0xA54);
                if (BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, 0x6C) != level || !ClassChain(BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, 0x24)).Contains("EAIPawn")) continue;
                if ((BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, 0x2C) & 0x10) != 0 || (BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, 0xA50) & 1) == 0) continue;
                if (BitConverter.ToInt32(data, 0x330) <= 0) continue;
                float height = BitConverter.ToSingle(data, 0x1B0);
                if (!float.IsFinite(height) || height < 5 || height > 200) height = 40;
                var location = new Vector3(BitConverter.ToSingle(data, 0xD4), BitConverter.ToSingle(data, 0xD8), BitConverter.ToSingle(data, 0xDC));
                if (!float.IsFinite(location.X) || !float.IsFinite(location.Y) || !float.IsFinite(location.Z)) continue;
                markers.Add(new EnemyMarker(location, height, Name(BitConverter.ToUInt32(data, 0x20))));
            }
            catch (InvalidOperationException) { }
        }
        // PlayerController caches the rendered camera view.
        var position = V(player + 0xD4);
        var rotation = Read(player + 0xE0, 12);
        float fov = F(player + 0x2B0);
        if (!float.IsFinite(fov) || fov < 10 || fov > 170) throw new InvalidOperationException("Camera field of view unavailable.");
        return new EnemySnapshot(position, BitConverter.ToInt32(rotation, 0), BitConverter.ToInt32(rotation, 4),
            BitConverter.ToInt32(rotation, 8), fov, markers.ToArray());
    }

    public void Dispose() { _handle.Dispose(); _game.Dispose(); }

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern SafeProcessHandle OpenProcess(uint access, bool inherit, int pid);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool ReadProcessMemory(SafeProcessHandle process, IntPtr address, [Out] byte[] buffer, nuint size, out nuint read);
}
```

`EnemyOverlay.cs`:

```cs
using System.Globalization;
using System.Numerics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace SCTrainer;

internal sealed class EnemyOverlay : Window
{
    private readonly EnemyMemoryReader _reader;
    private readonly Action<string> _status;
    private readonly DispatcherTimer _timer = new(DispatcherPriority.Render) { Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(16) };
    private readonly MarkerSurface _surface = new();
    private bool _busy, _closed;
    private IntPtr _hwnd;
    private DateTime _lastStatus;
    public EnemyOverlay(EnemyMemoryReader reader, Action<string> status)
    {
        _reader = reader; _status = status;
        WindowStyle = WindowStyle.None; AllowsTransparency = true; Background = Brushes.Transparent;
        ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize; ShowInTaskbar = false; ShowActivated = false; Topmost = true;
        Content = _surface; Width = 1; Height = 1; Left = -10000; Top = -10000;
        SourceInitialized += (_, _) =>
        {
            _hwnd = new WindowInteropHelper(this).Handle;
            int style = GetWindowLong(_hwnd, -20);
            SetWindowLong(_hwnd, -20, style | 0x20 | 0x80 | 0x08000000); // Click-through, tool window, no activation.
        };
        _timer.Tick += Tick;
        Closed += (_, _) => { _closed = true; _timer.Stop(); _timer.Tick -= Tick; if (!_busy) _reader.Dispose(); };
        _timer.Start();
    }

    private async void Tick(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        if (_busy || _closed) return;
        _busy = true;
        try
        {
            var game = _reader.GameWindow;
            if (!IsWindow(game)) { _status("Game closed. Enable ESP again after restarting."); Close(); return; }
            if (IsIconic(game) || GetForegroundWindow() != game)
            {
                _surface.Frame = null; _surface.InvalidateVisual();
                return;
            }
            if (!GetClientRect(game, out var rect)) return;
            var origin = new PointNative();
            if (!ClientToScreen(game, ref origin)) return;
            int width = rect.Right - rect.Left, height = rect.Bottom - rect.Top;
            if (width < 1 || height < 1) return;
            SetWindowPos(_hwnd, new IntPtr(-1), origin.X, origin.Y, width, height, 0x10 | 0x40);
            var snapshot = await Task.Run(_reader.Snapshot);
            if (_closed) return;
            // Focus may change while the snapshot is being read.
            _surface.Frame = GetForegroundWindow() == game ? snapshot : null;
            _surface.InvalidateVisual();
            if (DateTime.UtcNow - _lastStatus > TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1))
            {
                _lastStatus = DateTime.UtcNow;
                _status($"ESP enabled • {snapshot.Enemies.Length} live hostile characters tracked");
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or OverflowException)
        {
            _surface.Frame = null; _surface.InvalidateVisual();
            _status(ex.Message);
        }
        finally { _busy = false; if (_closed) _reader.Dispose(); }
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] private struct RectNative { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] private struct PointNative { public int X, Y; }
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool GetClientRect(IntPtr window, out RectNative rect);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool ClientToScreen(IntPtr window, ref PointNative point);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool IsWindow(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern int GetWindowLong(IntPtr window, int index);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern int SetWindowLong(IntPtr window, int index, int value);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr window, IntPtr after, int x, int y, int width, int height, uint flags);

    private sealed class MarkerSurface : FrameworkElement
    {
        public EnemySnapshot? Frame { get; set; }
        private static readonly Pen Outline = new(Brushes.Black, 4);
        private static readonly Pen Green = new(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(181, 239, 101)), 1.5);
        protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext dc)
        {
            base.OnRender(dc);
            var f = Frame;
            if (f is null) return;
            dc.PushClip(new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(0, 0, ActualWidth, ActualHeight)));
            foreach (var enemy in f.Enemies)
            {
                if (!EnemyProjection.TryProject(enemy.Position + new Vector3(0, 0, enemy.Height), f.Camera,
                    f.Pitch, f.Yaw, f.Roll, f.Fov, ActualWidth, ActualHeight, out var x, out var y)) continue;
                if (x < 0 || x > ActualWidth || y < 0 || y > ActualHeight) continue;
                var diamond = new StreamGeometry();
                using (var context = diamond.Open())
                {
                    context.BeginFigure(new Point(x, y - 6), false, true);
                    context.LineTo(new Point(x + 6, y), true, false);
                    context.LineTo(new Point(x, y + 6), true, false);
                    context.LineTo(new Point(x - 6, y), true, false);
                }
                dc.DrawGeometry(null, Outline, diamond); dc.DrawGeometry(null, Green, diamond);
                double distance = Vector3.Distance(f.Camera, enemy.Position);
                var label = new FormattedText($"{enemy.Name} • {distance:0} units", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface("Segoe UI"), 12, Green.Brush, VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(this).PixelsPerDip);
                dc.DrawRectangle(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(190, 0, 0, 0)), null,
                    new Rect(x - label.Width / 2 - 4, y + 9, label.Width + 8, label.Height + 4));
                dc.DrawText(label, new Point(x - label.Width / 2, y + 11));
            }
            dc.Pop();
        }
    }
}
```

`EnemyProjection.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.Numerics;

namespace SCTrainer;

public static class EnemyProjection
{
    public static bool TryProject(Vector3 point, Vector3 camera, int pitch, int yaw, int roll,
        float horizontalFov, double width, double height, out double x, out double y)
    {
        x = y = 0;
        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || !float.IsFinite(horizontalFov) || horizontalFov <= 5 || horizontalFov >= 175) return false;
        const double unit = Math.PI * 2 / 65536;
        double p = pitch * unit, a = yaw * unit, r = roll * unit;
        var forward = new Vector3((float)(Math.Cos(p) * Math.Cos(a)), (float)(Math.Cos(p) * Math.Sin(a)), (float)Math.Sin(p));
        var right = new Vector3((float)(Math.Sin(r) * Math.Sin(p) * Math.Cos(a) - Math.Cos(r) * Math.Sin(a)),
            (float)(Math.Sin(r) * Math.Sin(p) * Math.Sin(a) + Math.Cos(r) * Math.Cos(a)), (float)(-Math.Sin(r) * Math.Cos(p)));
        var up = Vector3.Cross(forward, right);
        var delta = point - camera;
        double depth = Vector3.Dot(delta, forward);
        if (!double.IsFinite(depth) || depth < 1) return false;
        double scale = width / (2 * Math.Tan(horizontalFov * Math.PI / 360));
        x = width / 2 + Vector3.Dot(delta, right) * scale / depth;
        y = height / 2 - Vector3.Dot(delta, up) * scale / depth;
        return double.IsFinite(x) && double.IsFinite(y);
    }
}
```

`GameMenuOverlay.cs`:

```cs
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace SCTrainer;

internal sealed record GameMenuEntry(string Label, bool On, bool Available, string Key, string? Value=null, bool Toggle=true,bool Navigation=false,GameMenuInput? Input=null);
internal sealed record GameMenuFrame(string Key,string Title,GameMenuEntry[] Entries);

// Desktop overlay for the windowed game. Keyboard handling never enters the game process.
internal sealed class GameMenuOverlay : Window
{
    private readonly Func<GameMenuFrame> _entries;
    private readonly Func<string,Task<string>> _activate;
    private readonly DispatcherTimer _timer=new() { Interval=TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100) };
    private readonly StackPanel _rows=new();
    private readonly TextBlock _message=new() { Foreground=new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(158,174,153)), FontSize=11, TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap, Margin=new Thickness(0,10,0,0) };
    private readonly Border _panel;
    private readonly Func<Task> _refresh;
    private readonly TextBlock _title=new() {Foreground=Brushes.White,FontSize=11,Margin=new Thickness(0,3,0,12)};
    private GameMenuFrame _frame=new("main","TRAINER MENU",Array.Empty<GameMenuEntry>());
    private bool _refreshing;
    private string? _selectedKey;
    private OverlayTextEditor? _editing;
    private readonly HookProc _hookProc;
    private readonly HashSet<int> _held=new();
    private IntPtr _hook,_hwnd,_game;
    private bool _open,_applying,_closed;
    private int _selected;
    private DateTime _nextDiscovery,_lastMove;
    private static readonly Brush Accent=new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(181,239,101));

    public GameMenuOverlay(Func<GameMenuFrame> entries,Func<string,Task<string>> activate,Func<Task> refresh)
    {
        _entries=entries;_activate=activate;_refresh=refresh;_hookProc=Keyboard;
        WindowStyle=WindowStyle.None;AllowsTransparency=true;Background=Brushes.Transparent;
        ResizeMode=ResizeMode.NoResize;ShowInTaskbar=false;ShowActivated=false;Topmost=true;
        Width=1;Height=1;Left=-10000;Top=-10000;
        var body=new StackPanel();
        body.Children.Add(new TextBlock {Text="SPLINTER ASSAULT",Foreground=Accent,FontSize=16,FontWeight=FontWeights.Bold});
        body.Children.Add(_title);
        body.Children.Add(_rows);
        body.Children.Add(new TextBlock {Text="↑ / ↓  Select     Enter  Select\n← / Esc  Back     F6  Close",Foreground=Accent,FontSize=11,Margin=new Thickness(0,12,0,0)});
        body.Children.Add(_message);
        _panel=new Border { Background=new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(238,11,19,14)),BorderBrush=Accent,BorderThickness=new Thickness(1),CornerRadius=new CornerRadius(6),Padding=new Thickness(14),Child=new ScrollViewer {Content=body,VerticalScrollBarVisibility=ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,HorizontalScrollBarVisibility=ScrollBarVisibility.Disabled},Visibility=Visibility.Hidden };
        Content=_panel;
        SourceInitialized+=(_,_)=>{
            _hwnd=new WindowInteropHelper(this).Handle;
            SetWindowLong(_hwnd,-20,GetWindowLong(_hwnd,-20)|0x20|0x80|0x08000000);
            _hook=SetWindowsHookEx(13,_hookProc,GetModuleHandle(null),0);
            if(_hook==IntPtr.Zero)throw new Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
        };
        _timer.Tick+=Tick;
        Closed+=(_,_)=>{_closed=true;_open=false;_timer.Stop();_timer.Tick-=Tick;if(_hook!=IntPtr.Zero){UnhookWindowsHookEx(_hook);_hook=IntPtr.Zero;}};
        _timer.Start();
    }

    private IntPtr Keyboard(int code,IntPtr message,IntPtr data)
    {
        if(code<0||_closed||_game==IntPtr.Zero||GetForegroundWindow()!=_game||IsIconic(_game))
            return CallNextHookEx(_hook,code,message,data);
        int key=Marshal.ReadInt32(data);
        if(_open&&_editing is not null&&key!=0x75)return EditKeyboard(code,message,data,key);
        bool down=message.ToInt64() is 0x100 or 0x104;
        bool up=message.ToInt64() is 0x101 or 0x105;
        if(key!=0x75&&(!_open||key is not (0x26 or 0x28 or 0x25 or 0x0d or 0x1b))&&!_held.Contains(key))
            return CallNextHookEx(_hook,code,message,data);
        if(up){_held.Remove(key);return new IntPtr(1);}
        if(!down)return CallNextHookEx(_hook,code,message,data);
        bool repeat=!_held.Add(key);
        if(key==0x75&&!repeat){_open=!_open;_editing=null;_message.Text="";}
        else if(_open&&key is 0x1b or 0x25&&!repeat) {
            if(_frame.Key=="main")_open=false;
            else Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(async()=>await ApplyAsync("back")));
        }
        else if(_open&&key is 0x26 or 0x28) {
            int count=_frame.Entries.Length;
            if(count>0&&(!repeat||DateTime.UtcNow-_lastMove>TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(140))){_selected=(_selected+(key==0x28?1:count-1))%count;_selectedKey=_frame.Entries[_selected].Key;_lastMove=DateTime.UtcNow;}
        }
        else if(_open&&key==0x0d&&!repeat) {
            // Return from the hook immediately; native cheat requests run asynchronously later.
            string? selected=_selected>=0&&_selected<_frame.Entries.Length?_frame.Entries[_selected].Key:null;
            if(selected is not null)Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(async()=>await ApplyAsync(selected)));
        }
        return new IntPtr(1);
    }

    private async Task ApplyAsync(string selected,string? inputValue=null)
    {
        if(_closed||!_open||GetForegroundWindow()!=_game)return;
        var entry=_entries().Entries.FirstOrDefault(e=>e.Key==selected);
        bool navigation=inputValue is null&&(selected=="back"||entry?.Navigation==true);
        if(selected!="back"&&entry?.Available!=true){if(inputValue is not null)_message.Text="That NPC control is no longer available. Select the NPC again.";return;}
        if(_applying&&!navigation)return;
        if(entry?.Input is not null&&inputValue is null){_editing=new OverlayTextEditor(selected,entry.Input);_message.Text="Type a value / Enter apply / Esc cancel / Ctrl+A select all";return;}
        if(inputValue is not null){if(entry?.Input is null)return;selected+=":"+Uri.EscapeDataString(inputValue);}
        if(navigation) {
            try {_message.Text=await _activate(selected);}
            catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or FormatException or ArgumentException){_message.Text=ex.Message;}
            return;
        }
        _applying=true;_message.Text="Applying…";
        try {_message.Text=await _activate(selected);}
        catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or FormatException or ArgumentException) {_message.Text=ex.Message;}
        finally {_applying=false;}
    }

    private async void Tick(object? sender,EventArgs e)
    {
        if(_closed)return;
        if(DateTime.UtcNow>=_nextDiscovery){
            _nextDiscovery=DateTime.UtcNow.AddSeconds(1);
            var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
            try {_game=games.Length==1?games[0].MainWindowHandle:IntPtr.Zero;}
            catch(InvalidOperationException){_game=IntPtr.Zero;}
            finally{foreach(var game in games)game.Dispose();}
        }
        bool focused=_game!=IntPtr.Zero&&IsWindow(_game)&&!IsIconic(_game)&&GetForegroundWindow()==_game;
        if(!focused){_open=false;_editing=null;_held.Clear();_panel.Visibility=Visibility.Hidden;return;}
        if(!_open){_panel.Visibility=Visibility.Hidden;return;}
        if(!GetClientRect(_game,out var rect))return;
        var origin=new PointNative();if(!ClientToScreen(_game,ref origin))return;
        double scale=VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(this).DpiScaleX;
        int width=Math.Min((int)(310*scale),Math.Max(1,rect.Right-24));
        int height=Math.Min((int)(470*scale),Math.Max(1,rect.Bottom-32));
        SetWindowPos(_hwnd,new IntPtr(-1),origin.X+12,origin.Y+16,width,height,0x10|0x40);
        _panel.Visibility=Visibility.Visible;
        if(_editing is not null){
            _title.Text="EDIT / "+_editing.Input.Label;_rows.Children.Clear();
            _rows.Children.Add(new TextBlock {Text=_editing.Display,Foreground=Accent,FontSize=15,TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap,Margin=new Thickness(0,8,0,8)});
            _rows.Children.Add(new TextBlock {Text="Enter: apply   Esc: cancel\nBackspace / Delete: edit\nCtrl+A: select all\nDecimal coordinates use a dot.",Foreground=Brushes.White,FontSize=12,TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap});
            return;
        }
        var frame=_entries();
        if(frame.Key!=_frame.Key){_selected=0;_selectedKey=null;}
        var items=frame.Entries;
        if(_selectedKey is not null){int index=Array.FindIndex(items,e=>e.Key==_selectedKey);if(index>=0)_selected=index;}
        _frame=frame;_title.Text=frame.Title;_rows.Children.Clear();
        _selected=Math.Clamp(_selected,0,Math.Max(0,items.Length-1));
        _selectedKey=items.Length>0?items[_selected].Key:null;
        for(int i=0;i<items.Length;++i){
            var item=items[i];
            var line=new DockPanel();
            var state=new TextBlock {Text=item.Toggle?(item.On?"ON":"OFF"):(item.Value??"›"),Foreground=item.On?Accent:Brushes.Gray,FontWeight=FontWeights.Bold,FontSize=11,Margin=new Thickness(8,0,0,0)};
            DockPanel.SetDock(state,Dock.Right);line.Children.Add(state);
            line.Children.Add(new TextBlock {Text=(i==_selected?"›  ":"   ")+item.Label,Foreground=item.Available?Brushes.White:Brushes.Gray,FontSize=12,TextWrapping=TextWrapping.Wrap});
            _rows.Children.Add(new Border {Child=line,Padding=new Thickness(5,7,5,7),CornerRadius=new CornerRadius(3),Background=i==_selected?new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(42,64,30)):Brushes.Transparent});
        }
        if(_rows.Children.Count>_selected)((FrameworkElement)_rows.Children[_selected]).BringIntoView();
        if(!_refreshing&&!_applying) {
            _refreshing=true;
            try {await _refresh();}
            catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or FormatException or ArgumentException) {_message.Text=ex.Message;}
            finally {_refreshing=false;}
        }
    }

    private IntPtr EditKeyboard(int code,IntPtr message,IntPtr data,int key)
    {
        // Do not interfere with OS window switching. Focus loss cancels the editor.
        if(key is 0x5b or 0x5c||GetAsyncKeyState(0x12)<0)return CallNextHookEx(_hook,code,message,data);
        bool down=message.ToInt64() is 0x100 or 0x104;
        if(!down){_held.Remove(key);return new IntPtr(1);}
        bool repeat=!_held.Add(key);
        if(repeat&&key is 0x0d or 0x1b)return new IntPtr(1);
        var edit=_editing;if(edit is null)return new IntPtr(1);
        if(key==0x1b){_editing=null;_message.Text="Edit cancelled.";return new IntPtr(1);}
        if(key==0x0d){
            try {string value=edit.Value();_editing=null;Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(async()=>await ApplyAsync(edit.Key,value)));}
            catch(ArgumentException ex){_message.Text=ex.Message;}
            return new IntPtr(1);
        }
        if(GetAsyncKeyState(0x11)<0){if(key==0x41)edit.SelectAll();return new IntPtr(1);}
        switch(key){
            case 0x08:edit.Backspace();break;
            case 0x2e:edit.Delete();break;
            case 0x25:edit.Move(-1);break;
            case 0x27:edit.Move(1);break;
            case 0x24:edit.Home();break;
            case 0x23:edit.End();break;
            default:
                var state=new byte[256];GetKeyboardState(state);state[key]=0x80;
                foreach(int modifier in new[]{0x10,0x11,0x12})if(GetAsyncKeyState(modifier)<0)state[modifier]=0x80;
                var text=new System.Text.StringBuilder(8);
                uint thread=GetWindowThreadProcessId(_game,out _);
                int length=ToUnicodeEx((uint)key,(uint)Marshal.ReadInt32(data,4),state,text,text.Capacity,4,GetKeyboardLayout(thread));
                if(length>0)edit.Insert(text.ToString(0,Math.Min(length,text.Length)));
                break;
        }
        return new IntPtr(1);
    }
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern short GetAsyncKeyState(int key);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool GetKeyboardState(byte[] state);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr window,out uint process);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern IntPtr GetKeyboardLayout(uint thread);
    [DllImport("user32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode,ExactSpelling=true)]private static extern int ToUnicodeEx(uint key,uint scan,byte[] state,System.Text.StringBuilder text,int capacity,uint flags,IntPtr layout);

    private delegate IntPtr HookProc(int code,IntPtr message,IntPtr data);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]private struct RectNative {public int Left,Top,Right,Bottom;}
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]private struct PointNative {public int X,Y;}
    [DllImport("user32.dll",SetLastError=true)]private static extern IntPtr SetWindowsHookEx(int id,HookProc callback,IntPtr module,uint thread);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool UnhookWindowsHookEx(IntPtr hook);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern IntPtr CallNextHookEx(IntPtr hook,int code,IntPtr message,IntPtr data);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)]private static extern IntPtr GetModuleHandle(string? name);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool IsWindow(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool GetClientRect(IntPtr window,out RectNative rect);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool ClientToScreen(IntPtr window,ref PointNative point);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern int GetWindowLong(IntPtr window,int index);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern int SetWindowLong(IntPtr window,int index,int value);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]private static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr window,IntPtr after,int x,int y,int width,int height,uint flags);
```

`GameWindowFocus.cs`:

```cs
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace SCTrainer;

internal static class GameWindowFocus
{
    public static async Task PrepareAsync(IntPtr window)
    {
        if (window == IntPtr.Zero || !IsWindow(window))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Game window is not ready.");
        if (IsIconic(window)) ShowWindowAsync(window, 9);
        SetForegroundWindow(window);
        for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++)
        {
            if (GetForegroundWindow() == window && !IsIconic(window))
            {
                // Let the renderer reacquire its device before loading draws progress frames.
                await Task.Delay(350);
                if (GetForegroundWindow() == window) return;
            }
            await Task.Delay(100);
        }
        throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not focus the game safely. No map request was sent. Restore the game, then try again.");
    }

    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool IsWindow(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool ShowWindowAsync(IntPtr window, int command);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr window);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] private static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
}
```

`InfiniteHealthSession.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;

namespace SCTrainer;

internal sealed class InfiniteHealthSession : IDisposable
{
    private readonly Process _game;
    private readonly SafeProcessHandle _handle;
    private readonly uint _viewport, _player, _index, _offset, _mask;
    public bool Enabled { get; private set; }

    private InfiniteHealthSession(Process game, string reply)
    {
        _game = game;
        if (!reply.StartsWith("HEALTH:")) throw new InvalidOperationException("Unexpected health-loader response.");
        var values = reply.Substring(7).Split(':').Select(x => uint.Parse(x, NumberStyles.HexNumber)).ToArray();
        if (values.Length != 5) throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid health-loader response.");
        (_viewport, _player, _index, _offset, _mask) = (values[0], values[1], values[2], values[3], values[4]);
        _handle = OpenProcess(0x410, false, game.Id);
        if (_handle.IsInvalid) { _handle.Dispose(); throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error()); }
        Enabled = true;
    }

    public static async Task<InfiniteHealthSession> EnableAsync(Process game)
    {
        // Open the read handle before writing, so a local handle error cannot leave an enabled feature behind.
        using var check = OpenProcess(0x410, false, game.Id);
        if (check.IsInvalid) throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
        var reply = await DirectLevelLoader.SetHealthAsync(game, true);
        try { return new InfiniteHealthSession(game, reply); }
        catch { await DirectLevelLoader.SetHealthAsync(game, false); throw; }
    }

    private uint U(uint address)
    {
        var buffer = new byte[4];
        if (!ReadProcessMemory(_handle, (IntPtr)(long)address, buffer, 4, out var count) || count != 4)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Player data is unavailable.");
        return BitConverter.ToUInt32(buffer);
    }

    public bool IsCurrent()
    {
        try
        {
            return !_game.HasExited && U(_viewport + 0x34) == _player && U(_player + 4) == _index &&
                (U(_player + _offset) & _mask) != 0;
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException) { return false; }
    }

    public async Task DisableAsync()
    {
        if (!Enabled) return;
        if (IsCurrent()) await DirectLevelLoader.SetHealthAsync(_game, false);
        Enabled = false;
    }

    public void Dispose() { _handle.Dispose(); _game.Dispose(); }

    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern SafeProcessHandle OpenProcess(uint access, bool inherit, int pid);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool ReadProcessMemory(SafeProcessHandle process, IntPtr address, [Out] byte[] buffer, nuint size, out nuint read);
}

```

`Launch Trainer.cmd`:

```cmd
@echo off
setlocal
set "TRAINER_ROOT=%~dp0"
set "TRAINER_BUILD_LOG=%TRAINER_ROOT%bin\launcher-build.log"
:choose_output
set "TRAINER_OUTPUT=%TRAINER_ROOT%bin\Runs\v0.23.0-%RANDOM%-%RANDOM%"
if exist "%TRAINER_OUTPUT%" goto choose_output
if not exist "%TRAINER_ROOT%bin" mkdir "%TRAINER_ROOT%bin"
echo Building Splinter Assault Trainer...
call "%TRAINER_ROOT%Native\build.cmd" >"%TRAINER_BUILD_LOG%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 goto failed
cd /d "%TRAINER_ROOT%"
dotnet build "%TRAINER_ROOT%SCTrainer.csproj" --nologo -o "%TRAINER_OUTPUT%" >>"%TRAINER_BUILD_LOG%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 goto failed
if /i "%~1"=="--build-only" goto built
start "" "%TRAINER_OUTPUT%\SCTrainer.exe"
exit /b 0
:built
echo Build ready: %TRAINER_OUTPUT%\SCTrainer.exe
exit /b 0
:failed
type "%TRAINER_BUILD_LOG%"
echo Trainer build failed. No older trainer was launched.
if /i not "%~1"=="--build-only" pause
exit /b 1
```

`LevelOverlayMenu.cs`:

```cs
namespace SCTrainer;

internal sealed record LevelMenuChoice(string Map,string Label)
{
    public string Mission {get {int split=Label.IndexOf("  /  ",StringComparison.Ordinal);return split<0?"Additional maps":Label[..split];}}
}

internal sealed class LevelOverlayMenu
{
    private readonly Func<LevelMenuChoice[]> _catalog;
    private readonly Func<LevelMenuChoice,Task<string>> _load;
    private string? _mission,_confirm;
    public bool Busy {get;private set;}
    public LevelOverlayMenu(Func<LevelMenuChoice[]> catalog,Func<LevelMenuChoice,Task<string>> load){_catalog=catalog;_load=load;}
    private static GameMenuEntry Row(string label,string key,bool available=true,bool navigation=false)=>new(label,false,available,key,null,false,navigation);
    public GameMenuFrame Frame
    {
        get {
            var rows=new List<GameMenuEntry>();
            string title="LOAD LEVEL / MAIN MENU ONLY";
            var maps=_catalog();
            if(_confirm is not null){
                title="LOAD SELECTED MAP?";
                rows.Add(Row("Cancel","level-cancel",navigation:true));
                rows.Add(Row($"Load {_confirm}","load:"+_confirm,!Busy));
            } else if(_mission is not null){
                title="LOAD LEVEL / "+_mission;
                foreach(var map in maps.Where(m=>m.Mission==_mission))rows.Add(Row(map.Map,"level:"+map.Map,navigation:true));
            } else {
                foreach(var mission in maps.Select(m=>m.Mission).Distinct())rows.Add(Row(mission,"mission:"+mission,navigation:true));
                if(maps.Length==0)rows.Add(Row("No installed maps found","no-levels",false));
            }
            rows.Add(Row("Back","back",navigation:true));
            return new($"levels:{_mission}:{_confirm}",title,rows.ToArray());
        }
    }
    public void Open(){_mission=null;_confirm=null;}
    public bool Back(){if(_confirm is not null){_confirm=null;return true;}if(_mission is not null){_mission=null;return true;}return false;}
    public async Task<string> ActivateAsync(string key)
    {
        var maps=_catalog();
        if(key=="level-cancel"){_confirm=null;return "Load cancelled.";}
        if(key.StartsWith("mission:",StringComparison.Ordinal)){
            var mission=key[8..];
            if(!maps.Any(m=>m.Mission==mission))throw new InvalidOperationException("That mission is no longer installed.");
            _mission=mission;_confirm=null;return "Return to the game's main menu before loading a map.";
        }
        if(key.StartsWith("level:",StringComparison.Ordinal)){
            string map=key[6..];
            if(!maps.Any(m=>m.Map==map&&m.Mission==_mission))throw new InvalidOperationException("That map is no longer available in this mission.");
            _confirm=map;return "Load only from the game's main menu. Unsaved progress will be lost.";
        }
        if(!key.StartsWith("load:",StringComparison.Ordinal)||key[5..]!=_confirm)throw new InvalidOperationException("Select and confirm a map first.");
        if(Busy)return "A level is already loading.";
        var selected=maps.FirstOrDefault(m=>m.Map==_confirm&&m.Mission==_mission)??throw new InvalidOperationException("The selected map is no longer installed.");
        Busy=true;
        try {return await _load(selected);}
        finally {Busy=false;}
    }
}
```
