`MainWindow.xaml.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace SCTrainer;

public partial class MainWindow : Window
{
    private readonly DispatcherTimer _timer = new() { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2) };

    public MainWindow()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        _cheatControlsReady=true;
        // WPF work-area dimensions already account for Windows display scaling.
        var workArea = SystemParameters.WorkArea;
        MaxWidth = Math.Max(1, workArea.Width - 24);
        MaxHeight = Math.Max(1, workArea.Height - 24);
        MinWidth = Math.Min(MinWidth, MaxWidth);
        MinHeight = Math.Min(MinHeight, MaxHeight);
        Width = Math.Min(Width, MaxWidth);
        Height = Math.Min(Height, MaxHeight);
        Closing += Window_Closing;
        PopulateLevels();
        RefreshGameStatus();
        _timer.Tick += Timer_Tick;
        _timer.Start();
        Closed += (_, _) => { _gameMenu?.Close(); _overlayNpcs.Dispose(); _enemyOverlay?.Close(); _timer.Stop(); _timer.Tick -= Timer_Tick; };
    }

    // The former window is a hidden control host during the tray migration; it is never shown.
    public event Action? ExitRequested;
    private bool _shutdownRunning,_shutdownApproved,_worldActionChanging;
    public bool IsBusy=>_levelLoading||_overlayNpcs.Busy||_overlayNpcs.Refreshing||_healthChanging||_cheatsChanging||_cheatRefreshBusy||_worldActionChanging;
    public void StartBackground()=>StartGameMenu();
    internal TrainerStatusSnapshot Status=>new(GameStatus.Text,GameDetail.Text,MenuStatus.Text,CheatStatus.Text,HealthStatus.Text,LevelStatus.Text,
        GameStatus.Text=="Game detected",MenuStatus.Text.StartsWith("In-game menu unavailable",StringComparison.Ordinal),_shutdownRunning);
    public string RestartOverlay()
    {
        if(_shutdownRunning||IsBusy)return MenuStatus.Text="Wait for the current trainer update to finish.";
        _gameMenu?.Close();_gameMenu=null;
        _showOverlayLevels=false;_showOverlayNpcs=false;_utilityPage=null;
        StartGameMenu();return MenuStatus.Text;
    }
    public async Task<bool> ShutdownTrainerAsync()
    {
        if(_shutdownRunning)return false;
        _shutdownRunning=true;
        _gameMenu?.Close();_gameMenu=null;
        MenuStatus.Text="Stopping trainer and restoring active cheats…";
        bool success=false;
        try {
            var waiting=Stopwatch.StartNew();
            while(IsBusy){if(waiting.Elapsed>TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60)){MenuStatus.Text="An update is still running. Wait for it to finish, then retry Exit.";return false;}await Task.Delay(100);}
            if(!await ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(new(CheatOptions.None))||!await SetInfiniteHealthAsync(false))return false;
            _shutdownApproved=true;success=true;Close();return true;
        }
        finally {
            if(!success){_shutdownRunning=false;StartGameMenu();}
        }
    }
    private void RequestExit()=>ExitRequested?.Invoke();
    private void Close_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => RequestExit();

    private GameMenuOverlay? _gameMenu;
    private readonly NpcOverlayMenu _overlayNpcs=new();
    private bool _showOverlayNpcs,_showOverlayLevels,_levelLoading;
    private string? _utilityPage;
    private LevelOverlayMenu? _overlayLevels;
    private LevelChoice[] _levels=Array.Empty<LevelChoice>();
    private static readonly CheatOptions[] MenuOptions={CheatOptions.UnlimitedAmmo,CheatOptions.NoReload,CheatOptions.UnlimitedGadgets,CheatOptions.ReducedDetection,CheatOptions.MovementSpeed,CheatOptions.Invisibility,CheatOptions.Noclip,CheatOptions.BypassLocks,CheatOptions.QuietLights};
    private void StartGameMenu()
    {
        if(_gameMenu is not null)return;
        try {
            _overlayLevels=new LevelOverlayMenu(()=>_levels.Select(l=>new LevelMenuChoice(l.Map,l.Label)).ToArray(),LoadOverlayLevelAsync);
            _gameMenu=new GameMenuOverlay(ReadGameMenu,ToggleGameMenuAsync,()=>_showOverlayNpcs&&!_levelLoading?_overlayNpcs.RefreshAsync():Task.CompletedTask);
            _gameMenu.Show();
            MenuStatus.Text="In-game menu: F6 to open / close; arrows to select; Enter to toggle.";
        }
        catch(System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception ex){_gameMenu?.Close();_gameMenu=null;MenuStatus.Text="In-game menu unavailable: "+ex.Message;}
    }
    private GameMenuFrame ReadGameMenu()
    {
        if(_shutdownRunning)return new("stopping","TRAINER STOPPING",new[]{new GameMenuEntry("Restoring active cheats…",false,false,"stopping",null,false)});
        if(_utilityPage is not null)return ReadUtilityMenu();
        if(_showOverlayLevels&&_overlayLevels is not null)return _overlayLevels.Frame;
        if(_showOverlayNpcs)return _overlayNpcs.Frame;
        bool ready=!_levelLoading&&!_overlayNpcs.Busy&&!_healthChanging&&!_cheatsChanging&&!_cheatRefreshBusy&&!_worldActionChanging;
        var settings=ReadCheatSettings();
        var items=new List<GameMenuEntry> {
            new("Enemy ESP",EnemyEspToggle.IsChecked==true,ready,"cheat:0"),
            new("Infinite health",InfiniteHealthToggle.IsChecked==true,ready,"cheat:1")
        };
        string[] labels={"Unlimited ammunition","No reload","Unlimited gadgets","Reduced detection",$"Movement speed ({settings.SpeedPercent/100.0:0.##}x)","Invisibility","Noclip (walls / doors)","Bypass locks","Quiet lights out"};
        for(int i=0;i<MenuOptions.Length;++i){
            if(MenuOptions[i]==CheatOptions.MovementSpeed)items.Add(new("Movement speed",false,true,"speed-menu",settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.MovementSpeed)?$"{settings.SpeedPercent/100.0:0.##}x":"OFF",false,true));
            else items.Add(new(labels[i],settings.Options.HasFlag(MenuOptions[i]),ready,$"cheat:{i+2}"));
        }
        items.Add(new("Enemies fight each other (30s)",false,ready,"guards-fight",null,false));
        items.Add(new("Disable all security cameras",false,ready,"cameras-disable",null,false));
        items.Add(new("Trainer tools / About / Exit",false,true,"tools-menu",null,false,true));
        items.Insert(0,new("NPC Manager",false,true,"npcs",null,false,true));
        items.Insert(0,new("Load level",false,true,"levels",null,false,true));
        return new("main","TRAINER MENU",items.ToArray());
    }
    private async Task<string> ToggleGameMenuAsync(string key)
    {
        if(key is "speed-menu" or "tools-menu"){
            _showOverlayNpcs=false;_showOverlayLevels=false;_utilityPage=key=="speed-menu"?"speed":"tools";return "";
        }
        if(key=="back"&&_utilityPage is not null){_utilityPage=_utilityPage is "about" or "exit"?"tools":null;return "";}
        if(_utilityPage is not null)return await ActivateUtilityAsync(key);
        if(key=="levels"){
            _showOverlayNpcs=false;_showOverlayLevels=true;_overlayLevels!.Open();return "Load maps from the game's main menu.";
        }
        if(key=="back"&&_showOverlayLevels){if(!_overlayLevels!.Back())_showOverlayLevels=false;return "";}
        if(_showOverlayLevels)return await _overlayLevels!.ActivateAsync(key);
        if(key=="npcs"){
            _showOverlayLevels=false;_showOverlayNpcs=true;
            _overlayNpcs.OpenManager();
            await _overlayNpcs.RefreshAsync();return "";
        }
        if(key=="back"){if(!_overlayNpcs.Back())_showOverlayNpcs=false;return "";}
        if(_showOverlayNpcs){if(_levelLoading)return "Wait for level loading to finish.";return await _overlayNpcs.ActivateAsync(key);}
        if(key=="cameras-disable")return await ApplyWorldActionAsync(false);
        if(key=="guards-fight")return await ApplyWorldActionAsync(true);
        if(!key.StartsWith("cheat:")||!int.TryParse(key.AsSpan(6),out int selected))return "Select an option.";
        if(_levelLoading||_healthChanging||_cheatsChanging||_cheatRefreshBusy||_worldActionChanging)return "Please wait for the current update.";
        if(selected==0){EnemyEspToggle.IsChecked=EnemyEspToggle.IsChecked!=true;return EspStatus.Text;}
        if(selected==1){await SetInfiniteHealthAsync(InfiniteHealthToggle.IsChecked!=true);return HealthStatus.Text;}
        if(selected<2||selected>=MenuOptions.Length+2)return "Select an option.";
        var settings=ReadCheatSettings();
        await ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(settings with {Options=settings.Options^MenuOptions[selected-2]});
        return CheatStatus.Text;
    }

    private async Task<string> ApplyWorldActionAsync(bool fighting)
    {
        if(IsBusy)return "Please wait for the current update.";
        _worldActionChanging=true;
        var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
        try {
            if(games.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell and load a mission.");
            return MenuStatus.Text=fighting?await DirectLevelLoader.FightGuardsAsync(games[0]):await DirectLevelLoader.DisableCamerasAsync(games[0]);
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException){return MenuStatus.Text=ex.Message;}
        finally{foreach(var game in games)game.Dispose();_worldActionChanging=false;}
    }

    private GameMenuFrame ReadUtilityMenu()
    {
        bool ready=!_levelLoading&&!_overlayNpcs.Busy&&!_overlayNpcs.Refreshing&&!_healthChanging&&!_cheatsChanging&&!_cheatRefreshBusy&&!_worldActionChanging;
        var rows=new List<GameMenuEntry>();string title="TRAINER TOOLS";
        GameMenuEntry Row(string label,string key,bool enabled=true,bool navigation=false,string? value=null)=>new(label,false,enabled,key,value,false,navigation);
        if(_utilityPage=="speed"){
            title="MOVEMENT SPEED";var settings=ReadCheatSettings();
            rows.Add(new("Enable movement speed",settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.MovementSpeed),ready,"speed-toggle"));
            foreach(int percent in new[]{50,100,150,200,300})rows.Add(Row($"{percent/100.0:0.##}x"+(percent==100?" (normal)":""),"speed:"+percent,ready,value:settings.SpeedPercent==percent?"Selected":""));
        } else if(_utilityPage=="about"){
            title="ABOUT / SUPPORTED GAME";
            rows.Add(Row(VersionInfo.TrainerLabel,"info:version",false,value:""));
            rows.Add(Row("Steam build "+VersionInfo.SupportedSteamBuild,"info:build",false,value:""));
            rows.Add(Row("Steam App ID 13560","info:app",false,value:""));
            rows.Add(Row("F6: menu / arrows: select / Enter: apply","info:keys",false,value:""));
        } else if(_utilityPage=="exit"){
            title="EXIT TRAINER?";
            rows.Add(Row("Cancel","exit-cancel",navigation:true));
            rows.Add(Row("Exit trainer and restore active cheats","exit-confirm",ready));
        } else {
            rows.Add(Row("Refresh game detection","detect-refresh",ready));
            rows.Add(Row("Test game connection","connection-test",ready));
            rows.Add(Row("Version / supported Steam build","about",navigation:true));
            rows.Add(Row("Exit trainer","exit-menu",navigation:true));
        }
        rows.Add(Row("Back","back",navigation:true));
        return new("utility:"+_utilityPage,title,rows.ToArray());
    }
    private async Task<string> ActivateUtilityAsync(string key)
    {
        if(key=="about"){_utilityPage="about";return "";}
        if(key=="exit-menu"){_utilityPage="exit";return "The game will remain running.";}
        if(key=="exit-cancel"){_utilityPage="tools";return "Exit cancelled.";}
        if(_levelLoading||_overlayNpcs.Busy||_overlayNpcs.Refreshing||_healthChanging||_cheatsChanging||_cheatRefreshBusy||_worldActionChanging)return "Please wait for the current update.";
        if(key=="exit-confirm"&&_utilityPage=="exit"){
            // Uses the existing close handler, which restores active cheat sessions first.
            _ = Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(RequestExit));return "Closing trainer…";
        }
        if(key=="detect-refresh"){RefreshGameStatus();return GameStatus.Text+" / "+GameDetail.Text;}
        if(key=="connection-test"){
            var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
            try {
                if(games.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell first.");
                return LevelStatus.Text=await DirectLevelLoader.RequestAsync(games[0],"--probe");
            } finally {foreach(var game in games)game.Dispose();}
        }
        var settings=ReadCheatSettings();
        if(key=="speed-toggle"){
            await ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(settings with {Options=settings.Options^CheatOptions.MovementSpeed});return CheatStatus.Text;
        }
        if(key.StartsWith("speed:")&&int.TryParse(key.AsSpan(6),out int percent)&&new[]{50,100,150,200,300}.Contains(percent)){
            var changed=settings with {SpeedPercent=percent};
            if(settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.MovementSpeed)){
                await ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(changed);return CheatStatus.Text;
            }
            _cheatsChanging=true;
            try{ShowCheatSettings(changed);}finally{_cheatsChanging=false;}
            return $"Speed set to {percent/100.0:0.##}x. Enable movement speed to apply it.";
        }
        return "Select an option.";
    }

    private EnemyOverlay? _enemyOverlay;

    private void EnemyEsp_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        var games = Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
        EnemyMemoryReader? reader = null;
        try
        {
            if (games.Length != 1) throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell and load a mission.");
            reader = new EnemyMemoryReader(games[0]);
            _enemyOverlay = new EnemyOverlay(reader, text => EspStatus.Text = text);
            _enemyOverlay.Closed += (_, _) =>
            {
                _enemyOverlay = null;
                EnemyEspToggle.IsChecked = false;
            };
            _enemyOverlay.Show();
            EspStatus.Text = "Enemy ESP enabled. Focus the game to display markers.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            reader?.Dispose();
            EnemyEspToggle.IsChecked = false;
            EspStatus.Text = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            foreach (var game in games)
                if (reader is null || _enemyOverlay is null) game.Dispose();
        }
    }

    private void EnemyEsp_Unchecked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        var overlay = _enemyOverlay;
        _enemyOverlay = null;
        overlay?.Close();
        EspStatus.Text = "Enemy ESP is off.";
    }

    private InfiniteHealthSession? _healthSession;
    private bool _healthChanging;

    private async void InfiniteHealth_Changed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_healthChanging) return;
        await SetInfiniteHealthAsync(InfiniteHealthToggle.IsChecked == true);
    }

    private async Task<bool> SetInfiniteHealthAsync(bool enabled)
    {
        _healthChanging = true;
        InfiniteHealthToggle.IsEnabled = false;
        try
        {
            if (enabled)
            {
                var games = Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
                if (games.Length != 1)
                {
                    foreach (var game in games) game.Dispose();
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell and load a mission first.");
                }
                try { _healthSession = await InfiniteHealthSession.EnableAsync(games[0]); }
                catch { games[0].Dispose(); throw; }
                HealthStatus.Text = "Infinite health is ON.";
            }
            else if (_healthSession is not null)
            {
                await _healthSession.DisableAsync();
                _healthSession.Dispose();
                _healthSession = null;
                HealthStatus.Text = "Infinite health is off.";
            }
            InfiniteHealthToggle.IsChecked = enabled;
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or FormatException)
        {
            InfiniteHealthToggle.IsChecked = _healthSession is not null;
            HealthStatus.Text = ex.Message;
            return false;
        }
        finally { _healthChanging = false; InfiniteHealthToggle.IsEnabled = true; }
    }

    private void RefreshHealthStatus()
    {
        if (_healthChanging || _healthSession is null || _healthSession.IsCurrent()) return;
        _healthSession.Dispose();
        _healthSession = null;
        _healthChanging = true;
        InfiniteHealthToggle.IsChecked = false;
        _healthChanging = false;
        HealthStatus.Text = "Infinite health reset after the player changed. Enable it again in the new mission.";
    }

    private void Window_Closing(object? sender,System.ComponentModel.CancelEventArgs e)
    {
        if(_shutdownApproved)return;
        e.Cancel=true;RequestExit();
    }
    private CheatSession? _cheatSession;
    private bool _cheatsChanging, _cheatRefreshBusy, _cheatControlsReady;

    private CheatSettings ReadCheatSettings()
    {
        CheatOptions options=CheatOptions.None;
        if(UnlimitedAmmoToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.UnlimitedAmmo;
        if(NoReloadToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.NoReload;
        if(UnlimitedGadgetsToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.UnlimitedGadgets;
        if(ReducedDetectionToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.ReducedDetection;
        if(MovementSpeedToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.MovementSpeed;
        if(InvisibilityToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.Invisibility;
        if(NoclipToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.Noclip;
        if(BypassLocksToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.BypassLocks;
        if(QuietLightsToggle.IsChecked==true)options|=CheatOptions.QuietLights;
        var selected=SpeedPicker.SelectedItem as System.Windows.Controls.ComboBoxItem;
        int speed=selected?.Tag is string value ? int.Parse(value,System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : 100;
        return new(options,speed);
    }

    private void SetCheatControls(bool available)
    {
        UnlimitedAmmoToggle.IsEnabled=NoReloadToggle.IsEnabled=UnlimitedGadgetsToggle.IsEnabled=
            ReducedDetectionToggle.IsEnabled=MovementSpeedToggle.IsEnabled=InvisibilityToggle.IsEnabled=NoclipToggle.IsEnabled=BypassLocksToggle.IsEnabled=QuietLightsToggle.IsEnabled=available;
        SpeedPicker.IsEnabled=available && MovementSpeedToggle.IsChecked==true;
    }

    private void ShowCheatSettings(CheatSettings settings)
    {
        UnlimitedAmmoToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.UnlimitedAmmo);
        NoReloadToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.NoReload);
        UnlimitedGadgetsToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.UnlimitedGadgets);
        ReducedDetectionToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.ReducedDetection);
        MovementSpeedToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.MovementSpeed);
        InvisibilityToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.Invisibility);
        NoclipToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.Noclip);
        BypassLocksToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.BypassLocks);
        QuietLightsToggle.IsChecked=settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.QuietLights);
        foreach(System.Windows.Controls.ComboBoxItem item in SpeedPicker.Items)
            if((string)item.Tag==settings.SpeedPercent.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))SpeedPicker.SelectedItem=item;
    }

    private async void CheatOption_Changed(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(!_cheatControlsReady||_cheatsChanging)return;
        await ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(ReadCheatSettings());
    }

    private async void SpeedPicker_Changed(object sender,System.Windows.Controls.SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(!_cheatControlsReady||_cheatsChanging||MovementSpeedToggle.IsChecked!=true)return;
        await ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(ReadCheatSettings());
    }

    private async Task<bool> ApplyCheatSettingsAsync(CheatSettings settings)
    {
        if(_cheatsChanging)return false;
        _cheatsChanging=true;SetCheatControls(false);
        try
        {
            if(settings.Options==CheatOptions.None)
            {
                if(_cheatSession is not null){await _cheatSession.DisableAsync();_cheatSession.Dispose();_cheatSession=null;}
                CheatStatus.Text="Additional cheats are off.";
            }
            else
            {
                var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
                if(games.Length!=1){foreach(var g in games)g.Dispose();throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one game and load a mission first.");}
                CheatSession updated;
                try{updated=await CheatSession.ApplyAsync(games[0],settings);}
                catch{games[0].Dispose();throw;}
                _cheatSession?.Dispose();_cheatSession=updated;
                CheatStatus.Text="Active: "+settings.Options+
                    (settings.Options.HasFlag(CheatOptions.MovementSpeed)?$" • Speed {settings.SpeedPercent/100.0:0.##}x":"");
            }
            ShowCheatSettings(settings);
            return true;
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or FormatException or ArgumentException)
        {
            ShowCheatSettings(_cheatSession?.Settings??new(CheatOptions.None));
            CheatStatus.Text=ex.Message;
            return false;
        }
        finally{_cheatsChanging=false;SetCheatControls(true);}
    }

    private async Task RefreshCheatStatusAsync()
    {
        if(_cheatRefreshBusy||_cheatsChanging||_cheatSession is null)return;
        _cheatRefreshBusy=true;
        try
        {
            var session=_cheatSession;
            if(!session.IsCurrent())
            {
                session.Dispose();_cheatSession=null;
                _cheatsChanging=true;ShowCheatSettings(new(CheatOptions.None));_cheatsChanging=false;
                SetCheatControls(true);
                CheatStatus.Text="Cheats reset after a player change or expired session. Enable them again in the mission.";
            }
            else await session.PulseAsync();
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {CheatStatus.Text=ex.Message;}
        finally{_cheatRefreshBusy=false;}
    }


    private NpcManagerWindow? _npcManager;
    private void OpenNpcManager_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(_npcManager is null) {
            _npcManager=new NpcManagerWindow {Owner=this};
            _npcManager.Closed+=(_,_)=>_npcManager=null;
            _npcManager.Show();
        } else _npcManager.Activate();
    }

    private const string GameDirectory = @"C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Splinter Cell";
    private sealed record LevelChoice(string Map, string Label);

    private void PopulateLevels()
    {
        try
        {
            var directory = System.IO.Path.Combine(GameDirectory, "maps");
            var levels = System.IO.Directory.GetFiles(directory, "*.scl")
                .OrderBy(path => path, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                .Select(path => {
                    var map = System.IO.Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(path);
                    return new LevelChoice(map, FriendlyName(map));
                }).ToArray();
            _levels=levels;
            LevelPicker.ItemsSource = levels;
            if (levels.Length > 0) LevelPicker.SelectedIndex = 0;
            else LevelStatus.Text = "No installed maps found.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
        {
            _levels=Array.Empty<LevelChoice>();
            LevelStatus.Text = "Cannot read installed maps: " + ex.Message;
        }
    }

    private static string FriendlyName(string map)
    {
        string mission = map.Contains("Training") ? "Training"
            : map.StartsWith("1_1_") ? "Police Station"
            : map.StartsWith("1_2_") ? "Defense Ministry"
            : map.StartsWith("1_3_") ? "Oil Refinery"
            : map.StartsWith("2_1_") ? "CIA HQ"
            : map.StartsWith("2_2_") ? "Kalinatek"
            : map.StartsWith("4_1_") ? "Chinese Embassy"
            : map.StartsWith("4_2_") ? "Abattoir"
            : map.StartsWith("4_3_") ? "Chinese Embassy — Return"
            : map.StartsWith("5_1_") ? "Presidential Palace" : "Additional map";
        return $"{mission}  /  {map}";
    }

    private async Task<string> LoadOverlayLevelAsync(LevelMenuChoice level)
    {
        if(_levelLoading||_overlayNpcs.Busy||_overlayNpcs.Refreshing||_healthChanging||_cheatsChanging||_cheatRefreshBusy||_worldActionChanging)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Wait for the current trainer update to finish.");
        if(!_levels.Any(l=>l.Map==level.Map)||!System.IO.File.Exists(System.IO.Path.Combine(GameDirectory,"maps",level.Map+".scl")))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("The selected map is no longer installed.");
        var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
        try {
            if(games.Length!=1)throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell and return to its main menu.");
            _levelLoading=true;LoadLevelButton.IsEnabled=false;
            LevelStatus.Text="Requesting map travel…";
            // Shared loader checks the current map and refuses active-mission travel before sending OPEN.
            return LevelStatus.Text=await DirectLevelLoader.RequestAsync(games[0],level.Map);
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(ex is System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or InvalidOperationException or NotSupportedException) {
            LevelStatus.Text=ex.Message;throw;
        }
        finally {_levelLoading=false;LoadLevelButton.IsEnabled=true;foreach(var game in games)game.Dispose();}
    }

    private async void LoadLevel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (LevelPicker.SelectedItem is not LevelChoice level) return;
        try
        {
            var games = Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
            try
            {
                if (games.Length > 0)
                {
                    if (games.Length != 1) { LevelStatus.Text = "More than one game process found. Keep only one running."; return; }
                    if (MessageBox.Show(this, "Load " + level.Label + "? Unsaved mission progress will be lost.",
                        "Load level", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Question) != MessageBoxResult.Yes) return;
                    LoadLevelButton.IsEnabled = false;
                    LevelStatus.Text = "Requesting map travel…";
                    try { LevelStatus.Text = await LoadOverlayLevelAsync(new(level.Map,level.Label)); }
                    finally { LoadLevelButton.IsEnabled = true; }
                    return;
                }
            }
            finally { foreach (var game in games) game.Dispose(); }

            var system = System.IO.Path.Combine(GameDirectory, "system");
            var exe = System.IO.Path.Combine(system, "splintercell.exe");
            var mapPath = System.IO.Path.Combine(GameDirectory, "maps", level.Map + ".scl");
            if (!System.IO.File.Exists(exe) || !System.IO.File.Exists(mapPath))
            {
                LevelStatus.Text = "Game executable or selected map is missing.";
                return;
            }
            var start = new ProcessStartInfo(exe) { WorkingDirectory = system, UseShellExecute = false };
            start.ArgumentList.Add(level.Map + ".scl");
            using var launched = Process.Start(start);
            LevelStatus.Text = "Launch requested: " + level.Label + ". Waiting for the game.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or InvalidOperationException or NotSupportedException)
        {
            LevelStatus.Text = "Could not launch level: " + ex.Message;
        }
    }

    private async void TestConnection_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        var games = Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
        try
        {
            if (games.Length != 1) { LevelStatus.Text = "Start one copy of Splinter Cell and wait for the main menu."; return; }
            TestConnectionButton.IsEnabled = false;
            LevelStatus.Text = await DirectLevelLoader.RequestAsync(games[0], "--probe");
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or InvalidOperationException or NotSupportedException)
        { LevelStatus.Text = ex.Message; }
        finally { TestConnectionButton.IsEnabled = true; foreach (var game in games) game.Dispose(); }
    }
    private async void Timer_Tick(object? sender, EventArgs e) { RefreshGameStatus(); RefreshHealthStatus(); await RefreshCheatStatusAsync(); }
    private void Refresh_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => RefreshGameStatus();

    private void RefreshGameStatus()
    {
        try
        {
            var games = Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
            try
            {
                var running = games.Length > 0;
                GameStatus.Text = running ? "Game detected" : "Waiting for Splinter Cell";
                GameDetail.Text = running
                    ? "SplinterCell.exe is running • Level loading requires the main menu"
                    : "Launch the game through Steam. Detection updates automatically.";
                StatusDot.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(running ? Color.FromRgb(181, 239, 101) : Color.FromRgb(138, 149, 139));
            }
            finally
            {
                foreach (var game in games) game.Dispose();
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or InvalidOperationException or NotSupportedException)
        {
            GameStatus.Text = "Unable to check game status";
            GameDetail.Text = "Press Refresh to try again.";
            StatusDot.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(224, 175, 99));
        }
    }
}
```

`Native/Cameras.h`:

```h
#pragma once
// One-shot, game-thread deactivation using the same state as scripted camera shutdown.
namespace TrainerCameras {
using namespace TrainerCheats;
static bool Handle(Request* r,void* view,HMODULE core,DWORD* objects) {
    if(wcscmp(r->map,L"--cameras-disable"))return false;
    objectArray=objects;
    name=reinterpret_cast<NameFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
    auto go=reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD)>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GotoState@UObject@@UAE?AW4EGotoState@@VFName@@@Z"));
    if(!name||!go){r->result=47;return true;}
    auto controller=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(static_cast<Byte*>(view),0x34));
    auto info=Alive(controller)?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(controller,0x6c)):nullptr;
    auto package=Alive(info)?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(info,0x18)):nullptr;
    if(!IsA(controller,L"EPlayerController")||!Alive(package)||!_wcsicmp(name(package),L"menu")){r->result=16;return true;}
    Field f[]={ {L"ESensor",L"SoundDisable",L"ObjectProperty"}, {L"EGameplayObject",L"ChangeListWhenDamaged",L"BoolProperty"} };
    DWORD state=0;
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i){
        auto obj=table[i];if(!Alive(obj))continue;
        auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x18));
        auto type=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x24));
        if(!Alive(owner)||!Alive(type))continue;
        if(!wcscmp(name(owner),L"ESensor")&&!wcscmp(name(type),L"State")&&!wcscmp(name(obj),L"s_Deactivated"))state=U(obj,0x20);
        for(auto& field:f)if(!wcscmp(name(owner),field.owner)&&!wcscmp(name(obj),field.name)&&!wcscmp(name(type),field.type)){
            field.offset=U(obj,0x44);field.mask=U(obj,0x54);
            if(*reinterpret_cast<WORD*>(obj+0x38)!=1||*reinterpret_cast<WORD*>(obj+0x3a)!=4)field.offset=0;
        }
    }
    if(!state||f[0].offset<0x28||f[0].offset>0x8000||f[1].offset<0x28||f[1].offset>0x8000||!f[1].mask){r->result=47;return true;}
    DWORD changed=0,already=0;
    const DWORD count=objects[1];
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i){
        auto camera=table[i];
        if(!IsA(camera,L"EBaseCam")||U(camera,0x6c)!=reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(info)||(U(camera,0x2c)&0x10))continue;
        auto current=TrainerSpawn::State(camera,name);
        if(!_wcsicmp(current,L"s_Deactivated")){++already;continue;}
        if(!_wcsicmp(current,L"s_Destructed")||!_wcsicmp(current,L"s_Jammed"))continue;
        auto sound=reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(camera+f[0].offset);
        auto change=reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(camera+f[1].offset);
        DWORD originalSound=*sound,originalChange=*change&f[1].mask;
        // Do not emit the disable sound or an AI disabled-turret change notification.
        __try { *sound=0;*change&=~f[1].mask;go(camera,state); }
        __finally { *sound=originalSound;*change=(*change&~f[1].mask)|originalChange; }
        if(!_wcsicmp(TrainerSpawn::State(camera,name),L"s_Deactivated"))++changed;
    }
    swprintf_s(r->map,L"Disabled %lu security cameras (%lu already disabled).",changed,already);
    r->result=46;return true;
}
}
```

`Native/Cheats.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

// All calls and timer callbacks execute on the game's window thread.
namespace TrainerCheats {
using Byte = unsigned char;
using NameFn = const wchar_t* (__thiscall*)(void*);
struct Field { const wchar_t* owner; const wchar_t* name; const wchar_t* type; DWORD offset=0, mask=0; WORD count=0; };
enum { Ammo=1, NoReload=2, Gadgets=4, Detection=8, Speed=16, Invisible=32, Noclip=64, BypassLocks=128, QuietLights=256 };
static Field fields[] = {
    {L"EPlayerController",L"EPawn",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"FullInventory",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EInventory",L"InventoryList",L"ArrayProperty"},
    {L"EInventoryItem",L"Quantity",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"EInventoryItem",L"MaxQuantity",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"EWeapon",L"Ammo",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"EWeapon",L"MaxAmmo",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"EWeapon",L"ClipAmmo",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"EWeapon",L"ClipMaxAmmo",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"bFullAmmo",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"bHostile",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedRun",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedRunCr",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedWalk",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedWalkCr",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedWalkFP",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedWalkFPCr",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedWalkSniping",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedBTWCr",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPlayerController",L"m_speedBTW",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"m_VisibilityMaxDistance",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"m_MaxPeripheralVisionDistance",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"VisTable_Alert",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"VisTable_Investigate",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"VisTable_Surprised",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"EPawn",L"VisTable_Peripheral",L"FloatProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"bHidden",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"Pawn",L"Visibility",L"ByteProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"bCollideWorld",L"BoolProperty"}
};
struct Saved { Byte* object; DWORD index,offset,mask,original,last,group; };
struct Array { Byte* data; int count,capacity; };
static DWORD* objectArray=nullptr;
static NameFn name=nullptr;
static Byte *viewport=nullptr,*player=nullptr,*pawn=nullptr;
static DWORD playerIndex=0;
static volatile LONG activeMask=0;
static int speedPercent=100;
static HWND window=nullptr;
static UINT_PTR timer=0;
static ULONGLONG heartbeat=0,nextGuards=0;
static std::vector<Saved> saved;
static std::vector<Byte*> guards;
static DWORD U(Byte* p,DWORD o=0){return *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(p+o);}
static bool Alive(Byte* p,DWORD index) {
    return p && objectArray && index<objectArray[1] && index<1000000 &&
        reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0])[index]==p;
}
static bool Alive(Byte* p){return p && Alive(p,U(p,4));}
static bool IsA(Byte* p,const wchar_t* wanted) {
    if(!Alive(p))return false;
    auto cls=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(p,0x24));
    for(int i=0;cls && i<24;++i) {
        if(!wcscmp(name(cls),wanted))return true;
        cls=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(cls,0x2c));
    }
    return false;
}
static bool Current() {
    return window && IsWindow(window) && Alive(player,playerIndex) && Alive(viewport) &&
        reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(viewport,0x34))==player && Alive(pawn) &&
        reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,fields[0].offset))==pawn;
}
static void Resolve() {
    for(auto& f:fields){f.offset=0;f.mask=0;f.count=0;}
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0]); DWORD count=objectArray[1];
    if(count>1000000)throw 1;
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i) {
        auto obj=table[i];if(!obj)continue;
        const auto n=name(obj);
        for(auto& f:fields) {
            if(wcscmp(n,f.name))continue;
            auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x18)),type=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x24));
            if(!owner||!type||wcscmp(name(owner),f.owner)||wcscmp(name(type),f.type))continue;
            f.offset=U(obj,0x44);f.mask=U(obj,0x54);f.count=*reinterpret_cast<WORD*>(obj+0x38);
        }
    }
    for(const auto& f:fields) {
        if(f.offset<0x28||f.offset>0x8000||!f.count||f.count>64)throw 1;
        if(!wcscmp(f.type,L"BoolProperty") && (!f.mask || (f.mask&(f.mask-1))))throw 1;
    }
}
static void QuietRestore();
static void QuietClear();
static void QuietResolve();
static void QuietTick();
static void LockRestore();
static void LockClear();
static void LockResolve();
static void LockTick();
static bool ghostOwned=false;
static bool Command(const wchar_t* command) {
    auto engine=GetModuleHandleW(L"Core.dll");
    auto log=reinterpret_cast<void**>(GetProcAddress(engine,"?GLog@@3PAVFOutputDevice@@A"));
    auto target=viewport+0x30;
    auto exec=reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*,const wchar_t*,void*)>((*reinterpret_cast<void***>(target))[0]);
    return log&&*log&&exec(target,command,*log)!=0;
}
static void Restore(DWORD groups) {
    if(groups&BypassLocks)LockRestore();
    if(groups&QuietLights)QuietRestore();
    if((groups&Noclip)&&ghostOwned) {
        if(Current()&&!Command(L"Walk"))throw 1;
        ghostOwned=false;
    }
    for(auto it=saved.begin();it!=saved.end();) {
        if(!(it->group&groups)){++it;continue;}
        if(Alive(it->object,it->index)) {
            auto address=reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(it->object+it->offset);
            if(it->mask) {
                InterlockedAnd(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(address),~it->mask);
                InterlockedOr(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(address),it->original & it->mask);
            } else if(*address==it->last) *address=it->original;
        }
        it=saved.erase(it);
    }
}
static void Stop(bool restore) {
    if(restore)Restore(511);
    else {LockClear();QuietClear();}
    saved.clear();guards.clear();activeMask=0;
    if(timer && window)KillTimer(window,timer);
    timer=0;
}
static void Scale(Byte* object,DWORD offset,float factor,DWORD group) {
    auto index=U(object,4);
    Saved* slot=nullptr;
    for(auto& item:saved)if(item.object==object&&item.index==index&&item.offset==offset&&item.group==group){slot=&item;break;}
    if(!slot){saved.push_back({object,index,offset,0,U(object,offset),0,group});slot=&saved.back();}
    float original;memcpy(&original,&slot->original,4);
    if(!std::isfinite(original)||original<0||original>1000000)throw 1;
    float value=original*factor;
    memcpy(&slot->last,&value,4);
    *reinterpret_cast<float*>(object+offset)=value;
}
static void FullAmmoFlag() {
    const auto& f=fields[9]; Saved* slot=nullptr;
    for(auto& item:saved)if(item.object==player&&item.group==NoReload){slot=&item;break;}
    if(!slot){saved.push_back({player,playerIndex,f.offset,f.mask,U(player,f.offset),0,NoReload});}
    InterlockedOr(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(player+f.offset),f.mask);
}
static void InvisiblePlayer() {
    const auto& hidden=fields[26];const auto& visibility=fields[27];
    for(int i=0;i<2;++i) {
        DWORD offset=i?visibility.offset:hidden.offset,mask=i?255:hidden.mask;
        Saved* slot=nullptr;
        for(auto& item:saved)if(item.object==pawn&&item.index==U(pawn,4)&&item.offset==offset&&item.group==Invisible){slot=&item;break;}
        if(!slot)saved.push_back({pawn,U(pawn,4),offset,mask,U(pawn,offset),0,Invisible});
        if(i)*reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(pawn+offset)=0;
        else InterlockedOr(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(pawn+offset),mask);
    }
}
static bool Consumable(Byte* item) {
    static const wchar_t* types[]={L"ESecondaryAmmo",L"EMedKit",L"EFragGrenade",L"EChemFlare",L"EFlare",L"EWallMine",L"EDisposablePick",L"ESmokeGrenade",L"EStickyCamera",L"EDiversionCamera",L"EStickyShocker",L"ERingAirfoilRound"};
    for(auto t:types)if(IsA(item,t))return true;
    return false;
}
static void Inventory() {
    auto inv=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,fields[1].offset));
    if(!IsA(inv,L"EInventory"))return;
    auto cats=*reinterpret_cast<Array*>(inv+fields[2].offset);
    if(cats.count<0||cats.count>16||cats.count>cats.capacity||!cats.data)return;
    for(int c=0;c<cats.count;++c) {
        auto items=reinterpret_cast<Array*>(cats.data)[c];
        if(items.count<0||items.count>128||items.count>items.capacity||!items.data)continue;
        for(int j=0;j<items.count;++j) {
            auto item=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(items.data)[j];
            if(!IsA(item,L"EInventoryItem"))continue;
            if(IsA(item,L"EWeapon")) {
                int max=*reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[6].offset);
                int clipMax=*reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[8].offset);
                if(max<1||max>100000||clipMax<1||clipMax>1000)continue;
                if(activeMask&Ammo)*reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[5].offset)=max;
                if(activeMask&NoReload) {
                    *reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[7].offset)=clipMax;
                    auto total=reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[5].offset);
                    if(*total<clipMax)*total=clipMax;
                }
            } else if((activeMask&Gadgets)&&Consumable(item)) {
                int max=*reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[4].offset);
                if(max>0&&max<10000)*reinterpret_cast<int*>(item+fields[3].offset)=(max > 2 ? max : 2);
            }
        }
    }
}
static void FindGuards() {
    guards.clear();
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0]);auto count=objectArray[1];
    if(count>1000000)throw 1;
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i) {
        auto p=table[i];
        if(!IsA(p,L"EAIPawn")||IsA(p,L"EAINonHostile"))continue;
        if(U(p,0x6c)!=U(player,0x6c)||!(U(p,fields[10].offset)&fields[10].mask))continue;
        guards.push_back(p);
    }
    nextGuards=GetTickCount64()+1500;
}
#include "Locks.h"
#include "QuietLights.h"

static void Tick() {
    if(!Current()||GetTickCount64()-heartbeat>15000){Stop(true);return;}
    if(activeMask&BypassLocks)LockTick();
    if(activeMask&QuietLights)QuietTick();
    if(activeMask&Invisible)InvisiblePlayer();
    if(activeMask&NoReload)FullAmmoFlag();
    if(activeMask&(Ammo|NoReload|Gadgets))Inventory();
    if(activeMask&Speed)for(int i=11;i<=19;++i)Scale(player,fields[i].offset,speedPercent/100.0f,Speed);
    if(activeMask&Detection) {
        if(GetTickCount64()>=nextGuards)FindGuards();
        for(auto p:guards) {
            if(!Alive(p))continue;
            for(int f=20;f<=25;++f)for(int i=0;i<fields[f].count;++i)
                Scale(p,fields[f].offset+i*4,0.25f,Detection);
        }
    }
}
static bool SafeTick() {
    __try{Tick();return true;}
    __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER){Stop(false);return false;}
}
static void CALLBACK TimerProc(HWND,UINT,UINT_PTR,DWORD) {
    try{SafeTick();}catch(...){Stop(false);}
}
static bool HandleRequest(Request* r,void* view,HMODULE core,DWORD* objects) {
    bool ping=!wcscmp(r->map,L"--cheats-ping");
    if(!ping && wcsncmp(r->map,L"--cheats:",9))return false;
    objectArray=objects;
    name=reinterpret_cast<NameFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
    if(!name){r->result=3;return true;}
    if(ping) {
        if(!Current()){Stop(true);r->result=17;return true;}
        heartbeat=GetTickCount64();
    } else {
        unsigned mask=0;int percent=0;wchar_t extra=0;
        if(swscanf_s(r->map,L"--cheats:%u:%d%c",&mask,&percent,&extra,1)!=2||mask>511||percent<50||percent>300){r->result=6;return true;}
        auto vp=static_cast<Byte*>(view);
        auto pc=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(vp,0x34));
        if(activeMask && (pc!=player||!Current()))Stop(true);
        if(mask==0)Stop(true);
        else {
            if(!activeMask) {
                Resolve();
                auto level=pc?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pc,0x6c)):nullptr;
                auto package=level?reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(level,0x18)):nullptr;
                if(!IsA(pc,L"EPlayerController")||!package||!_wcsicmp(name(package),L"menu")){r->result=16;return true;}
                viewport=vp;player=pc;playerIndex=U(pc,4);pawn=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pc,fields[0].offset));window=r->window;
                if(!IsA(pawn,L"EPawn")){r->result=16;return true;}
            }
            if((mask&Noclip)&&!(activeMask&Noclip)) {
                auto frame=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,0x0c));
                auto state=frame?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(frame,0x1c)):nullptr;
                // Avoid interrupting ladders, interactions, death or scripted movement.
                if(!Alive(state)||wcscmp(name(state),L"s_PlayerWalking")){r->result=43;return true;}
                ghostOwned=true;
                if(!Command(L"Ghost")||(U(pawn,fields[28].offset)&fields[28].mask)) {
                    Restore(Noclip);r->result=3;return true;
                }
            }
            if((mask&BypassLocks)&&!(activeMask&BypassLocks)){try{LockResolve();}catch(...){r->result=44;return true;}}
            if((mask&QuietLights)&&!(activeMask&QuietLights)){try{QuietResolve();}catch(...){r->result=45;return true;}}
            Restore(static_cast<DWORD>(activeMask)&~mask);
            activeMask=mask;speedPercent=percent;heartbeat=GetTickCount64();nextGuards=0;
            Tick();
            if(!timer)timer=SetTimer(window,0x53435452,16,&TimerProc);
            if(!timer){Stop(true);r->result=7;return true;}
        }
    }
    swprintf_s(r->map,L"%08lX:%08lX:%08lX:%08lX",
        reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(viewport),reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(player),playerIndex,reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(&activeMask));
    r->result=18;return true;
}
static bool SafeRequest(Request* r,void* view,HMODULE core,DWORD* objects) {
    try{return HandleRequest(r,view,core,objects);}
    catch(...){Stop(true);r->result=3;return true;}
}
} // namespace TrainerCheats

```

`Native/FightPolicy.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <cmath>
namespace FightPolicy {
inline bool MaintainSession(unsigned long long now,unsigned long long deadline,bool samePlayer,bool sameLevel) {
    return now<deadline&&samePlayer&&sameLevel;
}
struct Candidate { unsigned id; float x,y,z; };
inline bool Eligible(bool guard,bool hostile,bool sameLevel,bool armed,bool conscious,bool controlled,bool deleted,int health) {
    return guard&&hostile&&sameLevel&&armed&&conscious&&controlled&&!deleted&&health>0;
}
inline int Nearest(const Candidate* actors,int count,int self) {
    if(!actors||self<0||self>=count)return -1;
    int best=-1;float distance=2000.0f*2000.0f;
    for(int i=0;i<count;++i){
        if(i==self||actors[i].id==actors[self].id)continue;
        float x=actors[i].x-actors[self].x,y=actors[i].y-actors[self].y,z=actors[i].z-actors[self].z;
        float d=x*x+y*y+z*z;
        if(std::isfinite(d)&&d<=distance&&(best<0||d<distance)){best=i;distance=d;}
    }
    return best;
}
}

```

`Native/Fighting.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include "FightPolicy.h"
// Bounded game-thread combat session; all actor identities are revalidated on each tick.
namespace TrainerFighting {
using namespace TrainerCheats;
using TrainerSpawn::Property;
struct Record {Byte* pawn;Byte* controller;DWORD index,controllerIndex;void* add;};
static Record participants[256]={};
static int participantCount=0,attackType=0,guardType=0,moveFlags=0;
static DWORD healthOffset=0,controlOffset=0,pawnOffset=0,goalListOffset=0,headOffset=0,goalTypeOffset=0,goalTargetOffset=0,
    goalFocusOffset=0,goalUpdateOffset=0,goalUpdateMask=0,fireTargetOffset=0,enemyOffset=0,seenOffset=0,seenMask=0,alertState=0,shootOffset=0,shootMask=0,initializedOffset=0,initializedMask=0,goalLocationOffset=0;
static Byte *sessionLevel=nullptr,*sessionViewport=nullptr,*sessionPlayer=nullptr;static DWORD levelIndex=0,sessionPlayerIndex=0,sessionViewportIndex=0;
static HWND sessionWindow=nullptr;static UINT_PTR sessionTimer=0;static ULONGLONG deadline=0;
static TrainerSpawn::EventFn dispatch=nullptr;
static int (__thiscall* gotoState)(void*,DWORD)=nullptr;
static bool Live(const Record& r) {
    if(!Alive(r.pawn,r.index)||!Alive(r.controller,r.controllerIndex)||(U(r.pawn,0x2c)&0x10)||(U(r.controller,0x2c)&0x10)||
        U(r.pawn,0x6c)!=reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(sessionLevel)||static_cast<int>(U(r.pawn,healthOffset))<=0||
        U(r.pawn,controlOffset)!=reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(r.controller)||U(r.controller,pawnOffset)!=reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(r.pawn))return false;
    auto state=TrainerSpawn::State(r.controller,name),ps=TrainerSpawn::State(r.pawn,name);
    return (!wcscmp(state,L"s_Default")||!wcscmp(state,L"s_Investigate")||!wcscmp(state,L"s_Alert"))&&
        !TrainerSpawn::Unconscious(ps,state)&&wcscmp(ps,L"s_Grabbed");
}
static void Stop() {
    if(sessionTimer&&sessionWindow)KillTimer(sessionWindow,sessionTimer);
    sessionTimer=0;sessionWindow=nullptr;participantCount=0;sessionLevel=nullptr;
}
static void Maintain() {
    bool samePlayer=Alive(sessionPlayer,sessionPlayerIndex)&&Alive(sessionViewport,sessionViewportIndex)&&
        U(sessionViewport,0x34)==reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(sessionPlayer);
    bool sameLevel=samePlayer&&Alive(sessionLevel,levelIndex)&&U(sessionPlayer,0x6c)==reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(sessionLevel);
    if(!FightPolicy::MaintainSession(GetTickCount64(),deadline,samePlayer,sameLevel)){Stop();return;}
    FightPolicy::Candidate candidates[256]={};Record live[256]={};int count=0;
    for(int i=0;i<participantCount;++i)if(Live(participants[i])){
        const auto& r=participants[i];auto v=*reinterpret_cast<TrainerSpawn::Vec*>(r.pawn+0xd4);
        if(std::isfinite(v.x)&&std::isfinite(v.y)&&std::isfinite(v.z)){live[count]=r;candidates[count]={r.index,v.x,v.y,v.z};++count;}
    }
    for(int i=0;i<count;++i){
        auto& a=live[i];int j=FightPolicy::Nearest(candidates,count,i);
        auto list=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(a.controller,goalListOffset));
        auto goal=IsA(list,L"EGoalList")?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(list,headOffset)):nullptr;
        auto target=j<0?nullptr:live[j].pawn;
        // Ordinary perception and group scripts can set these back to Sam between ticks.
        *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(a.controller+seenOffset)&=~seenMask;
        *reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(a.controller+enemyOffset)=target;
        *reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(a.controller+fireTargetOffset)=target;
        if(j<0){
            // No opponent remains: hold position instead of giving native attack code a null target.
            *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(a.controller+shootOffset)&=~shootMask;
            if(IsA(goal,L"EGoal")){
                goal[goalTypeOffset]=static_cast<Byte>(guardType);
                memcpy(goal+goalLocationOffset,a.pawn+0xd4,12);memcpy(goal+goalFocusOffset,a.pawn+0xd4,12);
                *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(goal+initializedOffset)&=~initializedMask;
                *reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(goal+goalTargetOffset)=nullptr;
                *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(goal+goalUpdateOffset)&=~goalUpdateMask;
            }
            continue;
        }
        if(IsA(goal,L"EGoal")&&goal[goalTypeOffset]==attackType){
            *reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(goal+goalTargetOffset)=target;
            memcpy(goal+goalFocusOffset,target+0xd4,12);
            *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(goal+goalUpdateOffset)&=~goalUpdateMask;
        }else{
            alignas(4) Byte params[0x54]={};params[0]=static_cast<Byte>(attackType);params[1]=255;
            memcpy(params+4,a.pawn+0xd4,12);memcpy(params+0x10,target+0xd4,12);memcpy(params+0x1c,&target,4);
            DWORD force=1;float seconds=static_cast<float>(deadline>GetTickCount64()?deadline-GetTickCount64():0)/1000.0f;
            memcpy(params+0x2c,&force,4);memcpy(params+0x30,&seconds,4);
            params[0x34]=static_cast<Byte>(moveFlags);params[0x44]=static_cast<Byte>(moveFlags);
            dispatch(a.controller,a.add,params,nullptr);
        }
        if(wcscmp(TrainerSpawn::State(a.controller,name),L"s_Alert"))gotoState(a.controller,alertState);
    }
}
static void CALLBACK TimerProc(HWND,UINT,UINT_PTR,DWORD) {
    __try { Maintain(); } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { Stop(); }
}
static bool Handle(Request* r,void* view,HMODULE core,DWORD* objects) {
    if(wcscmp(r->map,L"--guards-fight"))return false;
    objectArray=objects;name=reinterpret_cast<NameFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
    auto find=reinterpret_cast<TrainerSpawn::FindFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?FindFunction@UObject@@QAEPAVUFunction@@VFName@@H@Z"));
    auto process=reinterpret_cast<TrainerSpawn::EventFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?ProcessEvent@UObject@@UAEXPAVUFunction@@PAX1@Z"));
    auto go=reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD)>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GotoState@UObject@@UAE?AW4EGotoState@@VFName@@@Z"));
    if(!name||!find||!process||!go){r->result=49;return true;}
    auto pc=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(static_cast<Byte*>(view),0x34));
    auto info=Alive(pc)?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pc,0x6c)):nullptr;
    auto package=Alive(info)?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(info,0x18)):nullptr;
    if(!IsA(pc,L"EPlayerController")||!Alive(package)||!_wcsicmp(name(package),L"menu")){r->result=16;return true;}
    DWORD hp=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health"),control=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller"),
        cp=Property(objects,name,L"Controller",L"Pawn"),weapon=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"CurrentWeapon"),
        group=Property(objects,name,L"EAIController",L"Group");
    Field hostile={L"EPawn",L"bHostile",L"BoolProperty"};
    DWORD alert=0;auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i){
        auto obj=table[i];if(!Alive(obj))continue;
        auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x18));if(!Alive(owner))continue;
        if(!wcscmp(name(owner),L"EAIController")&&IsA(obj,L"State")&&!wcscmp(name(obj),L"s_Alert"))alert=U(obj,0x20);
        if(!wcscmp(name(owner),hostile.owner)&&!wcscmp(name(obj),hostile.name)&&IsA(obj,L"BoolProperty")){hostile.offset=U(obj,0x44);hostile.mask=U(obj,0x54);}
    }
    int attack=TrainerSpawn::EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"GoalType",L"GOAL_Attack");
    int guard=TrainerSpawn::EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"GoalType",L"GOAL_Guard");
    int flags=TrainerSpawn::EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"MoveFlags",L"MOVE_WalkAlert");
    if(!hp||!control||!cp||!weapon||!group||!hostile.offset||!hostile.mask||!alert||attack<0||guard<0||flags<0){r->result=49;return true;}
    Record records[256]={};FightPolicy::Candidate positions[256]={};int count=0;
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i){
        auto p=table[i];if(!IsA(p,L"EAIPawn")||IsA(p,L"EAINonHostile"))continue;
        auto ctrl=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(p,control));
        auto w=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(p,weapon));
        auto ps=TrainerSpawn::State(p,name),cs=IsA(ctrl,L"EAIController")?TrainerSpawn::State(ctrl,name):L"None";
        bool conscious=(!wcscmp(cs,L"s_Default")||!wcscmp(cs,L"s_Investigate")||!wcscmp(cs,L"s_Alert"))&&
            !TrainerSpawn::Unconscious(ps,cs)&&wcscmp(ps,L"s_Grabbed");
        bool controlled=IsA(ctrl,L"EAIController")&&U(ctrl,cp)==reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(p)&&
            !(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)&&IsA(reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(ctrl,group)),L"EGroupAI");
        if(!FightPolicy::Eligible(true,(U(p,hostile.offset)&hostile.mask)!=0,U(p,0x6c)==reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(info),
            IsA(w,L"EWeapon"),conscious,controlled,(U(p,0x2c)&0x10)!=0,static_cast<int>(U(p,hp))))continue;
        if(count==256){r->result=49;return true;}
        auto location=*reinterpret_cast<TrainerSpawn::Vec*>(p+0xd4);
        if(!std::isfinite(location.x)||!std::isfinite(location.y)||!std::isfinite(location.z))continue;
        auto add=TrainerSpawn::Function(ctrl,L"AddGoal",objects,name,find);
        // Verify the complete input layout before changing any AI.
        if(!add||!TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Type",0,1,objects,name)||!TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Priority",1,1,objects,name)||
            !TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Location",4,12,objects,name)||!TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Focus",0x10,12,objects,name)||
            !TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Target",0x1c,4,objects,name)||!TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Flag",0x2c,4,objects,name)||
            !TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_Value",0x30,4,objects,name)||!TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_MoveFlags",0x34,1,objects,name)||
            !TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_WaitFlags",0x44,1,objects,name)||!TrainerSpawn::Param(add,L"_UpdatePlayerPos",0x48,4,objects,name)){
            r->result=49;return true;
        }
        records[count]={p,ctrl,U(p,4),U(ctrl,4),add};positions[count]={U(p,4),location.x,location.y,location.z};++count;
    }
    // Resolve every field required by the maintenance callback before issuing any orders.
    auto prop=[&](const wchar_t* owner,const wchar_t* field){return Property(objects,name,owner,field);};
    goalListOffset=prop(L"EAIController",L"m_pGoalList");headOffset=prop(L"EGoalList",L"pHead");
    goalTypeOffset=prop(L"EGoal",L"m_GoalType");goalTargetOffset=prop(L"EGoal",L"GoalTarget");
    goalFocusOffset=prop(L"EGoal",L"GoalFocus");fireTargetOffset=prop(L"EAIController",L"TargetActorToFire");
    enemyOffset=prop(L"Controller",L"Enemy");goalLocationOffset=prop(L"EGoal",L"GoalLocation");
    Field bools[]={ {L"EAIController",L"bPlayerSeen",L"BoolProperty"}, {L"EGoal",L"GoalUpdatePlayerPos",L"BoolProperty"}, {L"EAIController",L"bShootTarget",L"BoolProperty"}, {L"EGoal",L"bInitialized",L"BoolProperty"} };
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i){
        auto obj=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0])[i];if(!IsA(obj,L"BoolProperty"))continue;
        auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x18));if(!Alive(owner))continue;
        for(auto& f:bools)if(!wcscmp(name(owner),f.owner)&&!wcscmp(name(obj),f.name)){f.offset=U(obj,0x44);f.mask=U(obj,0x54);}
    }
    if(!goalListOffset||!headOffset||!goalTypeOffset||!goalTargetOffset||!goalFocusOffset||!fireTargetOffset||!enemyOffset||
        !goalLocationOffset||!bools[0].offset||!bools[0].mask||!bools[1].offset||!bools[1].mask||!bools[2].offset||!bools[2].mask||!bools[3].offset||!bools[3].mask){r->result=49;return true;}
    Stop();
    healthOffset=hp;controlOffset=control;pawnOffset=cp;seenOffset=bools[0].offset;seenMask=bools[0].mask;
    shootOffset=bools[2].offset;shootMask=bools[2].mask;initializedOffset=bools[3].offset;initializedMask=bools[3].mask;guardType=guard;
    goalUpdateOffset=bools[1].offset;goalUpdateMask=bools[1].mask;attackType=attack;moveFlags=flags;alertState=alert;
    sessionViewport=static_cast<Byte*>(view);sessionViewportIndex=U(sessionViewport,4);sessionPlayer=pc;sessionPlayerIndex=U(pc,4);
    sessionLevel=info;levelIndex=U(info,4);sessionWindow=r->window;dispatch=process;gotoState=go;
    deadline=GetTickCount64()+30000;participantCount=count;memcpy(participants,records,sizeof(Record)*count);
    sessionTimer=SetTimer(sessionWindow,0x53434654,16,&TimerProc);
    if(!sessionTimer){Stop();r->result=49;return true;}
    int changed=0;
    for(int i=0;i<count;++i){
        int j=FightPolicy::Nearest(positions,count,i);if(j<0)continue;
        auto& a=records[i];auto& b=records[j];
        if(!Alive(a.pawn,a.index)||!Alive(a.controller,a.controllerIndex)||!Alive(b.pawn,b.index))continue;
        if(static_cast<int>(U(a.pawn,hp))<=0||static_cast<int>(U(b.pawn,hp))<=0)continue;
        alignas(4) Byte params[0x54]={};params[0]=static_cast<Byte>(attack);params[1]=255;
        // Hold the current position and aim at another guard; never update Sam's location.
        memcpy(params+4,a.pawn+0xd4,12);memcpy(params+0x10,b.pawn+0xd4,12);memcpy(params+0x1c,&b.pawn,4);
        DWORD force=1;float seconds=30;memcpy(params+0x2c,&force,4);memcpy(params+0x30,&seconds,4);
        params[0x34]=static_cast<Byte>(flags);params[0x44]=static_cast<Byte>(flags);
        go(a.controller,alert);process(a.controller,a.add,params,nullptr);++changed;
    }
    Maintain();
    swprintf_s(r->map,L"Maintaining %d guards against nearby guards for 30 seconds.",changed);
    r->result=48;return true;
}
}
```

`Native/LockPolicy.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <cmath>
namespace LockPolicy {
constexpr float RadiusSquared=250.0f*250.0f;
inline bool PrepareDoor(bool usable,bool locked,bool closed,bool specialOpener,float distanceSquared,float radiusSquared=RadiusSquared) {
    return usable&&locked&&closed&&!specialOpener&&std::isfinite(distanceSquared)&&distanceSquared>=0&&distanceSquared<=radiusSquared;
}
inline bool RestoreDoor(bool closed,bool interactionCommitted){return closed&&!interactionCommitted;}
inline bool GrantKeypad(bool playerInKeypad,bool ownedByPlayer,bool keypadInUse){return playerInKeypad&&ownedByPlayer&&keypadInUse;}
}
```

`Native/Locks.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include "LockPolicy.h"
// Included inside TrainerCheats; every operation executes on the game window thread.
static Field lockFields[]={
    {L"EDoorMover",L"Locked",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"EDoorMover",L"Usable",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"ESwingingDoor",L"MyKnob",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EDoorMover",L"FrontOpener",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EDoorMover",L"BackOpener",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EDoorMover",L"LinkedDoor",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"Mover",L"bClosed",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"Interaction",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EDoorInteraction",L"MyDoor",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EKeyPadInteraction",L"MyKeyPad",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"EPopObjectInteraction",L"InteractionController",L"ObjectProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"Location",L"StructProperty"}
};
struct LockDoorRef {Byte* object;DWORD index,objectName;};
static std::vector<LockDoorRef> lockDoors,preparedDoors;
static bool locksReady=false;
static ULONGLONG nextDoorScan=0,nextLockTick=0;
static DWORD grantedState=0;
static Byte* refreshDoorFunction=nullptr;
using LockGotoFn=int (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD);
using LockEventFn=void (__thiscall*)(void*,void*,void*,void*);
using LockFindFn=void* (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD,int);
static LockGotoFn lockGoto=nullptr;
static LockEventFn lockEvent=nullptr;
static LockFindFn lockFind=nullptr;
static const wchar_t* LockState(Byte* object) {
    if(!Alive(object))return L"None";
    auto frame=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,0x0c));
    auto state=frame?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(frame,0x1c)):nullptr;
    return Alive(state)?name(state):L"None";
}
static bool LockRefAlive(const LockDoorRef& ref) {
    return Alive(ref.object,ref.index)&&U(ref.object,0x20)==ref.objectName&&!(U(ref.object,0x2c)&0x10);
}
static bool LockSameLevel(Byte* object) {return Alive(object)&&U(object,0x6c)==U(player,0x6c);}
static bool LockFlag(Byte* object,int field){const auto& f=lockFields[field];return (U(object,f.offset)&f.mask)!=0;}
static Byte* LockObject(Byte* object,int field){return reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,lockFields[field].offset));}
static bool LockPlainDoor(Byte* door) {
    if(!IsA(door,L"ESwingingDoor")||!LockSameLevel(door))return false;
    auto knob=LockObject(door,2);
    return IsA(knob,L"EKnob")&&LockObject(door,3)==knob&&LockObject(door,4)==knob;
}
static float LockDistanceSquared(Byte* door) {
    auto a=reinterpret_cast<float*>(door+lockFields[11].offset);
    auto b=reinterpret_cast<float*>(pawn+lockFields[11].offset);
    float x=a[0]-b[0],y=a[1]-b[1],z=a[2]-b[2];return x*x+y*y+z*z;
}
static bool LockCommitted(Byte* door) {
    if(!Current()||wcscmp(LockState(player),L"s_OpenDoor"))return false;
    auto interaction=LockObject(player,7);
    return IsA(interaction,L"EDoorInteraction")&&LockObject(interaction,8)==door;
}
static void LockRefreshDoor(Byte* door) {
    if(!Current()||!LockSameLevel(door)||!Alive(refreshDoorFunction)||!lockEvent||!lockFind)return;
    auto knob=LockObject(door,2);
    if(!IsA(knob,L"EKnob"))return;
    auto interaction=LockObject(knob,7);
    if(!IsA(interaction,L"EDoorInteraction"))return;
    auto function=lockFind(interaction,U(refreshDoorFunction,0x20),0);
    if(function)lockEvent(interaction,function,nullptr,nullptr);
}
static void LockRestoreOne(const LockDoorRef& ref) {
    if(!LockRefAlive(ref)||!LockPolicy::RestoreDoor(LockFlag(ref.object,6),LockCommitted(ref.object)))return;
    const auto& f=lockFields[0];
    // Only restore a lock still in the value written by this feature.
    if(!LockFlag(ref.object,0))InterlockedOr(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(ref.object+f.offset),f.mask);
    LockRefreshDoor(ref.object);
}
static void LockRestore() {
    if(locksReady)for(const auto& ref:preparedDoors)LockRestoreOne(ref);
    preparedDoors.clear();lockDoors.clear();nextDoorScan=0;nextLockTick=0;
}
static void LockClear(){preparedDoors.clear();lockDoors.clear();nextDoorScan=0;nextLockTick=0;}
static void LockResolve() {
    locksReady=false;
    for(auto& f:lockFields){f.offset=0;f.mask=0;f.count=0;}
    refreshDoorFunction=nullptr;grantedState=0;
    auto core=GetModuleHandleW(L"Core.dll");
    lockGoto=reinterpret_cast<LockGotoFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GotoState@UObject@@UAE?AW4EGotoState@@VFName@@@Z"));
    lockEvent=reinterpret_cast<LockEventFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?ProcessEvent@UObject@@UAEXPAVUFunction@@PAX1@Z"));
    lockFind=reinterpret_cast<LockFindFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?FindFunction@UObject@@QAEPAVUFunction@@VFName@@H@Z"));
    if(!lockGoto||!lockEvent||!lockFind)throw 1;
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0]);auto count=objectArray[1];if(count>1000000)throw 1;
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i){
        auto object=table[i];if(!object)continue;
        auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,0x18));auto type=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,0x24));
        if(!owner||!type)continue;
        const auto n=name(object),on=name(owner),tn=name(type);
        if(!wcscmp(on,L"EKeyPad")&&!wcscmp(tn,L"State")&&!wcscmp(n,L"s_AccessGranted"))grantedState=U(object,0x20);
        if(!wcscmp(on,L"EDoorInteraction")&&!wcscmp(tn,L"Function")&&!wcscmp(n,L"RefreshInteractions"))refreshDoorFunction=object;
        for(auto& f:lockFields)if(!wcscmp(n,f.name)&&!wcscmp(on,f.owner)&&!wcscmp(tn,f.type)){
            f.offset=U(object,0x44);f.mask=U(object,0x54);f.count=*reinterpret_cast<WORD*>(object+0x38);
        }
    }
    for(const auto& f:lockFields){
        if(f.offset<0x28||f.offset>0x8000||f.count!=1)throw 1;
        if(!wcscmp(f.type,L"BoolProperty")&&(!f.mask||(f.mask&(f.mask-1))))throw 1;
    }
    if(!grantedState)throw 1;
    locksReady=true;nextDoorScan=0;nextLockTick=0;
}
static void LockPrepare(Byte* door,float radiusSquared) {
    if(!LockPlainDoor(door)||!LockPolicy::PrepareDoor(LockFlag(door,1),LockFlag(door,0),LockFlag(door,6),false,LockDistanceSquared(door),radiusSquared))return;
    for(const auto& ref:preparedDoors)if(ref.object==door&&ref.index==U(door,4))return;
    if(preparedDoors.size()>=64)return;
    preparedDoors.push_back({door,U(door,4),U(door,0x20)});
    InterlockedAnd(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(door+lockFields[0].offset),~lockFields[0].mask);
    LockRefreshDoor(door);
}
static void LockTick() {
    if(!locksReady)return;
    auto now=GetTickCount64();if(now<nextLockTick)return;nextLockTick=now+50;
    if(now>=nextDoorScan){
        lockDoors.clear();auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0]);auto count=objectArray[1];if(count>1000000)throw 1;
        for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i){auto door=table[i];if(IsA(door,L"ESwingingDoor")&&LockSameLevel(door)&&!(U(door,0x2c)&0x10))lockDoors.push_back({door,U(door,4),U(door,0x20)});}
        nextDoorScan=now+1000;
    }
    for(auto it=preparedDoors.begin();it!=preparedDoors.end();){
        if(!LockRefAlive(*it)){it=preparedDoors.erase(it);continue;}
        auto door=it->object;
        // Normal opening commits the unlock; do not relock normal mission progress.
        if(!LockPolicy::RestoreDoor(LockFlag(door,6),LockCommitted(door))){it=preparedDoors.erase(it);continue;}
        if(!LockPlainDoor(door)||!LockFlag(door,1)||LockDistanceSquared(door)>LockPolicy::RadiusSquared*4){LockRestoreOne(*it);it=preparedDoors.erase(it);continue;}
        ++it;
    }
    if(wcscmp(LockState(player),L"s_PickLock"))for(const auto& ref:lockDoors){
        if(!LockRefAlive(ref))continue;
        auto door=ref.object;
        if(LockPlainDoor(door)&&LockPolicy::PrepareDoor(LockFlag(door,1),LockFlag(door,0),LockFlag(door,6),false,LockDistanceSquared(door))){
            LockPrepare(door,LockPolicy::RadiusSquared);
            auto linked=LockObject(door,5);if(linked)LockPrepare(linked,LockPolicy::RadiusSquared*4);
        }
    }
    auto interaction=LockObject(player,7);
    if(!IsA(interaction,L"EKeyPadInteraction"))return;
    auto keypad=LockObject(interaction,9);
    if(!IsA(keypad,L"EKeyPad")||!LockSameLevel(keypad))return;
    if(LockPolicy::GrantKeypad(!wcscmp(LockState(player),L"s_KeyPadInteract"),LockObject(interaction,10)==player,!wcscmp(LockState(keypad),L"s_Use")))
        lockGoto(keypad,grantedState); // Normal access-granted code owns sounds, linked actors and mission events.
}
```

`Native/QuietLights.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include "LightPolicy.h"
// Included inside TrainerCheats. No damage, trigger, switch, sound or AI-change functions are called.
static Field quietFields[]={
    {L"Actor",L"LightType",L"ByteProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"LightBrightness",L"ByteProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"AmbientGlow",L"ByteProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"UsesSpotLightBeam",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"bGlowDisplay",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"RestoreInitialLightType",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"bLightChanged",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"bLightCachingValid",L"BoolProperty"},
    {L"PlayerController",L"LightmapTextureCache",L"IntProperty"},
    {L"Actor",L"Owner",L"ObjectProperty"}
};
struct QuietLightRef {Byte* object;DWORD index,objectName,level;DWORD original[6];};
static std::vector<QuietLightRef> quietLights;
static bool quietReady=false;
static ULONGLONG nextQuietScan=0,nextQuietTick=0;
static DWORD QuietRead(Byte* object,int field) {
    const auto& f=quietFields[field];
    return field<3?*reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(object+f.offset):((U(object,f.offset)&f.mask)?1:0);
}
static void QuietWrite(Byte* object,int field,DWORD value) {
    const auto& f=quietFields[field];
    if(field<3)*reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(object+f.offset)=static_cast<Byte>(value);
    else if(value)InterlockedOr(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(object+f.offset),f.mask);
    else InterlockedAnd(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(object+f.offset),~f.mask);
}
static bool QuietRefAlive(const QuietLightRef& ref) {
    return Alive(ref.object,ref.index)&&U(ref.object,0x20)==ref.objectName&&U(ref.object,0x6c)==ref.level&&!(U(ref.object,0x2c)&0x10);
}
static bool QuietDestroyed(Byte* object) {
    for(int depth=0;object&&depth<8;++depth){
        if(!IsA(object,L"Actor"))break;
        auto frame=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,0x0c));auto state=frame?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(frame,0x1c)):nullptr;
        if(Alive(state)&&(!wcscmp(name(state),L"s_Destructed")||!wcscmp(name(state),L"s_Destrocuted")))return true;
        object=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,quietFields[9].offset));
    }
    return false;
}
static bool QuietCarried(Byte* object) {
    for(int depth=0;object&&depth<8;++depth){
        if(!IsA(object,L"Actor"))break;
        if(object==pawn||object==player||IsA(object,L"EInventoryItem")||IsA(object,L"EPlayerController"))return true;
        object=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,quietFields[9].offset));
    }
    return false;
}
static void QuietDirty(Byte* object) {
    QuietWrite(object,6,1);QuietWrite(object,7,0);
}
static void QuietPlayerCache() {
    if(Alive(player,playerIndex))*reinterpret_cast<int*>(player+quietFields[8].offset)=1;
}
static void QuietClear(){quietLights.clear();nextQuietScan=0;nextQuietTick=0;}
static void QuietRestore() {
    bool changed=false;
    if(quietReady)for(const auto& ref:quietLights){
        if(!LightPolicy::ShouldRestore(QuietRefAlive(ref),QuietRefAlive(ref)&&QuietDestroyed(ref.object)))continue;
        bool lightChanged=false;
        for(int i=0;i<6;++i)if(LightPolicy::StillMuted(QuietRead(ref.object,i))&&ref.original[i]!=0){QuietWrite(ref.object,i,ref.original[i]);lightChanged=true;}
        if(lightChanged){QuietDirty(ref.object);changed=true;}
    }
    if(changed)QuietPlayerCache();
    QuietClear();
}
static void QuietResolve() {
    quietReady=false;
    for(auto& f:quietFields){f.offset=0;f.mask=0;f.count=0;}
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0]);auto count=objectArray[1];if(count>1000000)throw 1;
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i){
        auto object=table[i];if(!object)continue;
        auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,0x18)),type=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(object,0x24));if(!owner||!type)continue;
        const auto n=name(object),on=name(owner),tn=name(type);
        for(auto& f:quietFields)if(!wcscmp(n,f.name)&&!wcscmp(on,f.owner)&&!wcscmp(tn,f.type)){
            f.offset=U(object,0x44);f.mask=U(object,0x54);f.count=*reinterpret_cast<WORD*>(object+0x38);
        }
    }
    for(const auto& f:quietFields){
        if(f.offset<0x28||f.offset>0x8000||f.count!=1)throw 1;
        if(!wcscmp(f.type,L"BoolProperty")&&(!f.mask||(f.mask&(f.mask-1))||f.offset%4))throw 1;
    }
    quietReady=true;nextQuietScan=0;nextQuietTick=0;
}
static void QuietTick() {
    if(!quietReady)return;
    auto now=GetTickCount64();if(now<nextQuietTick)return;nextQuietTick=now+100;
    bool changed=false;
    if(now>=nextQuietScan){
        auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objectArray[0]);auto count=objectArray[1];if(count>1000000)throw 1;
        for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i){
            auto object=table[i];if(!object)continue;
            bool supported=IsA(object,L"Light")||IsA(object,L"EGamePlayObjectLight");if(!supported)continue;
            if(!LightPolicy::ShouldSuppress(true,U(object,0x6c)==U(player,0x6c),QuietCarried(object),(U(object,0x2c)&0x10)!=0)||QuietDestroyed(object))continue;
            bool exists=false;for(const auto& ref:quietLights)if(ref.object==object&&ref.index==U(object,4)){exists=true;break;}
            if(exists||quietLights.size()>=4096)continue;
            QuietLightRef ref={object,U(object,4),U(object,0x20),U(object,0x6c),{}};
            for(int f=0;f<6;++f)ref.original[f]=QuietRead(object,f);
            quietLights.push_back(ref);
        }
        nextQuietScan=now+1000;
    }
    for(auto it=quietLights.begin();it!=quietLights.end();){
        if(!QuietRefAlive(*it)||QuietDestroyed(it->object)){it=quietLights.erase(it);continue;}
        bool lightChanged=false;
        for(int i=0;i<6;++i){
            DWORD value=QuietRead(it->object,i);
            if(value){it->original[i]=value;QuietWrite(it->object,i,0);lightChanged=true;}
        }
        if(lightChanged){QuietDirty(it->object);changed=true;}
        ++it;
    }
    if(changed)QuietPlayerCache();
}
```
