`Native/Spawn.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <cmath>
#include "NpcTrackingPolicy.h"

// Bounded registry of map NPCs and trainer spawns; all access runs on the game thread.
namespace TrainerSpawn {
using Byte=unsigned char;
struct Vec {float x,y,z;};
struct Rot {int pitch,yaw,roll;};
using SpawnFn=Byte* (__thiscall*)(void*,void*,DWORD,Vec,Rot,void*,int,int,void*,void*);
using DestroyFn=int (__thiscall*)(void*,void*,int);
using EventFn=void (__thiscall*)(void*,void*,void*,void*);
using FindFn=void* (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD,int);
using AddFn=int (__thiscall*)(void*,int,int);
struct SpawnRecord {DWORD id;Byte *pawn,*controller,*level;DWORD pawnIndex,controllerIndex,levelIndex,pawnName,controllerName;bool removing,spawned;};
struct Registry {DWORD version,nextId;SpawnRecord records[256];};
static Registry* registry=nullptr;
static HANDLE recordHandle=nullptr;
static bool SharedRecord() {
    if(registry)return registry->version==3;
    wchar_t key[96];swprintf_s(key,L"Local\\SCTrainer.SpawnRegistry.v3.%lu",GetCurrentProcessId());
    recordHandle=CreateFileMappingW(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE,nullptr,PAGE_READWRITE,0,sizeof(Registry),key);
    if(!recordHandle)return false;
    bool exists=GetLastError()==ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS;
    registry=static_cast<Registry*>(MapViewOfFile(recordHandle,FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS,0,0,sizeof(Registry)));
    if(!registry){CloseHandle(recordHandle);recordHandle=nullptr;return false;}
    if(!exists){
        ZeroMemory(registry,sizeof(Registry));registry->version=3;registry->nextId=1;
        // Import the previous trainer registry so existing spawned NPCs keep their origin.
        swprintf_s(key,L"Local\\SCTrainer.SpawnRegistry.v2.%lu",GetCurrentProcessId());
        auto previous=OpenFileMappingW(FILE_MAP_READ,FALSE,key);
        if(previous) {
            struct Legacy {DWORD version,nextId;SpawnRecord records[32];};
            auto old=static_cast<Legacy*>(MapViewOfFile(previous,FILE_MAP_READ,0,0,sizeof(Legacy)));
            if(old&&old->version==2){registry->nextId=old->nextId;for(int i=0;i<32;++i){registry->records[i]=old->records[i];registry->records[i].spawned=old->records[i].id!=0;}}
            if(old)UnmapViewOfFile(old);CloseHandle(previous);
        }
    }
    return registry->version==3;
}
static DWORD U(Byte* p,DWORD o){return *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(p+o);}
static DWORD Property(DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name,const wchar_t* owner,const wchar_t* wanted) {
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i) {
        auto obj=table[i];if(!obj||wcscmp(name(obj),wanted))continue;
        auto outer=reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(obj,0x18));
        if(!outer||wcscmp(name(outer),owner))continue;
        auto type=reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(obj,0x24));
        if(!type||!wcsstr(name(type),L"Property"))continue;
        auto offset=U(obj,0x44);
        if(offset>=0x28&&offset<0x8000)return offset;
    }
    return 0;
}
static const wchar_t* State(Byte* obj,TrainerCheats::NameFn name) {
    if(!obj)return L"None";
    // Supported Core.dll execGetStateName reads UObject+0x0c -> FStateFrame+0x1c.
    auto frame=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x0c));
    auto state=frame?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(frame,0x1c)):nullptr;
    return state&&TrainerCheats::Alive(state)?name(state):L"None";
}
static bool Unconscious(const wchar_t* pawn,const wchar_t* ai) {
    return !wcscmp(pawn,L"s_Unconscious")||!wcscmp(pawn,L"s_InitiallyUnconscious")||
        !wcscmp(pawn,L"s_Electrocuted")||!wcscmp(ai,L"s_Unconscious");
}
static void* Function(Byte* actor,const wchar_t* wanted,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name,FindFn find) {
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i) {
        auto obj=table[i];if(!obj||_wcsicmp(name(obj),wanted))continue;
        if(TrainerCheats::IsA(obj,L"Function"))return find(actor,U(obj,0x20),0);
    }
    return nullptr;
}
static bool Param(void* function,const wchar_t* wanted,DWORD offset,DWORD size,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name) {
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i) {
        auto obj=table[i];if(!obj||reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(obj,0x18))!=function||_wcsicmp(name(obj),wanted))continue;
        return U(obj,0x44)==offset&&*reinterpret_cast<WORD*>(obj+0x3a)==size;
    }
    return false;
}
static void Condition(Request* r,SpawnRecord* record,int action,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name,FindFn find,EventFn process) {
    auto pawn=record->pawn,ctrl=record->controller;
    DWORD hp=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health"),pawnController=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller"),controllerPawn=Property(objects,name,L"Controller",L"Pawn");
    if(!hp||!pawnController||!controllerPawn){r->result=3;return;}
    if(record->removing||!TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,record->controllerIndex)||U(ctrl,0x20)!=record->controllerName||
        (U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,pawnController))!=ctrl||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(ctrl,controllerPawn))!=pawn){r->result=35;return;}
    auto ps=State(pawn,name),ai=State(ctrl,name);
    bool unconscious=Unconscious(ps,ai);
    bool dead=!wcscmp(ps,L"s_Dying")||!wcscmp(ps,L"s_Dead")||!wcscmp(ai,L"s_Dead");
    if(dead){r->result=35;return;}
    if(action==1) {
        // The controller's state-specific Revive enters s_Groggy and handles recovery,
        // collision, AI group notification and the pawn's return to DefaultState.
        if(!unconscious||wcscmp(ai,L"s_Unconscious")){r->result=35;return;}
        auto revive=Function(ctrl,L"Revive",objects,name,find);
        if(!revive){r->result=3;return;}
        process(ctrl,revive,nullptr,nullptr);r->result=36;return;
    }
    if(!unconscious && wcscmp(ai,L"s_Default")&&wcscmp(ai,L"s_Investigate")&&wcscmp(ai,L"s_Alert")){r->result=35;return;}
    if(!wcscmp(ps,L"s_Carried")||!wcscmp(ps,L"s_Grabbed")){r->result=35;return;}
    if(action==2) {
        if(unconscious||static_cast<int>(U(pawn,hp))<=0){r->result=35;return;}
        auto knocked=Function(pawn,L"Knocked",objects,name,find);
        if(!knocked||!Param(knocked,L"PillTag",0,4,objects,name)||!Param(knocked,L"momentum",4,12,objects,name)){r->result=3;return;}
        struct {int pill;Vec momentum;} parameters={1,{0,0,0}};
        process(pawn,knocked,&parameters,nullptr);r->result=36;return;
    }
    if(action==3) {
        auto died=Function(pawn,L"DiedE",objects,name,find);
        DWORD keep=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"bKeepNPCAlive"),mask=0;
        auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
        for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i) {
            auto obj=table[i];if(obj&&!wcscmp(name(obj),L"bKeepNPCAlive")&&reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(obj,0x18))&&
                !wcscmp(name(reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(obj,0x18))),L"EPawn"))mask=U(obj,0x54);
        }
        if(!died||!keep||!mask||(mask&(mask-1))||!Param(died,L"Killer",0,4,objects,name)||
            !Param(died,L"PillTag",4,4,objects,name)||!Param(died,L"momentum",8,12,objects,name)){r->result=3;return;}
        DWORD original=U(pawn,keep);*reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(pawn+keep)=original&~mask;
        *reinterpret_cast<int*>(pawn+hp)=0;
        struct {Byte* killer;int pill;Vec momentum;} parameters={nullptr,1,{0,0,0}};
        process(pawn,died,&parameters,nullptr);
        if(TrainerCheats::Alive(pawn,record->pawnIndex)&&U(pawn,0x20)==record->pawnName)
            *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(pawn+keep)=(U(pawn,keep)&~mask)|(original&mask);
        r->result=36;return;
    }
    r->result=6;
}
static int EnumValue(HMODULE core,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name,const wchar_t* enumeration,const wchar_t* wanted) {
    auto text=reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t* (__thiscall*)(void*)>(GetProcAddress(core,"??DFName@@QBEPBGXZ"));
    if(!text)return -1;
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i) {
        auto obj=table[i];if(!obj||wcscmp(name(obj),enumeration)||!TrainerCheats::IsA(obj,L"Enum"))continue;
        // UEnum's FName array in the validated Core.dll.
        auto values=reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(U(obj,0x38));DWORD count=U(obj,0x3c);
        if(!values||count>256)return -1;
        for(DWORD j=0;j<count;++j)if(!_wcsicmp(text(values+j),wanted))return static_cast<int>(j);
    }
    return -1;
}
static void Activity(Request* r,SpawnRecord* record,int activity,Byte* player,HMODULE core,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name,FindFn find,EventFn process) {
    if(activity<1||activity>5){r->result=6;return;}
    auto pawn=record->pawn,ctrl=record->controller;
    DWORD hp=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health"),pc=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller"),cp=Property(objects,name,L"Controller",L"Pawn");
    DWORD samOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPlayerController",L"EPawn"),group=Property(objects,name,L"EAIController",L"Group");
    if(!hp||!pc||!cp||!samOffset||!group){r->result=3;return;}
    if(record->removing||static_cast<int>(U(pawn,hp))<=0||!TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,record->controllerIndex)||
       U(ctrl,0x20)!=record->controllerName||(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,pc))!=ctrl||
       reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(ctrl,cp))!=pawn||!U(ctrl,group)){r->result=37;return;}
    auto ps=State(pawn,name),state=State(ctrl,name);
    if(Unconscious(ps,state)||(!wcscmp(ps,L"s_Grabbed"))||
       (wcscmp(state,L"s_Default")&&wcscmp(state,L"s_Investigate")&&wcscmp(state,L"s_Alert"))){r->result=37;return;}
    auto sam=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,samOffset));
    if(!TrainerCheats::IsA(sam,L"EPawn")){r->result=16;return;}
    auto reset=Function(ctrl,L"ResetGoalList",objects,name,find);
    auto routes=Function(ctrl,L"ClearRoutes",objects,name,find);
    auto unlock=Function(ctrl,L"UnlockNavPoint",objects,name,find);
    auto patrol=Function(ctrl,L"RemoveReferencesInPatrolInfo",objects,name,find);
    auto replace=Function(ctrl,L"ReplaceDefaultGoal",objects,name,find);
    auto add=Function(ctrl,L"AddGoal",objects,name,find);
    auto goState=reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD)>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GotoState@UObject@@UAE?AW4EGotoState@@VFName@@@Z"));
    int guard=EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"GoalType",L"GOAL_Guard");
    int move=EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"GoalType",activity==4?L"GOAL_QuickSearch":L"GOAL_MoveTo");
    int flags=EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"MoveFlags",activity==3?L"MOVE_JogAlert":activity==4?L"MOVE_Search":L"MOVE_WalkNormal");
    int wait=EnumValue(core,objects,name,L"MoveFlags",activity==5?L"MOVE_WalkAlert":L"MOVE_WalkNormal");
    DWORD stateName=0;const wchar_t* desired=activity==4?L"s_Investigate":activity==5?L"s_Alert":L"s_Default";
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i)if(table[i]&&!wcscmp(name(table[i]),desired)&&TrainerCheats::IsA(table[i],L"State"))stateName=U(table[i],0x20);
    if(!reset||!routes||!unlock||!patrol||!replace||!add||!goState||!stateName||guard<0||move<0||flags<0||wait<0){r->result=3;return;}
    // Verify every input layout before clearing an existing AI goal.
    for(auto fn:{replace,add}) {
        if(!Param(fn,L"_Type",0,1,objects,name)||!Param(fn,L"_Priority",1,1,objects,name)||
           !Param(fn,L"_Location",4,12,objects,name)||!Param(fn,L"_Focus",0x10,12,objects,name)||
           !Param(fn,L"_MoveFlags",0x34,1,objects,name)||!Param(fn,L"_WaitFlags",0x44,1,objects,name)||
           !Param(fn,L"_Value",0x30,4,objects,name)){r->result=3;return;}
    }
    Vec destination=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(pawn+0xd4);
    if(activity>=2&&activity<=4) {
        destination=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(sam+0xd4);
        float yaw=static_cast<int>(U(player,0xe4))*(6.28318530718f/65536.0f);
        destination.x+=std::cos(yaw)*400;destination.y+=std::sin(yaw)*400;
    }
    if(!std::isfinite(destination.x)||!std::isfinite(destination.y)||!std::isfinite(destination.z)){r->result=7;return;}
    process(ctrl,patrol,nullptr,nullptr);process(ctrl,unlock,nullptr,nullptr);process(ctrl,routes,nullptr,nullptr);
    process(ctrl,reset,nullptr,nullptr);
    alignas(4) Byte parameters[0x54]={};parameters[0]=static_cast<Byte>(guard);
    memcpy(parameters+4,&destination,sizeof(Vec));memcpy(parameters+0x10,&destination,sizeof(Vec));
    parameters[0x34]=static_cast<Byte>(wait);parameters[0x44]=static_cast<Byte>(wait);
    process(ctrl,replace,parameters,nullptr);
    if(activity>=2&&activity<=4) {
        ZeroMemory(parameters,sizeof(parameters));parameters[0]=static_cast<Byte>(move);parameters[1]=255;
        memcpy(parameters+4,&destination,sizeof(Vec));memcpy(parameters+0x10,&destination,sizeof(Vec));
        parameters[0x34]=static_cast<Byte>(flags);parameters[0x44]=static_cast<Byte>(wait);
        float arrivalRadius=50;memcpy(parameters+0x30,&arrivalRadius,4);
        process(ctrl,add,parameters,nullptr);
    }
    goState(ctrl,stateName);
    r->result=38;
}
static SpawnRecord* AvailableRecord() {
    for(auto& entry:registry->records)if(!entry.id)return &entry;
    SpawnRecord* candidate=nullptr;
    for(auto& entry:registry->records)if(!entry.pawn&&(!candidate||entry.id<candidate->id))candidate=&entry;
    return candidate;
}
static void DiscoverMap(Byte* level,Byte* info,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name) {
    if(!level||!info)return;
    DWORD controllerOffset=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller");
    if(!controllerOffset)return;
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i) {
        auto pawn=table[i];if(!pawn||!TrainerCheats::IsA(pawn,L"EAIPawn")||(U(pawn,0x2c)&0x10)||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,0x6c))!=info)continue;
        bool known=false;
        for(auto& entry:registry->records)if(entry.pawn==pawn&&entry.pawnIndex==i&&entry.pawnName==U(pawn,0x20)){known=true;break;}
        if(known)continue;
        auto entry=AvailableRecord();if(!entry)break;
        *entry={};entry->id=registry->nextId++;entry->pawn=pawn;entry->pawnIndex=i;entry->pawnName=U(pawn,0x20);
        entry->level=level;entry->levelIndex=U(level,4);
        auto ctrl=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,controllerOffset));
        if(TrainerCheats::IsA(ctrl,L"EAIController")&&!(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)){entry->controller=ctrl;entry->controllerIndex=U(ctrl,4);entry->controllerName=U(ctrl,0x20);}
    }
}
// Revival and mission scripts can replace a controller without replacing the pawn.
// Keep the stable pawn record, but refresh its current reciprocal controller link.
static void RefreshControllers(DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name) {
    DWORD pc=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller"),cp=Property(objects,name,L"Controller",L"Pawn");
    if(!pc||!cp)return;
    for(auto& item:registry->records){
        if(!item.pawn||item.removing)continue;
        auto ctrl=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(item.pawn,pc));
        bool valid=TrainerCheats::IsA(ctrl,L"EAIController");
        bool bind=NpcTrackingPolicy::CanBind(valid,valid&&U(ctrl,0x6c)==U(item.pawn,0x6c),
            valid&&(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)!=0,valid?U(ctrl,cp):0,reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(item.pawn));
        item.controller=bind?ctrl:nullptr;
        item.controllerIndex=bind?U(ctrl,4):0;item.controllerName=bind?U(ctrl,0x20):0;
    }
}
static void Position(Request* r,SpawnRecord* record,int mode,Vec destination,Byte* player,HMODULE engine,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name) {
    auto pawn=record->pawn,ctrl=record->controller;
    DWORD hp=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health"),pc=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller"),cp=Property(objects,name,L"Controller",L"Pawn");
    if(!hp||!pc||!cp){r->result=3;return;}
    if(record->removing||static_cast<int>(U(pawn,hp))<=0||!TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,record->controllerIndex)||
       U(ctrl,0x20)!=record->controllerName||(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,pc))!=ctrl||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(ctrl,cp))!=pawn){r->result=37;return;}
    auto state=State(ctrl,name),ps=State(pawn,name);
    if(Unconscious(ps,state)||(wcscmp(state,L"s_Default")&&wcscmp(state,L"s_Investigate")&&wcscmp(state,L"s_Alert"))){r->result=37;return;}
    if(mode) {
        DWORD samOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPlayerController",L"EPawn"),height=Property(objects,name,L"Actor",L"CollisionHeight");
        if(!samOffset||!height){r->result=3;return;}
        auto sam=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,samOffset));if(!TrainerCheats::IsA(sam,L"EPawn")){r->result=16;return;}
        destination=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(sam+0xd4);
        destination.z+=*reinterpret_cast<float*>(pawn+height)-*reinterpret_cast<float*>(sam+height);
        if(mode==1){float yaw=static_cast<int>(U(player,0xe4))*(6.28318530718f/65536.0f);destination.x+=std::cos(yaw)*200;destination.y+=std::sin(yaw)*200;}
    }
    for(float value:{destination.x,destination.y,destination.z})if(!std::isfinite(value)||std::fabs(value)>1000000){r->result=6;return;}
    // Exactly the normal Actor.SetLocation path: FarMoveActor with all three
    // optional flags zero. This preserves collision checks and volume updates.
    auto move=reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*,void*,Vec,int,int,int)>(GetProcAddress(engine,"?FarMoveActor@ULevel@@UAEHPAVAActor@@VFVector@@HHH@Z"));
    if(!move||reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(pawn,0x70))!=record->level){r->result=3;return;}
    if(!move(record->level,pawn,destination,0,0,0)){r->result=40;return;}
    auto actual=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(pawn+0xd4);
    swprintf_s(r->map,L"NPC %lu position: %.2f, %.2f, %.2f",record->id,actual.x,actual.y,actual.z);
    r->result=41;
}
static void Snapshot(Request* r,DWORD* objects,TrainerCheats::NameFn name,Byte* player) {
    wchar_t key[96];NpcSnapshotName(key,GetCurrentProcessId());
    auto handle=OpenFileMappingW(FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS,FALSE,key);
    if(!handle){r->result=7;return;}
    auto output=static_cast<NpcSnapshot*>(MapViewOfFile(handle,FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS,0,0,sizeof(NpcSnapshot)));
    if(!output){CloseHandle(handle);r->result=7;return;}
    ZeroMemory(output,sizeof(NpcSnapshot));output->version=3;
    DWORD hp=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health"),vel=Property(objects,name,L"Actor",L"Velocity");
    DWORD pawnOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPlayerController",L"EPawn");
    DWORD movement=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"BaseMoveFlags");
    DWORD weapon=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"CurrentWeapon");
    DWORD targetOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EAIController",L"TargetActorToFire");
    DWORD protectOffset=0,protectMask=0,hostileOffset=0,hostileMask=0;
    auto objectTable=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);
    for(DWORD i=0;i<objects[1];++i){
        auto obj=objectTable[i];if(!obj||!TrainerCheats::IsA(obj,L"BoolProperty"))continue;
        auto owner=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x18));if(!TrainerCheats::Alive(owner)||wcscmp(name(owner),L"EPawn"))continue;
        if(!wcscmp(name(obj),L"bKeepNPCAlive")){protectOffset=U(obj,0x44);protectMask=U(obj,0x54);}
        if(!wcscmp(name(obj),L"bHostile")){hostileOffset=U(obj,0x44);hostileMask=U(obj,0x54);}
    }
    auto sam=player&&pawnOffset?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,pawnOffset)):nullptr;
    for(auto& item:registry->records) {
        if(!item.id)continue;
        auto& n=output->entries[output->count++];n.id=item.id;n.spawned=item.spawned;
        wcscpy_s(n.pawnState,L"None");wcscpy_s(n.aiState,L"None");wcscpy_s(n.weapon,L"None");wcscpy_s(n.target,L"None");
        if(!item.pawn){n.present=0;continue;}
        n.present=1;n.removing=item.removing;
        // Unknown metadata is protected conservatively; spawned guards remain included.
        n.protectedGlobal=!item.spawned&&(!protectOffset||!protectMask||!hostileOffset||!hostileMask||
            (U(item.pawn,protectOffset)&protectMask)||!(U(item.pawn,hostileOffset)&hostileMask));
        auto position=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(item.pawn+0xd4);n.x=position.x;n.y=position.y;n.z=position.z;
        wcsncpy_s(n.actorName,name(item.pawn),_TRUNCATE);
        n.moveFlags=movement?*(item.pawn+movement):0;
        n.health=hp?static_cast<int>(U(item.pawn,hp)):-1;
        wcsncpy_s(n.type,name(reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(item.pawn,0x24))),_TRUNCATE);
        wcsncpy_s(n.pawnState,State(item.pawn,name),_TRUNCATE);
        auto ctrl=item.controller;
        bool controller=TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,item.controllerIndex)&&U(ctrl,0x20)==item.controllerName&&!(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10);
        n.controller=controller;
        if(controller)wcsncpy_s(n.aiState,State(ctrl,name),_TRUNCATE);
        if(controller&&targetOffset) {
            auto target=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(ctrl,targetOffset));
            if(TrainerCheats::Alive(target)&&!(U(target,0x2c)&0x10))wcsncpy_s(n.target,target==sam?L"Sam Fisher":name(target),_TRUNCATE);
        }
        if(weapon) {
            auto w=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(item.pawn,weapon));
            if(TrainerCheats::Alive(w))wcsncpy_s(n.weapon,name(reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(w,0x24))),_TRUNCATE);
        }
        if(vel){auto v=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(item.pawn+vel);n.speed=std::sqrt(v.x*v.x+v.y*v.y);}
        if(TrainerCheats::Alive(sam)) {
            auto a=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(item.pawn+0xd4),b=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(sam+0xd4);
            n.distance=std::sqrt((a.x-b.x)*(a.x-b.x)+(a.y-b.y)*(a.y-b.y)+(a.z-b.z)*(a.z-b.z))/100.0f;
        }
    }
    UnmapViewOfFile(output);CloseHandle(handle);r->result=32;
}
static bool Process(Request* r,void* view,HMODULE core,HMODULE engine,DWORD* objects) {
    bool list=!wcscmp(r->map,L"--npc-list");
    bool spawnRequest=!wcscmp(r->map,L"--npc-spawn");
    DWORD id=0;Vec destination={};int desiredHealth=0,desiredCondition=0,desiredActivity=0;wchar_t extra=0;
    bool remove=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-remove:%lu%c",&id,&extra,1)==1;
    bool heal=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-health:%lu:%d%c",&id,&desiredHealth,&extra,1)==2;
    bool condition=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-condition:%lu:%d%c",&id,&desiredCondition,&extra,1)==2;
    bool activity=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-activity:%lu:%d%c",&id,&desiredActivity,&extra,1)==2;
    bool coordinates=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-position:%lu:%f:%f:%f%c",&id,&destination.x,&destination.y,&destination.z,&extra,1)==4;
    bool front=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-front:%lu%c",&id,&extra,1)==1;
    bool toSam=swscanf_s(r->map,L"--npc-to-sam:%lu%c",&id,&extra,1)==1;
    bool position=coordinates||front||toSam;
    if(!list&&!spawnRequest&&!remove&&!heal&&!condition&&!activity&&!position)return false;
    if((remove||heal||condition||activity||position)&&!id){r->result=6;return true;}
    if(!SharedRecord()){r->result=7;return true;}
    auto name=reinterpret_cast<TrainerCheats::NameFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
    auto spawn=reinterpret_cast<SpawnFn>(GetProcAddress(engine,"?SpawnActor@ULevel@@UAEPAVAActor@@PAVUClass@@VFName@@VFVector@@VFRotator@@PAV2@HH4PAVAPawn@@@Z"));
    auto destroy=reinterpret_cast<DestroyFn>(GetProcAddress(engine,"?DestroyActor@ULevel@@UAEHPAVAActor@@H@Z"));
    auto process=reinterpret_cast<EventFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?ProcessEvent@UObject@@UAEXPAVUFunction@@PAX1@Z"));
    auto find=reinterpret_cast<FindFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?FindFunction@UObject@@QAEPAVUFunction@@VFName@@H@Z"));
    auto add=reinterpret_cast<AddFn>(GetProcAddress(core,"?Add@FArray@@QAEHHH@Z"));
    if(!name||!spawn||!destroy||!process||!find||!add){r->result=3;return true;}
    TrainerCheats::objectArray=objects;TrainerCheats::name=name;
    auto table=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(objects[0]);DWORD count=objects[1];
    if(count>1000000){r->result=4;return true;}
    auto player=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(static_cast<Byte*>(view),0x34));
    auto info=player?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,0x6c)):nullptr;
    auto package=info?reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(info,0x18)):nullptr;
    Byte* level=nullptr;
    DWORD possessName=0;
    bool mission=TrainerCheats::IsA(player,L"EPlayerController")&&package&&_wcsicmp(name(package),L"menu");
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i) {
        auto obj=table[i];if(!obj)continue;
        auto cls=reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(obj,0x24));
        if(mission&&cls&&!wcscmp(name(cls),L"Level")&&reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(obj,0x18))==package)level=obj;
        if(!wcscmp(name(obj),L"Possess")&&cls&&!wcscmp(name(cls),L"Function"))possessName=U(obj,0x20);
    }
    for(auto& item:registry->records) {
        if(item.pawn && (!level||item.level!=level||!TrainerCheats::Alive(item.level,item.levelIndex)||
          !TrainerCheats::Alive(item.pawn,item.pawnIndex)||U(item.pawn,0x20)!=item.pawnName||(U(item.pawn,0x2c)&0x10))) {
            item.pawn=nullptr;item.controller=nullptr;item.removing=false;
        }
    }
    DiscoverMap(level,info,objects,name);
    RefreshControllers(objects,name);
    if(list){Snapshot(r,objects,name,player);return true;}
    if(!level||!possessName){r->result=16;return true;}
    SpawnRecord* record=nullptr;
    if(remove||heal||condition||activity||position) {
        for(auto& item:registry->records)if(item.id==id)record=&item;
        if(!record||!record->pawn){r->result=22;return true;}
    }
    if(GetForegroundWindow()!=r->window || IsIconic(r->window)){r->result=14;return true;}
    if(position){Position(r,record,front?1:toSam?2:0,destination,player,engine,objects,name);return true;}
    if(activity){Activity(r,record,desiredActivity,player,core,objects,name,find,process);return true;}
    if(condition){Condition(r,record,desiredCondition,objects,name,find,process);return true;}
    if(remove) {
        if(!record->spawned){r->result=39;return true;}
        if(record->removing){r->result=28;return true;}
        DWORD lifeSpan=Property(objects,name,L"Actor",L"LifeSpan");
        if(!lifeSpan){r->result=3;return true;}
        // Do not call DestroyActor from the window hook. Expire the pawn during the
        // engine's normal actor tick. First perform the game's normal AI death cleanup;
        // generic Pawn.Destroyed alone skips AI-group, goal and patrol references.
        auto ctrl=record->controller;
        if(TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,record->controllerIndex) && U(ctrl,0x20)==record->controllerName && !(U(ctrl,0x2c)&0x10)) {
            auto goState=reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*,DWORD)>(GetProcAddress(core,"?GotoState@UObject@@UAE?AW4EGotoState@@VFName@@@Z"));
            DWORD deadState=0,removePatrolName=0;
            for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i) {
                auto obj=table[i];if(!obj)continue;
                if(!wcscmp(name(obj),L"s_Dead"))deadState=U(obj,0x20);
                if(!wcscmp(name(obj),L"RemoveReferencesInPatrolInfo"))removePatrolName=U(obj,0x20);
            }
            DWORD health=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health");
            DWORD group=Property(objects,name,L"EAIController",L"Group");
            if(!goState||!deadState||!removePatrolName||!health||!group){r->result=3;return true;}
            auto removePatrol=find(ctrl,removePatrolName,0);
            if(!removePatrol){r->result=3;return true;}
            *reinterpret_cast<int*>(record->pawn+health)=0;
            goState(ctrl,deadState);
            process(ctrl,removePatrol,nullptr,nullptr);
            if(U(ctrl,group)!=0){r->result=30;return true;}
        }
        *reinterpret_cast<float*>(record->pawn+lifeSpan)=0.1f;
        record->removing=true;r->result=28;return true;
    }
    if(heal) {
        if(desiredHealth<1||desiredHealth>100||record->removing){r->result=6;return true;}
        DWORD health=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health");
        if(!health){r->result=3;return true;}
        // A health edit never revives a dead or unconscious actor.
        auto pawnState=State(record->pawn,name);
        auto aiState=TrainerCheats::Alive(record->controller,record->controllerIndex)?State(record->controller,name):L"None";
        if(static_cast<int>(U(record->pawn,health))<=0||wcsstr(pawnState,L"Unconscious")||!wcscmp(pawnState,L"s_Electrocuted")||!wcscmp(aiState,L"s_Unconscious")||!wcscmp(aiState,L"s_Dead")){r->result=33;return true;}
        *reinterpret_cast<int*>(record->pawn+health)=desiredHealth;r->result=34;return true;
    }
    int active=0;
    for(auto& item:registry->records)if(item.pawn&&item.spawned)++active;
    if(active>=8){r->result=23;return true;}
    record=AvailableRecord();
    if(!record){r->result=42;return true;}
    DWORD pawnOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPlayerController",L"EPawn");
    DWORD invOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"FullInventory");
    DWORD listOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EInventory",L"InventoryList");
    DWORD initialOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"DynInitialInventory");
    DWORD controllerOffset=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Controller");
    DWORD healthOffset=Property(objects,name,L"Pawn",L"Health");
    DWORD hostileOffset=Property(objects,name,L"EPawn",L"bHostile");
    if(!pawnOffset||!invOffset||!listOffset||!initialOffset||!controllerOffset||!healthOffset||!hostileOffset){r->result=3;return true;}
    auto sam=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(player,pawnOffset));
    if(!TrainerCheats::IsA(sam,L"EPawn")){r->result=16;return true;}
    Vec location=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(sam+0xd4);
    Rot rotation=*reinterpret_cast<Rot*>(player+0xe0);rotation.pitch=0;rotation.roll=0;
    Byte *source=nullptr,*weaponClass=nullptr,*controllerClass=nullptr;
    float closest=3.4e38f;
    for(DWORD i=0;i<count;++i) {
        auto p=table[i];if(!TrainerCheats::IsA(p,L"EAIProfessional"))continue;
        if((U(p,0x2c)&0x10)||U(p,0x6c)!=reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(info)||static_cast<int>(U(p,healthOffset))<=0||!(U(p,hostileOffset)&1))continue;
        auto ctrl=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(p,controllerOffset));if(!TrainerCheats::IsA(ctrl,L"EAIController"))continue;
        auto inv=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(p,invOffset));if(!TrainerCheats::IsA(inv,L"EInventory"))continue;
        auto cats=*reinterpret_cast<TrainerCheats::Array*>(inv+listOffset);
        if(cats.count<0||cats.count>16||cats.count>cats.capacity||!cats.data)continue;
        Byte* weapon=nullptr;
        for(int c=0;c<cats.count&&!weapon;++c) {
            auto items=reinterpret_cast<TrainerCheats::Array*>(cats.data)[c];
            if(items.count<0||items.count>128||items.count>items.capacity||!items.data)continue;
            for(int j=0;j<items.count;++j) {
                auto item=reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(items.data)[j];
                if(TrainerCheats::IsA(item,L"EWeapon")){weapon=item;break;}
            }
        }
        if(!weapon)continue;
        Vec pLoc=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(p+0xd4);
        float dx=pLoc.x-location.x,dy=pLoc.y-location.y,dz=pLoc.z-location.z;
        float distance=dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz;
        if(distance<closest){closest=distance;source=p;weaponClass=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(weapon,0x24));controllerClass=reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(ctrl,0x24));}
    }
    if(!source){r->result=24;return true;}
    Byte* created=nullptr;
    static const float angles[]={0,0.523599f,-0.523599f};
    for(float distance:{160.0f,240.0f}) {
        for(float offset:angles) {
            float angle=rotation.yaw*(6.28318530718f/65536.0f)+offset;
            Vec candidate={location.x+std::cos(angle)*distance,location.y+std::sin(angle)*distance,location.z+10};
            created=spawn(level,reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(source,0x24)),0,candidate,rotation,nullptr,0,0,nullptr,nullptr);
            if(created)break;
        }
        if(created)break;
    }
    if(!created){r->result=25;return true;}
    *record={};record->id=registry->nextId++;record->spawned=true;
    record->level=level;record->levelIndex=U(level,4);record->pawn=created;record->pawnIndex=U(created,4);record->pawnName=U(created,0x20);record->controller=nullptr;
    // Use engine allocation so reflected class arrays have normal ownership and GC behaviour.
    auto inventory=reinterpret_cast<TrainerCheats::Array*>(created+initialOffset);
    if(inventory->count==0) {
        int index=add(inventory,1,sizeof(void*));
        reinterpret_cast<Byte**>(inventory->data)[index]=weaponClass;
    }
    Vec at=*reinterpret_cast<Vec*>(created+0xd4);
    auto ctrl=spawn(level,controllerClass,0,at,rotation,nullptr,0,0,nullptr,nullptr);
    if(!ctrl){destroy(level,created,0);record->pawn=nullptr;r->result=26;return true;}
    record->controller=ctrl;record->controllerIndex=U(ctrl,4);record->controllerName=U(ctrl,0x20);
    auto possess=find(ctrl,possessName,0);
    if(!possess){destroy(level,created,0);if(TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,record->controllerIndex))destroy(level,ctrl,0);record->pawn=nullptr;record->controller=nullptr;r->result=26;return true;}
    struct {Byte* pawn;} parameters={created};
    process(ctrl,possess,&parameters,nullptr);
    if(reinterpret_cast<Byte*>(U(created,controllerOffset))!=ctrl) {
        destroy(level,created,0);if(TrainerCheats::Alive(ctrl,record->controllerIndex))destroy(level,ctrl,0);
        record->pawn=nullptr;record->controller=nullptr;r->result=26;return true;
    }
    swprintf_s(r->map,L"NPC %lu: %s",record->id,name(reinterpret_cast<void*>(U(created,0x24))));
    r->result=21;return true;
}
static bool SafeRequest(Request* r,void* view,HMODULE core,HMODULE engine,DWORD* objects) {
    try{return Process(r,view,core,engine,objects);}
    catch(...){r->result=7;return true;}
}
}

```

`Native/TravelBridge.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <windows.h>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cwchar>
#include "NpcProtocol.h"

struct Request { DWORD version; DWORD pid; HWND window; LONG result; wchar_t map[128]; };
static constexpr DWORD Version = 22;
static const wchar_t* MessageName = L"SCTrainer.DirectTravel.v22";
static void MappingName(wchar_t* out, DWORD pid) { swprintf_s(out, 80, L"Local\\SCTrainer.Travel.v22.%lu", pid); }

#ifdef BRIDGE_DLL
#include "Cheats.h"
#include "Spawn.h"
#include "Cameras.h"
#include "Fighting.h"
#ifdef SC_TRAVEL_DIAGNOSTICS
static wchar_t faultPath[MAX_PATH];
static LONG faultCount = 0;
static LONG faultSetup = 0;
static LONG CALLBACK CaptureFault(EXCEPTION_POINTERS* exception) {
    if (exception->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionCode != EXCEPTION_ACCESS_VIOLATION || InterlockedIncrement(&faultCount) > 24) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    HANDLE file = CreateFileW(faultPath, FILE_APPEND_DATA, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
    char text[2048]; auto c = exception->ContextRecord;
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION region = {};
    VirtualQuery(reinterpret_cast<void*>(c->Eip), &region, sizeof(region));
    wchar_t module[MAX_PATH] = L"<unmapped>";
    if(region.AllocationBase) GetModuleFileNameW(static_cast<HMODULE>(region.AllocationBase),module,MAX_PATH);
    int len = sprintf_s(text, "AV EIP=%08lX MODULE=%ls RVA=%08lX ESP=%08lX EAX=%08lX EBX=%08lX ECX=%08lX EDX=%08lX ESI=%08lX EDI=%08lX VA=%08lX\r\n",
        c->Eip,module,c->Eip-reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(region.AllocationBase),c->Esp,c->Eax,c->Ebx,c->Ecx,c->Edx,c->Esi,c->Edi,static_cast<DWORD>(exception->ExceptionRecord->ExceptionInformation[1]));
    DWORD written; WriteFile(file,text,len,&written,nullptr);
    __try {
        for(int i=0;i<64;++i) { len=sprintf_s(text,"%08lX ",reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(c->Esp)[i]); WriteFile(file,text,len,&written,nullptr); }
        WriteFile(file,"\r\n",2,&written,nullptr);
    } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
    CloseHandle(file); return EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_SEARCH;
}
static void EnableFaultCapture(HMODULE self) {
    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&faultSetup,1,0)) return;
    GetModuleFileNameW(self,faultPath,MAX_PATH);
    auto slash=wcsrchr(faultPath,L'\\');
    if(slash) wcscpy_s(slash+1, MAX_PATH-(slash+1-faultPath),L"SCTravelFault.txt");
    AddVectoredExceptionHandler(1,CaptureFault);
}
#endif

static void Handle(Request* r) {
    // UnhookWindowsHookEx does not guarantee every concurrent callback has returned.
    // Pin callback code until process exit rather than allowing it to unload after a request.
    HMODULE self = nullptr;
    if (!GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_PIN,
        reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&Handle), &self)) { r->result = 9; return; }
#ifdef SC_TRAVEL_DIAGNOSTICS
    EnableFaultCapture(self);
#endif
    if (r->version != Version || r->pid != GetCurrentProcessId()) { r->result = 2; return; }
    HMODULE core = GetModuleHandleW(L"Core.dll"), engine = GetModuleHandleW(L"Engine.dll"), win = GetModuleHandleW(L"WinDrv.dll");
    auto objects = reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(GetProcAddress(core, "?GObjObjects@UObject@@0V?$TArray@PAVUObject@@$0A@@@A"));
    auto engineVT = GetProcAddress(engine, "??_7UGameEngine@@6BUObject@@@");
    auto viewportVT = GetProcAddress(win, "??_7UWindowsViewport@@6BUObject@@@");
    auto getWindow = reinterpret_cast<void* (__thiscall*)(void*)>(GetProcAddress(win, "?GetWindow@UWindowsViewport@@UAEPAXXZ"));
    auto travel = reinterpret_cast<void (__thiscall*)(void*, void*, const wchar_t*, int, int, const wchar_t*)>(
        GetProcAddress(engine, "?SetClientTravel@UGameEngine@@UAEXPAVUPlayer@@PBGHW4ETravelType@@1@Z"));
    auto exec = reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*, const wchar_t*, void*)>(GetProcAddress(engine, "?Exec@UGameEngine@@UAEHPBGAAVFOutputDevice@@@Z"));
    auto log = reinterpret_cast<void**>(GetProcAddress(core, "?GLog@@3PAVFOutputDevice@@A"));
    if (!objects || !engineVT || !viewportVT || !getWindow || !travel || !exec || !log) { r->result = 3; return; }
    void* gameEngine = nullptr;
    void* viewport = nullptr;
    __try {
        auto table = reinterpret_cast<void**>(objects[0]);
        DWORD count = objects[1], capacity = objects[2];
        if (!table || count > capacity || count > 1000000) { r->result = 4; return; }
        for (DWORD i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            void* object = table[i];
            if (!object) continue;
            auto vt = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(object);
            if (vt == engineVT) gameEngine = object;
            if (vt == viewportVT && getWindow(object) == r->window) viewport = object;
        }
        if (!gameEngine || !viewport) { r->result = 5; return; }
        auto playerExec = static_cast<BYTE*>(viewport) + 0x30;
        auto viewportExec = reinterpret_cast<int (__thiscall*)(void*, const wchar_t*, void*)>((*reinterpret_cast<void***>(playerExec))[0]);
        if (!wcscmp(r->map, L"--probe")) { r->result = 10; return; }
        if (!wcscmp(r->map, L"--state")) {
            auto getName = reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t* (__thiscall*)(void*)>(GetProcAddress(core, "?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
            if (!getName) { r->result = 3; return; }
            auto player = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(static_cast<BYTE*>(viewport) + 0x34);
            if (!player) { r->result = 12; return; }
            auto level = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(player + 0x6C);
            if (!level) { r->result = 12; return; }
            auto package = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(level + 0x18);
            if (!package) { r->result = 12; return; }
            wcsncpy_s(r->map, getName(package), _TRUNCATE);
            r->result = 11;
            return;
        }
        if (TrainerCheats::SafeRequest(r, viewport, core, objects)) return;
        if (TrainerSpawn::SafeRequest(r, viewport, core, engine, objects)) return;
        if (TrainerCameras::Handle(r, viewport, core, objects)) return;
        if (TrainerFighting::Handle(r, viewport, core, objects)) return;
        if (!wcscmp(r->map, L"--health-on") || !wcscmp(r->map, L"--health-off")) {
            bool enable = !wcscmp(r->map, L"--health-on");
            auto getName = reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t* (__thiscall*)(void*)>(GetProcAddress(core, "?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
            if (!getName) { r->result = 3; return; }
            auto player = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(static_cast<BYTE*>(viewport) + 0x34);
            if (!player) { r->result = 16; return; }
            auto cls = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(player + 0x24);
            bool isPlayer = false;
            for (int depth = 0; cls && depth < 24; ++depth) {
                if (!wcscmp(getName(cls), L"EPlayerController")) isPlayer = true;
                cls = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(cls + 0x2C);
            }
            auto level = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(player + 0x6C);
            auto package = level ? *reinterpret_cast<void**>(level + 0x18) : nullptr;
            if (!isPlayer || !package || !_wcsicmp(getName(package), L"menu")) { r->result = 16; return; }
            DWORD offset = 0, mask = 0;
            for (DWORD i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
                auto object = static_cast<BYTE*>(table[i]);
                if (!object || wcscmp(getName(object), L"bInvincible")) continue;
                auto owner = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(object + 0x18);
                auto type = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(object + 0x24);
                if (!owner || !type || wcscmp(getName(owner), L"EPlayerController") || wcscmp(getName(type), L"BoolProperty")) continue;
                offset = *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(object + 0x44);
                mask = *reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(object + 0x54);
                break;
            }
            if (offset < 0x28 || offset > 0x4000 || offset % 4 || !mask || (mask & (mask - 1))) { r->result = 3; return; }
            auto flags = reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG*>(player + offset);
            if (enable) InterlockedOr(flags, static_cast<LONG>(mask));
            else InterlockedAnd(flags, static_cast<LONG>(~mask));
            if (((*flags & mask) != 0) != enable) { r->result = 7; return; }
            swprintf_s(r->map,L"%08lX:%08lX:%08lX:%08lX:%08lX",
                reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(viewport),reinterpret_cast<DWORD>(player),*reinterpret_cast<DWORD*>(player + 4),offset,mask);
            r->result = 15;
            return;
        }

        // Active-mission travel reproducibly crashes this installation even via OPEN.
        // Permit fresh launches from the menu only until mission teardown is understood.
        auto getMapName = reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t* (__thiscall*)(void*)>(GetProcAddress(core, "?GetName@UObject@@QBEPBGXZ"));
        auto currentPlayer = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(static_cast<BYTE*>(viewport) + 0x34);
        if (!getMapName || !currentPlayer) { r->result = 13; return; }
        auto currentLevel = *reinterpret_cast<BYTE**>(currentPlayer + 0x6C);
        if (!currentLevel) { r->result = 13; return; }
        auto currentPackage = *reinterpret_cast<void**>(currentLevel + 0x18);
        if (!currentPackage || _wcsicmp(getMapName(currentPackage), L"menu")) { r->result = 13; return; }
        if (!r->map[0] || !wmemchr(r->map, L'\0', 128)) { r->result = 6; return; }
        for (const wchar_t* c = r->map; *c; ++c)
            if (!((*c >= L'0' && *c <= L'9') || (*c >= L'a' && *c <= L'z') ||
                  (*c >= L'A' && *c <= L'Z') || *c == L'_')) { r->result = 6; return; }
        // Use the same viewport FExec route as PlayerController.ConsoleCommand.
        if (GetForegroundWindow() != r->window || IsIconic(r->window)) { r->result = 14; return; }
        wchar_t command[160];
        swprintf_s(command, L"OPEN %s", r->map);
        // The console UI stays closed; only the internal menu command handler is used.
        if (!*log || !viewportExec(playerExec, command, *log)) { r->result = 8; return; }
        r->result = 1;
    } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { r->result = 7; }
}
extern "C" __declspec(dllexport) LRESULT CALLBACK TravelHook(int code, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp) {
    if (code >= 0) {
        auto msg = reinterpret_cast<CWPSTRUCT*>(lp);
        if (msg->message == RegisterWindowMessageW(MessageName)) {
            wchar_t name[80]; MappingName(name, GetCurrentProcessId());
            HANDLE mapping = OpenFileMappingW(FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS, FALSE, name);
            if (mapping) {
                auto r = static_cast<Request*>(MapViewOfFile(mapping, FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS, 0, 0, sizeof(Request)));
                if (r) { if (r->result == 0) Handle(r); UnmapViewOfFile(r); }
                CloseHandle(mapping);
            }
        }
    }
    return CallNextHookEx(nullptr, code, wp, lp);
}
BOOL WINAPI DllMain(HINSTANCE, DWORD, LPVOID) { return TRUE; }
#else
struct Search { DWORD pid; HWND window; };
static BOOL CALLBACK FindWindow(HWND window, LPARAM context) {
    auto s = reinterpret_cast<Search*>(context); DWORD pid;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(window, &pid);
    if (pid == s->pid && IsWindowVisible(window) && !GetWindow(window, GW_OWNER)) {
        wchar_t cls[128]; GetClassNameW(window, cls, 128);
        // Only target the Unreal viewport, never a crash dialog.
        if (wcsstr(cls, L"Unreal")) { s->window = window; return FALSE; }
    }
    return TRUE;
}
int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    if (argc == 2 && !wcscmp(argv[1], L"--version")) { puts("SCTravelHost:0.23.0:protocol22"); return 0; }
    if (argc != 3) { puts("Expected process ID and map."); return 2; }
    Search search = { wcstoul(argv[1], nullptr, 10), nullptr };
    EnumWindows(FindWindow, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(&search));
    if (!search.window) { puts("Game viewport not found. Leave the game at its menu or in a mission."); return 3; }
    wchar_t path[MAX_PATH]; GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, path, MAX_PATH);
    auto slash = wcsrchr(path, L'\\'); if (!slash) return 4;
    wcscpy_s(slash + 1, MAX_PATH - (slash + 1 - path), L"SCTravelBridge.dll");
    auto dll = LoadLibraryW(path); if (!dll) { puts("Could not load travel bridge."); return 4; }
    auto proc = reinterpret_cast<HOOKPROC>(GetProcAddress(dll, "_TravelHook@12"));
    if (!proc) { puts("Bridge entry point missing."); FreeLibrary(dll); return 4; }
    wchar_t name[80]; MappingName(name, search.pid);
    auto mapping = CreateFileMappingW(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE, nullptr, PAGE_READWRITE, 0, sizeof(Request), name);
    if (!mapping || GetLastError() == ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS) { puts("Another request is active."); return 4; }
    auto request = static_cast<Request*>(MapViewOfFile(mapping, FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS, 0, 0, sizeof(Request)));
    if (!request) return 4;
    ZeroMemory(request, sizeof(Request)); request->version = Version; request->pid = search.pid; request->window = search.window;
    if (wcsncpy_s(request->map, argv[2], _TRUNCATE) != 0) return 4;
    HANDLE npcMapping=nullptr;
    NpcSnapshot* npcData=nullptr;
    if(!wcscmp(argv[2],L"--npc-list")) {
        wchar_t key[96];NpcSnapshotName(key,search.pid);
        npcMapping=CreateFileMappingW(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE,nullptr,PAGE_READWRITE,0,sizeof(NpcSnapshot),key);
        if(!npcMapping||GetLastError()==ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS){puts("NPC snapshot busy.");return 4;}
        npcData=static_cast<NpcSnapshot*>(MapViewOfFile(npcMapping,FILE_MAP_ALL_ACCESS,0,0,sizeof(NpcSnapshot)));
        if(!npcData){CloseHandle(npcMapping);return 4;}
    }
    auto thread = GetWindowThreadProcessId(search.window, nullptr);
    auto hook = SetWindowsHookExW(WH_CALLWNDPROC, proc, dll, thread);
    if (!hook) { printf("Could not connect to game thread (Windows error %lu).\n", GetLastError()); return 5; }
    DWORD_PTR response = 0;
    auto sent = SendMessageTimeoutW(search.window, RegisterWindowMessageW(MessageName), 0, 0, SMTO_ABORTIFHUNG | SMTO_BLOCK, 5000, &response);
    UnhookWindowsHookEx(hook);
    LONG result = request->result;
    wchar_t requestMap[128]; wcsncpy_s(requestMap, request->map, _TRUNCATE);
    UnmapViewOfFile(request); CloseHandle(mapping); FreeLibrary(dll);
    if (!sent) { puts("Game did not respond; map load not confirmed. Do not retry until responsive."); return 6; }
    if(npcData) {
        bool valid=sent&&result==32&&npcData->version==3&&npcData->count<=256;
        if(valid) {
            puts("NPCS:3");
            for(DWORD i=0;i<npcData->count;++i) {
                auto& n=npcData->entries[i];
                wprintf(L"%lu|%lu|%lu|%lu|%lu|%d|%.2f|%.2f|%s|%s|%s|%s|%s|%lu|%.3f|%.3f|%.3f|%s|%lu\n",n.id,n.present,n.removing,n.controller,n.moveFlags,n.health,n.speed,n.distance,n.type,n.pawnState,n.aiState,n.weapon,n.target,n.spawned,n.x,n.y,n.z,n.actorName,n.protectedGlobal);
            }
        }
        UnmapViewOfFile(npcData);CloseHandle(npcMapping);
        if(valid)return 0;
    }
    switch (result) {
    case 1: puts("Map travel queued on the game thread."); return 0;
    case 11: wprintf(L"STATE:%s\n", requestMap); return 0;
    case 12: puts("STATE:loading"); return 0;
    case 48: wprintf(L"%s\n", requestMap); return 0;
    case 49: puts("Guard combat is unavailable for the loaded AI scripts. No attack orders were issued."); return 27;
    case 46: wprintf(L"%s\n", requestMap); return 0;
    case 47: puts("Camera deactivation is unavailable for the loaded scripts. No cameras were changed."); return 26;
    case 45: puts("Quiet lights out is unavailable for the loaded lighting properties. No lights were changed."); return 25;
    case 44: puts("Lock bypass is unavailable for the loaded game scripts. No locks were changed."); return 24;
    case 43: puts("Enable noclip while Sam is standing normally, outside interactions or menus."); return 23;
    case 39: puts("Map NPC removal is disabled. Only trainer-spawned NPCs can be removed."); return 20;
    case 40: puts("Position rejected by the game collision check. Choose an unobstructed destination."); return 21;
    case 41: wprintf(L"%s\n",requestMap); return 0;
    case 42: puts("NPC registry is full (256 entries). Change maps to release old actors."); return 22;
    case 37: puts("Activity editing requires a living, conscious NPC in a normal AI state with its original controller."); return 19;
    case 38: puts("NPC activity goal assigned. Movement and search target a point four metres in front of Sam; the game handles pathfinding and reactions."); return 0;
    case 35: puts("This condition transition is unavailable. Revive requires an unconscious NPC with its original AI; dead NPCs cannot be resurrected."); return 18;
    case 36: puts("NPC condition transition requested."); return 0;
    case 33: puts("Health editing is only available for living conscious NPCs; this does not revive an NPC."); return 17;
    case 34: puts("NPC health updated."); return 0;
    case 30: puts("AI-group cleanup did not complete; actor removal was not scheduled."); return 16;
    case 28: puts("Spawned guard removal scheduled."); return 0;
    case 29: wprintf(L"NPC:%s\n",requestMap); return 0;
    case 21: wprintf(L"SPAWN:%s\n",requestMap); return 0;
    case 22: puts("Spawned guard removed (or no longer present)."); return 0;
    case 23: puts("The limit of eight active trainer NPCs has been reached."); return 12;
    case 24: puts("No ordinary armed guard type is available in this map."); return 13;
    case 25: puts("No clear spawn position in front of Sam. Move into an open area."); return 14;
    case 26: puts("Guard AI setup failed; created actors were removed."); return 15;
    case 18: wprintf(L"CHEATS:%s\n", requestMap); return 0;
    case 17: puts("Cheat session ended. Enable options again in the current mission."); return 11;
    case 15: wprintf(L"HEALTH:%s\n", requestMap); return 0;
    case 16: puts("Load a mission before enabling trainer options."); return 10;
    case 14: puts("Load blocked: the game must be visible and focused before map travel."); return 9;
    case 13: puts("Map change blocked: return to the main menu using the game's own menu first. Active-mission travel is not supported safely yet."); return 8;
    case 10: puts("Engine and active viewport located successfully; no map change requested."); return 0;
    case 5: puts("Active engine or viewport not found; no map change requested."); break;
    default: printf("Bridge validation failed (%ld); map load not confirmed.\n", result); break;
    }
    return 7;
}
#endif

```

`Native/build.cmd`:

```cmd
@echo off
setlocal
set "NATIVE_ROOT=%~dp0"
call "C:\Program Files\Microsoft Visual Studio\2022\Community\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars32.bat" >nul
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
pushd "%NATIVE_ROOT%"
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
if not exist out mkdir out
cl /nologo /W4 /EHsc /O2 /MT /DUNICODE /D_UNICODE /DBRIDGE_DLL /LD TravelBridge.cpp /Foout\bridge.obj /Feout\SCTravelBridge.dll /link user32.lib
if errorlevel 1 goto failed
cl /nologo /W4 /EHsc /O2 /MT /DUNICODE /D_UNICODE TravelBridge.cpp /Foout\host.obj /Feout\SCTravelHost.exe /link user32.lib
if errorlevel 1 goto failed
popd
exit /b 0
:failed
popd
exit /b 1
```

`NpcManagerWindow.xaml.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace SCTrainer;

public partial class NpcManagerWindow : Window
{
    private sealed class Row : INotifyPropertyChanged
    {
        public NpcSnapshot Snapshot { get; private set; }
        public uint Id => Snapshot.Id;
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public string HealthText => Snapshot.Present ? $"{Snapshot.Health} HP" : "—";
        public string Summary => Snapshot.Present ? $"{Snapshot.Origin} / {Snapshot.Condition} / {Snapshot.Activity} / {Snapshot.Distance:0.0} m" : $"{Snapshot.Origin} / Removed";
        public Row(NpcSnapshot snapshot) { Snapshot=snapshot; Name=snapshot.TrainerSpawned?$"Guard {snapshot.Id:00}":string.IsNullOrEmpty(snapshot.InstanceName)?$"Map NPC {snapshot.Id}":snapshot.InstanceName; }
        public void Update(NpcSnapshot snapshot) { Snapshot=!snapshot.Present ? snapshot with {Type=Snapshot.Type,PawnState=Snapshot.PawnState,AiState=Snapshot.AiState} : snapshot; Notify(); }
        public void Notify() => PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this,new PropertyChangedEventArgs(null));
        public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;
    }

    private readonly ObservableCollection<Row> _rows=new();
    private readonly DispatcherTimer _timer=new() {Interval=TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(750)};
    private Process? _game;
    private bool _busy,_refreshing,_closed,_stale=true,_ready;
    private Row? Selected => NpcList.SelectedItem as Row;

    public NpcManagerWindow()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        _ready=true;
        var work=SystemParameters.WorkArea;
        MaxWidth=Math.Max(1,work.Width-24); MaxHeight=Math.Max(1,work.Height-24);
        MinWidth=Math.Min(MinWidth,MaxWidth);MinHeight=Math.Min(MinHeight,MaxHeight);
        Width=Math.Min(Width,MaxWidth);Height=Math.Min(Height,MaxHeight);
        NpcList.ItemsSource=_rows;
        _timer.Tick+=Refresh_Tick;
        Loaded+=async(_,_)=>{_timer.Start();await RefreshAsync();};
        Closing+=(_,e)=>{if(_busy)e.Cancel=true;};
        Closed+=(_,_)=>{_closed=true;_timer.Stop();_timer.Tick-=Refresh_Tick;_game?.Dispose();};
        ShowSelected(true);
    }

    private Process Game()
    {
        if(_game is not null&&!_game.HasExited)return _game;
        _game?.Dispose();_game=null;_rows.Clear();
        var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
        if(games.Length!=1) {foreach(var g in games)g.Dispose();throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell and load a mission.");}
        try {DirectLevelLoader.ValidateGame(games[0]);_game=games[0];return _game;}
        catch {games[0].Dispose();throw;}
    }

    private static bool Expected(Exception ex) => ex is InvalidOperationException or System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception or
        System.IO.IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or FormatException or ArgumentException;

    private async void Refresh_Tick(object? sender,EventArgs e)=>await RefreshAsync();
    private async Task RefreshAsync()
    {
        if(_closed||_busy||_refreshing)return;
        _refreshing=true;
        try
        {
            var snapshots=await DirectLevelLoader.ReadNpcsAsync(Game());
            if(snapshots is null)return;
            if(_closed)return;
            snapshots=snapshots.Where(n=>n.ShowInRoster).ToArray();
            var ids=snapshots.Select(n=>n.Id).ToHashSet();
            foreach(var row in _rows.Where(n=>!ids.Contains(n.Id)).ToArray())_rows.Remove(row);
            foreach(var n in snapshots.OrderBy(n=>n.Id))
            {
                var row=_rows.FirstOrDefault(r=>r.Id==n.Id);
                if(row is null)_rows.Add(new Row(n));else row.Update(n);
            }
            var order=_rows.OrderByDescending(r=>r.Snapshot.Present).ThenBy(r=>r.Snapshot.TrainerSpawned).ThenBy(r=>r.Id).ToArray();
            for(int i=0;i<order.Length;++i){int from=_rows.IndexOf(order[i]);if(from!=i)_rows.Move(from,i);}
            _stale=false;
            if(Selected is null&&_rows.Count>0)NpcList.SelectedItem=_rows.LastOrDefault(n=>n.Snapshot.Present)??_rows[^1];
            CountText.Text=$"{_rows.Count(n=>n.Snapshot.Present&&!n.Snapshot.TrainerSpawned)} map NPCs / {_rows.Count(n=>n.Snapshot.Present&&n.Snapshot.TrainerSpawned)} of 8 spawned";
            ConnectionText.Text="Connected · Live updates every 0.75 seconds";
            ShowSelected(false);
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(Expected(ex))
        {
            _stale=true;ConnectionText.Text="Updates unavailable — displayed values may be stale";StatusText.Text=ex.Message;
            ShowSelected(false);
        }
        finally{_refreshing=false;}
    }

    private void ShowSelected(bool selectionChanged)
    {
        var row=Selected;var n=row?.Snapshot;
        SelectedTitle.Text=row is null?"Select an NPC":$"{row.Name} · #{row.Id}";
        TypeText.Text=n is null?"Load a mission to discover map NPCs.":$"{n.Origin}  /  {n.Type}  /  {n.InstanceName}";
        ConditionText.Text=n is null?"":n.Condition+(_stale?" · last observed":"");
        StatsText.Text=n is null?"":n.Present?$"Health: {n.Health} / 100\nActivity: {n.Activity}\nMovement: {n.Movement}\nDistance: {n.Distance:0.0} m\nWeapon: {n.Weapon}\nPosition: {n.X:0.00}, {n.Y:0.00}, {n.Z:0.00}":"This NPC is no longer present in the current game state.";
        StateText.Text=n is null?"":$"Pawn: {n.PawnState}\nAI: {n.AiState}\nTarget to fire at: {n.Target}\nController: {(n.Controller?"Attached":"None")}";
        if(selectionChanged){NameEdit.Text=row?.Name??"";HealthEdit.Text=(n?.Health>0?n.Health:100).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);ShowCoordinates();}
        SpawnButton.IsEnabled=!_busy&&!_stale&&_rows.Count(r=>r.Snapshot.Present&&r.Snapshot.TrainerSpawned)<8;
        RemoveButton.IsEnabled=!_busy&&!_stale&&n?.CanRemove==true;
        HealthButton.IsEnabled=HealButton.IsEnabled=!_busy&&!_stale&&n?.CanEditHealth==true;
        RenameButton.IsEnabled=NameEdit.IsEnabled=!_busy&&row is not null;
        HealthEdit.IsEnabled=HealthButton.IsEnabled;
        bool available=!_busy&&!_stale&&n is not null;
        AliveCondition.IsEnabled=available&&n!.CanSetCondition(NpcConditionEdit.Alive);
        KnockedCondition.IsEnabled=available&&n!.CanSetCondition(NpcConditionEdit.KnockedOut);
        DeadCondition.IsEnabled=available&&n!.CanSetCondition(NpcConditionEdit.Dead);
        ConditionPicker.IsEnabled=AliveCondition.IsEnabled||KnockedCondition.IsEnabled||DeadCondition.IsEnabled;
        ActivityButton.IsEnabled=ActivityPicker.IsEnabled=available&&n!.CanEditActivity;
        FrontButton.IsEnabled=ToSamButton.IsEnabled=PositionButton.IsEnabled=PositionX.IsEnabled=PositionY.IsEnabled=PositionZ.IsEnabled=available&&n!.CanEditPosition;
        CurrentPositionButton.IsEnabled=!_busy&&n?.Present==true;
        ConditionButton.IsEnabled=available&&ReadCondition() is NpcConditionEdit desired&&n!.CanSetCondition(desired);
    }

    private void ShowCoordinates()
    {
        var n=Selected?.Snapshot;
        PositionX.Text=n?.X.ToString("0.###",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)??"";
        PositionY.Text=n?.Y.ToString("0.###",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)??"";
        PositionZ.Text=n?.Z.ToString("0.###",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)??"";
    }
    private void CurrentPosition_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>ShowCoordinates();
    private async void Front_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>await ActionAsync("front");
    private async void ToSam_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>await ActionAsync("to-sam");
    private async void Position_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        try {await ActionAsync("position",position:NpcPosition.Parse(PositionX.Text,PositionY.Text,PositionZ.Text));}
        catch(Exception ex) when(Expected(ex)){StatusText.Text=ex.Message;}
    }
    private async void Activity_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(Selected?.Snapshot.CanEditActivity!=true||ActivityPicker.SelectedItem is not ComboBoxItem item||!int.TryParse(item.Tag?.ToString(),out int value)||!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(NpcActivityEdit),value))return;
        await ActionAsync("activity",activity:(NpcActivityEdit)value);
    }
    private NpcConditionEdit? ReadCondition() => ConditionPicker.SelectedItem is ComboBoxItem item && int.TryParse(item.Tag?.ToString(),out int value) && Enum.IsDefined(typeof(NpcConditionEdit),value) ? (NpcConditionEdit)value : null;
    private void Condition_SelectionChanged(object sender,SelectionChangedEventArgs e) {if(_ready)ShowSelected(false);}
    private async void Condition_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(ReadCondition() is not NpcConditionEdit desired||Selected?.Snapshot.CanSetCondition(desired)!=true)return;
        await ActionAsync("condition",condition:desired);
    }
    private void NpcList_SelectionChanged(object sender,SelectionChangedEventArgs e)=>ShowSelected(true);
    private void Rename_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(Selected is not Row row)return;
        string name=NameEdit.Text.Trim();if(name.Length==0){StatusText.Text="Enter a display name.";return;}
        row.Name=name;row.Notify();ShowSelected(false);StatusText.Text="Display name changed in this trainer window.";
    }
    private async void Spawn_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>await ActionAsync("spawn");
    private async void Remove_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>await ActionAsync("remove");
    private async void Heal_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>await ActionAsync("health",100);
    private async void Health_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if(!int.TryParse(HealthEdit.Text,NumberStyles.None,CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,out int health)||health is <1 or >100)
        {StatusText.Text="Health must be a whole number from 1 to 100.";return;}
        await ActionAsync("health",health);
    }
    private async Task ActionAsync(string action,int health=100,NpcConditionEdit? condition=null,NpcActivityEdit? activity=null,NpcPosition? position=null)
    {
        if(_busy||_stale)return;
        uint id=Selected?.Id??0;
        if(action!="spawn"&&id==0)return;
        _busy=true;ShowSelected(false);
        try
        {
            StatusText.Text=action=="spawn"?"Spawning a guard…":$"Updating NPC {id}…";
            StatusText.Text=await DirectLevelLoader.NpcActionAsync(Game(),action,id,health,condition,activity,position);
        }
        catch(Exception ex) when(Expected(ex)){StatusText.Text=ex.Message;}
        finally{_busy=false;await RefreshAsync();ShowSelected(false);}
        if(action=="spawn")NpcList.SelectedItem=_rows.LastOrDefault(n=>n.Snapshot.Present);
    }
    private void Close_Click(object sender,RoutedEventArgs e)=>Close();
}

```

`NpcOverlayMenu.cs`:

```cs
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
namespace SCTrainer;

// Stable actor IDs are used for every action; list positions never identify an NPC.
internal sealed class NpcOverlayMenu : IDisposable
{
    private readonly Func<Process,uint,NpcPosition,Task<string>> _positionAction;
    private readonly Dictionary<uint,string> _names=new();
    private readonly HashSet<uint> _globalExcluded=new();
    private readonly Dictionary<uint,NpcPosition> _positions=new();
    private readonly Func<Process> _openGame;
    private readonly Func<Process,Task<NpcSnapshot[]?>> _read;
    private readonly Func<Process,string,uint,int,NpcConditionEdit?,NpcActivityEdit?,Task<string>> _action;
    public NpcOverlayMenu(Func<Process>? openGame=null,Func<Process,Task<NpcSnapshot[]?>>? read=null,Func<Process,string,uint,int,NpcConditionEdit?,NpcActivityEdit?,Task<string>>? action=null,Func<Process,uint,NpcPosition,Task<string>>? positionAction=null)
    {
        _positionAction=positionAction??((game,id,position)=>DirectLevelLoader.NpcActionAsync(game,"position",id,position:position));
        _openGame=openGame??OpenGame;
        _read=read??DirectLevelLoader.ReadNpcsAsync;
        _action=action??((game,act,id,hp,condition,activity)=>DirectLevelLoader.NpcActionAsync(game,act,id,hp,condition,activity,waitForCompletion:false));
    }
    private enum Page { Spawn, Roster, Edit, Condition, Activity, Health, Position, Details, RemoveAll, GlobalCondition, GlobalActivity, GlobalConfirm, Global, Filter, Scope, Cleanup, RemoveOne }
    private Page _page;
    private sealed record GlobalEdit(int Pid,uint[] Ids,NpcConditionEdit? Condition,NpcActivityEdit? Activity,string Label,Page Parent,int Token,string Scope);
    private GlobalEdit? _global;
    private int _globalToken;
    private Page _globalReturnPage=Page.GlobalCondition;
    private static string ConditionLabel(NpcConditionEdit c)=>c==NpcConditionEdit.Alive?"Alive / revive":c==NpcConditionEdit.KnockedOut?"Knocked out":"Dead";
    private static readonly string[] ActivityLabels={"Idle / guard","Walk to point in front of Sam","Run to point in front of Sam","Search point in front of Sam","Alerted"};
    private bool Eligible(NpcSnapshot n,NpcConditionEdit? condition)=>!n.ProtectedGlobal&&!_globalExcluded.Contains(n.Id)&&n.Present&&(condition.HasValue?n.CanSetCondition(condition.Value):n.CanEditActivity);
    private bool GlobalPage=>_page is Page.GlobalCondition or Page.GlobalActivity or Page.GlobalConfirm;

    private enum NpcScope { All, Map, Spawned }
    private NpcScope _filter=NpcScope.All,_scope=NpcScope.All;
    private static string ScopeLabel(NpcScope scope)=>scope switch {NpcScope.Map=>"Map",NpcScope.Spawned=>"Spawned",_=>"Map + Spawned"};
    private static bool InScope(NpcSnapshot n,NpcScope scope)=>scope==NpcScope.All||(scope==NpcScope.Spawned)==n.TrainerSpawned;
    public void OpenManager(){_global=null;_page=Page.Spawn;}

    public void OpenSpawns(){OpenManager();}
    public void OpenMapRoster(){_global=null;_filter=NpcScope.Map;_page=Page.Roster;}
    private NpcSnapshot[] Spawned=>_npcs.Where(n=>n.TrainerSpawned).ToArray();
    private string DisplayName(NpcSnapshot npc)=>_names.TryGetValue(npc.Id,out var name)?name:npc.TrainerSpawned?$"Guard {npc.Id:00}":string.IsNullOrEmpty(npc.InstanceName)?$"Map NPC {npc.Id}":npc.InstanceName;
    private string SelectedName=>Selected is NpcSnapshot npc?DisplayName(npc):$"NPC {_selected}";
    private NpcPosition Draft(NpcSnapshot npc)=>_positions.TryGetValue(npc.Id,out var value)?value:npc.Position;
    private string InputKey(string kind)=>$"{kind}:{_game?.Id??0}:{_selected}";
    private Process? _game;
    private NpcSnapshot[] _npcs=Array.Empty<NpcSnapshot>();
    private uint _selected;
    private int _selectedPid;
    private DateTime _nextRefresh;
    private bool _stale=true,_disposed;
    public bool Busy {get;private set;}
    public bool Refreshing {get;private set;}
    private NpcSnapshot? Selected=>_npcs.FirstOrDefault(n=>n.Id==_selected);
    private static GameMenuEntry Row(string label,string key,bool available=true,string? value=null,bool navigation=false)=>new(label,false,available,key,value,false,navigation);
    private GameMenuEntry BackRow()=>Row("Back","back",navigation:true);
    public GameMenuFrame Frame
    {
        get {
            bool ready=!Busy&&!_stale;
            var n=Selected;
            var rows=new List<GameMenuEntry>();
            string title="NPC MANAGER";
            switch(_page) {
                case Page.Spawn:
                    rows.Add(Row("Spawn guard","spawn",ready&&Spawned.Length<8,$"{Spawned.Length} / 8"));
                    rows.Add(Row("Browse NPCs","browse",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Global controls","global-menu",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Cleanup","cleanup",navigation:true));
                    break;
                case Page.Roster:
                    title="BROWSE NPCs"+(_stale?" / UPDATING":"");
                    rows.Add(Row("Filter","filter-menu",value:_filter==NpcScope.All?"All":ScopeLabel(_filter),navigation:true));
                    var roster=_npcs.Where(n=>InScope(n,_filter)).ToArray();
                    foreach(var npc in roster)rows.Add(Row(DisplayName(npc),$"npc:{_game?.Id??0}:{npc.Id}",!_stale,
                        npc.ProtectedGlobal?"Protected":_globalExcluded.Contains(npc.Id)?"Excluded":npc.Condition,true));
                    if(roster.Length==0)rows.Add(Row(_stale?"Load a mission to connect":"No NPCs in this filter","empty",false,""));
                    break;
                case Page.Filter:
                case Page.Scope:
                    title=_page==Page.Filter?"BROWSE FILTER":"GLOBAL TARGET SCOPE";
                    foreach(var choice in Enum.GetValues<NpcScope>())rows.Add(Row(choice==NpcScope.All?"All (map + spawned)":ScopeLabel(choice),
                        (_page==Page.Filter?"filter:":"scope:")+(int)choice,value:(_page==Page.Filter?_filter:_scope)==choice?"Selected":"",navigation:true));
                    break;
                case Page.Global:
                    title="GLOBAL CONTROLS";
                    rows.Add(Row("Scope","scope-menu",value:ScopeLabel(_scope),navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Condition","global-condition",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Activity","global-activity",navigation:true));
                    int available=_npcs.Count(npc=>InScope(npc,_scope)&&!npc.ProtectedGlobal&&!_globalExcluded.Contains(npc.Id)&&
                        (npc.CanEditActivity||npc.CanSetCondition(NpcConditionEdit.Alive)));
                    int excluded=_npcs.Count(npc=>InScope(npc,_scope)&&(npc.ProtectedGlobal||_globalExcluded.Contains(npc.Id)));
                    rows.Add(Row($"Eligible: {available} / Excluded: {excluded}","scope-counts",false,""));
                    break;
                case Page.Cleanup:
                    title="NPC CLEANUP";
                    rows.Add(Row("Remove all spawned NPCs","remove-all",ready&&_npcs.Any(npc=>npc.CanRemove)));
                    rows.Add(Row("Map NPCs cannot be removed","cleanup-info",false,""));
                    break;
                case Page.RemoveOne:
                    title=$"REMOVE {SelectedName}?";
                    rows.Add(Row("Cancel","remove-cancel",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Yes / remove NPC",$"remove-confirm:{_selectedPid}:{_selected}",ready&&n?.CanRemove==true));
                    break;
                case Page.Edit:
                    title=$"{SelectedName} / {(n?.TrainerSpawned==true?"SPAWNED":"MAP")}";
                    rows.Add(Row(n is null?"Unavailable":$"{n.Condition} / {n.Activity} / {n.Health} HP","npc-summary",false,""));
                    rows.Add(Row("Condition","condition",n is not null,n?.Condition??"--",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Activity","activity",n is not null,n?.Activity??"--",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Health","health",n is not null,n is null?"--":$"{n.Health} HP",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Position","position-menu",n is not null,navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row("Details / Rename","details",n is not null,navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(new("Exclude from global edits",n is not null&&(n.ProtectedGlobal||_globalExcluded.Contains(n.Id)),n is not null&&!Busy&&!n.ProtectedGlobal,"global-exclude"));
                    rows.Add(Row("Remove NPC","remove-menu",ready&&n?.CanRemove==true));
                    break;
                case Page.Condition:
                    title=$"{SelectedName} / CONDITION";
                    foreach(var c in Enum.GetValues<NpcConditionEdit>())rows.Add(Row(ConditionLabel(c),$"condition:{(int)c}",ready&&n?.CanSetCondition(c)==true));
                    break;
                case Page.Activity:
                    title=$"{SelectedName} / ACTIVITY";
                    var activities=ActivityLabels;
                    for(int i=0;i<activities.Length;++i)rows.Add(Row(activities[i],$"activity:{i+1}",ready&&n?.CanEditActivity==true));
                    break;
                case Page.Health:
                    title=$"{SelectedName} / HEALTH";
                    rows.Add(new("Custom health (1-100)",false,ready&&n?.CanEditHealth==true,InputKey("custom-health"),null,false,false,new("NPC health",(n?.Health??100).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),MenuInputKind.Health,3)));
                    foreach(int hp in new[]{100,75,50,25,1})rows.Add(Row($"Set health to {hp}",$"health:{hp}",ready&&n?.CanEditHealth==true));
                    break;
                case Page.Position:
                    title=$"{SelectedName} / POSITION";
                    if(n is not null){
                        var draft=Draft(n);
                        foreach(var axis in new[]{("X",draft.X),("Y",draft.Y),("Z",draft.Z)})rows.Add(new($"{axis.Item1}: {axis.Item2:0.###}",false,ready&&n.CanEditPosition,InputKey("coord")+":"+axis.Item1,null,false,false,new($"Position {axis.Item1}",axis.Item2.ToString("0.###",CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),MenuInputKind.Coordinate,24)));
                        rows.Add(Row("Apply XYZ position","apply-position",ready&&n.CanEditPosition));
                        rows.Add(Row("Use current position","use-current",!_stale));
                        rows.Add(Row("In front of Sam","front",ready&&n.CanEditPosition));
                        rows.Add(Row("Move to Sam","to-sam",ready&&n.CanEditPosition));
                    }
                    break;
                case Page.Details:
                    title=$"{SelectedName} / DETAILS";
                    if(n is not null){
                        rows.Add(new("Rename",false,!Busy,InputKey("rename"),null,false,false,new("NPC display name",SelectedName,MenuInputKind.Name)));
                        string[] stats={n.Origin+" / "+n.Type,"Instance: "+n.InstanceName,$"Health: {n.Health} / 100","Condition: "+n.Condition,"Activity: "+n.Activity,"Movement: "+n.Movement,$"Distance: {n.Distance:0.0} m","Weapon: "+n.Weapon,$"XYZ: {n.X:0.###}, {n.Y:0.###}, {n.Z:0.###}","Pawn state: "+n.PawnState,"AI state: "+n.AiState,"Target: "+n.Target,"Controller: "+(n.Controller?"Attached":"None")};
                        for(int i=0;i<stats.Length;++i)rows.Add(Row(stats[i],"stat:"+i,false,""));
                    }
                    break;
                case Page.GlobalCondition:
                    title=ScopeLabel(_scope).ToUpperInvariant()+" / CONDITION";
                    foreach(var c in Enum.GetValues<NpcConditionEdit>()){
                        int count=_npcs.Count(npc=>InScope(npc,_scope)&&Eligible(npc,c));
                        rows.Add(Row(ConditionLabel(c),$"global-condition:{(int)c}",ready&&count>0,$"{count} eligible",true));
                    }
                    break;
                case Page.GlobalActivity:
                    title=ScopeLabel(_scope).ToUpperInvariant()+" / ACTIVITY";
                    int eligible=_npcs.Count(npc=>InScope(npc,_scope)&&Eligible(npc,null));
                    for(int i=0;i<ActivityLabels.Length;++i)rows.Add(Row(ActivityLabels[i],$"global-activity:{i+1}",ready&&eligible>0,$"{eligible} eligible",true));
                    break;
                case Page.GlobalConfirm:
                    title=$"APPLY TO {_global?.Scope??ScopeLabel(_scope)}?";
                    if(_global is not null)rows.Add(Row("Cancel","global-cancel",navigation:true));
                    else if(Busy)rows.Add(Row("Applying requests...","global-pending",false,""));
                    if(_global is not null)rows.Add(Row($"{_global.Label} / {_global.Ids.Length} NPCs",$"global-confirm:{_global.Token}",ready));
                    break;
                case Page.RemoveAll:
                    title="REMOVE ALL SPAWNED NPCs?";
                    rows.Add(Row("Cancel","cancel",navigation:true));
                    rows.Add(Row($"Yes / remove {_npcs.Count(n=>n.CanRemove)} guards","confirm-remove-all",ready&&_npcs.Any(n=>n.CanRemove)));
                    break;
            }
            rows.Add(BackRow());
            return new($"npc:{_page}:{_filter}:{_scope}:{_selected}:{_game?.Id??0}",title,rows.ToArray());
        }
    }
    public bool Back()
    {
        if(_page==Page.Spawn)return false;
        if(GlobalPage){_page=_page==Page.GlobalConfirm?(_global?.Parent??_globalReturnPage):Page.Global;_global=null;return true;}
        _page=_page switch {
            Page.Roster or Page.Global or Page.Cleanup=>Page.Spawn,
            Page.Filter=>Page.Roster,Page.Scope=>Page.Global,Page.RemoveAll=>Page.Cleanup,
            Page.Edit=>Page.Roster,Page.RemoveOne=>Page.Edit,_=>Page.Edit
        };
        return true;
    }
    private Process Game()
    {
        if(_game is not null&&!_game.HasExited)return _game;
        _game?.Dispose();_game=null;_names.Clear();_globalExcluded.Clear();_positions.Clear();_npcs=Array.Empty<NpcSnapshot>();_stale=true;_selected=0;_selectedPid=0;
        return _game=_openGame();
    }
    private static Process OpenGame()
    {
        var games=Process.GetProcessesByName("SplinterCell");
        if(games.Length!=1){foreach(var g in games)g.Dispose();throw new InvalidOperationException("Start one copy of Splinter Cell and load a mission.");}
        try{DirectLevelLoader.ValidateGame(games[0]);return games[0];}
        catch{games[0].Dispose();throw;}
    }
    public async Task RefreshAsync(bool force=false)
    {
        if(_disposed||Refreshing||(!force&&(Busy||DateTime.UtcNow<_nextRefresh)))return;
        Refreshing=true;_nextRefresh=DateTime.UtcNow.AddMilliseconds(750);
        try {
            var game=Game();
            var snapshot=await _read(game);
            if(snapshot is null){if(force)throw new InvalidOperationException("Wait for the current trainer request to finish.");return;}
            if(_disposed)return;
            _npcs=snapshot.Where(n=>n.Present).OrderBy(n=>n.Id).ToArray();_stale=false;
            if(_selected!=0&&!_npcs.Any(n=>n.Id==_selected)){
                _selected=0;_selectedPid=0;
                if(_page is Page.Edit or Page.Condition or Page.Activity or Page.Health or Page.Position or Page.Details or Page.RemoveOne)_page=Page.Roster;
            }
        }
        catch{_stale=true;throw;}
        finally{Refreshing=false;}
    }
    public async Task<string> ActivateAsync(string key)
    {
        if(key is "browse" or "global-menu" or "cleanup" or "filter-menu" or "scope-menu"){
            _global=null;
            _page=key switch {"browse"=>Page.Roster,"global-menu"=>Page.Global,"cleanup"=>Page.Cleanup,"filter-menu"=>Page.Filter,_=>Page.Scope};return "";
        }
        if(key.StartsWith("filter:")||key.StartsWith("scope:")){
            bool filter=key.StartsWith("filter:");
            if(_page!=(filter?Page.Filter:Page.Scope)||!int.TryParse(key[(key.IndexOf(':')+1)..],out int value)||!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(NpcScope),value))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Select a valid NPC scope.");
            if(filter){_filter=(NpcScope)value;_page=Page.Roster;}
            else{_scope=(NpcScope)value;_page=Page.Global;}
            return "";
        }
        if(key=="remove-menu"){
            if(Selected?.CanRemove!=true)throw new InvalidOperationException("Only trainer-spawned NPCs can be removed.");
            _page=Page.RemoveOne;return "Confirm removal of this spawned NPC.";
        }
        if(key=="remove-cancel"){_page=Page.Edit;return "Removal cancelled.";}
        if(key.StartsWith("remove-confirm:")){
```
